 = (parts, x0, x1, y, zs, step, mw, mh, md) => { for (let x = x0 + step / 2; x < x1; x += step) for (const z of zs) parts.push([box(mw, mh, md, x, y, z), 0]); };
  const ring = (parts, r, y, n, mw, mh, md) => { for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) { const a = (i + 0.5) / n * Math.PI * 2; parts.push([box(mw, mh, md).rotateY(-a).translate(Math.cos(a) * r, y, Math.sin(a) * r), 0]); } };
  const banner = (parts, x, y, z, ry, w, h) => { const s = new THREE.Shape(), t = h * 0.16; s.moveTo(-w / 2, 0); s.lineTo(w / 2, 0); s.lineTo(w / 2, -h); s.lineTo(0, -h + t); s.lineTo(-w / 2, -h); s.closePath();
    const g = new THREE.ShapeGeometry(s).toNonIndexed(), p = g.attributes.position, uv = new Float32Array(p.count * 2); for (let i = 0; i < p.count; i++) { uv[i * 2] = p.getX(i) / w + 0.5; uv[i * 2 + 1] = 1 + p.getY(i) / h; }
    g.setAttribute('uv', new THREE.BufferAttribute(uv, 2)); g.rotateY(ry).translate(x, y, z); g.computeVertexNormals(); parts.push([g, 5, true]);
    parts.push([new THREE.BoxGeometry(w + 0.3, 0.09, 0.09).rotateY(ry).translate(x, y + 0.04, z), 6]); };
  const slit = (parts, x, y, z, ry) => parts.push([box(0.22, 1.1, 0.08).rotateY(ry || 0).translate(x, y, z), 4]);
  const arch = (a, s, h, n) => { const R = (a * a + h * h) / (2 * a), c = a - R, th = Math.atan2(h, -c), pts = [];   // قوس مدبّب: نصف عرض a، بداية القوس s، ارتفاع القوس h
    for (let i = 0; i <= n; i++) { const t = th * i / n; pts.push([c + R * Math.cos(t), s + R * Math.sin(t)]); } return pts; };          // من (a,s) إلى القمة (0,s+h)
  // ── قطعة السور (محلي: x ±4.22، يُقاس بمصفوفة النسخة) ──
  const wall = () => { const p = [];
    p.push([box(8.6, 7.0, 2.3, 0, 0, 0), 0], [box(8.6, 1.0, 2.86, 0, 0, 0), 1], [box(8.6, 0.25, 2.62, 0, 1.0, 0), 1], [box(8.6, 0.32, 2.62, 0, 6.9, 0), 1]);
    merlons(p, -4.3, 4.3, 7.22, [1.13, -1.13], 1.43, 0.78, 1.0, 0.34);
    banner(p, 0, 6.55, 1.18, 0, 1.25, 3.3); banner(p, 0, 6.55, -1.18, Math.PI, 1.25, 3.3); return merge(p); };
  // ── برج مربّع (محلي ±3.68) بسطح مشرّف ورايات ──
  const towerQ = () => { const p = [];
    p.push([box(6.6, 10.2, 6.6), 0], [box(7.36, 1.2, 7.36), 1], [box(7.4, 0.9, 7.4, 0, 10.1, 0), 1]);
    merlons(p, -3.7, 3.7, 11.0, [3.52, -3.52], 1.48, 0.8, 0.95, 0.36);
    for (const s of [-1, 1]) for (let k = 0; k < 4; k++) p.push([box(0.36, 0.95, 0.8, s * 3.52, 11.0, -2.96 + k * 1.48 + 0.74), 0]);
    for (const [x, z, ry] of [[0, 3.72, 0], [0, -3.72, Math.PI], [3.72, 0, Math.PI / 2], [-3.72, 0, -Math.PI / 2]]) banner(p, x, 9.5, z, ry, 1.7, 4.4);
    for (const [x, z, ry] of [[0, 3.31, 0], [0, -3.31, 0], [3.31, 0, Math.PI / 2], [-3.31, 0, Math.PI / 2]]) { slit(p, x, 4.2, z, ry); slit(p, x, 7.4, z, ry); }
    return merge(p); };
  // ── برج دائري (محلي r≈2.66) بقبة صغيرة ──
  const towerR = () => { const p = [];
    p.push([new THREE.CylinderGeometry(2.45, 2.6, 15.6, 18).translate(0, 7.8, 0), 0], [new THREE.CylinderGeometry(2.7, 2.78, 1.5, 18).translate(0, 0.75, 0), 1], [new THREE.CylinderGeometry(2.88, 2.6, 1.0, 18).translate(0, 16.0, 0), 1]);
    ring(p, 2.7, 16.5, 12, 0.62, 0.9, 0.34);
    p.push([new THREE.CylinderGeometry(2.2, 2.2, 0.9, 18).translate(0, 16.95, 0), 1], [new THREE.SphereGeometry(2.2, 18, 7, 0, Math.PI * 2, 0, Math.PI / 2).scale(1, 1.08, 1).translate(0, 17.4, 0), 0], [new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.07, 0.12, 1.1, 6).translate(0, 20.2, 0), 6], [new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.2, 8, 6).translate(0, 20.8, 0), 6]);
    for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) { const a = i * Math.PI / 2 + 0.4; slit(p, Math.cos(a) * 2.47, 6 + (i % 2) * 3.5, Math.sin(a) * 2.47, -a + Math.PI / 2); }
    return merge(p); };
  // ── البوابة: كتلة بفتحة قوس مدبّب (نصف عرض 1.5 محلي = فتحة الاصطدام) + إطار القوس + الأبواب ──
  const A = 1.5, SP = 3.3, AH = 1.75, ARC = arch(A, SP, AH, 10);
  const gateShape = () => { const s = new THREE.Shape(); s.moveTo(-4.3, 0); s.lineTo(-A, 0);
    for (let i = 0; i < ARC.length; i++) s.lineTo(-ARC[i][0], ARC[i][1]); for (let i = ARC.length - 2; i >= 0; i--) s.lineTo(ARC[i][0], ARC[i][1]);
    s.lineTo(A, 0); s.lineTo(4.3, 0); s.lineTo(4.3, 7.4); s.lineTo(-4.3, 7.4); s.closePath(); return s; };
  const archRing = (w) => { const s = new THREE.Shape(), OUT = arch(A + w, SP, AH + w * 0.9, 10); s.moveTo(-A - w, 0);
    for (let i = 0; i < OUT.length; i++) s.lineTo(-OUT[i][0], OUT[i][1]); for (let i = OUT.length - 2; i >= 0; i--) s.lineTo(OUT[i][0], OUT[i][1]);
    s.lineTo(A + w, 0); s.lineTo(A, 0);
    for (let i = 0; i < ARC.length; i++) s.lineTo(ARC[i][0], ARC[i][1]); for (let i = ARC.length - 2; i >= 0; i--) s.lineTo(-ARC[i][0], ARC[i][1]);
    s.lineTo(-A, 0); s.closePath(); return s; };
  const leaf = (side) => { const s = new THREE.Shape(); s.moveTo(0, 0); s.lineTo(side * A, 0); s.lineTo(side * A, SP); for (let i = 1; i < ARC.length; i++) s.lineTo(side * ARC[i][0], ARC[i][1]); s.lineTo(0, 0); return new THREE.ExtrudeGeometry(s, { depth: 0.16, bevelEnabled: false }).translate(0, 0, -0.08); };
  const doorUV = (g) => { g = g.index ? g.toNonIndexed() : g; const p = g.attributes.position, uv = new Float32Array(p.count * 2); for (let i = 0; i < p.count; i++) { uv[i * 2] = (p.getX(i) + p.getZ(i)) / 1.5; uv[i * 2 + 1] = p.getY(i) / 2.4; } g.setAttribute('uv', new THREE.BufferAttribute(uv, 2)); g.computeVertexNormals(); return g; };
  const gate = (closed) => { const p = [];
    p.push([new THREE.ExtrudeGeometry(gateShape(), { depth: 2.9, bevelEnabled: false }).translate(0, 0, -1.45), 0]);
    for (const z of [1.45, -1.6]) p.push([new THREE.ExtrudeGeometry(archRing(0.42), { depth: 0.15, bevelEnabled: false }).translate(0, 0, z), 1]);
    p.push([box(8.6, 0.3, 3.1, 0, 7.4, 0), 1]); merlons(p, -4.3, 4.3, 7.7, [1.38, -1.38], 1.43, 0.78, 1.0, 0.34);
    p.push([box(8.9, 0.9, 3.25, 0, 0, 0), 1]);
    for (const zz of [1.62, -1.77]) for (const xx of [-3.05, 3.05]) banner(p, xx, 6.9, zz, zz > 0 ? 0 : Math.PI, 1.15, 3.0);
    for (const s of [-1, 1]) { const g = doorUV(leaf(s)); if (closed) p.push([g, 3, true]); else { g.translate(-s * A, 0, 0); g.rotateY(s * 1.62); g.translate(s * A, 0, -0.9); p.push([g, 3, true]); } }
    return merge(p); };
  let G = null;
  const api = { mats: M, get geo() { if (!G) { const t0 = performance.now(); G = { wlS: wall(), wlQ: towerQ(), wlR: towerR(), wlG: gate(false), wlC: gate(true) }; console.info('[v15] السور الجديد: ' + (performance.now() - t0).toFixed(0) + ' م.ث'); } return G; } };
  return api; })();
window.__C15 = C15;

/* ═══ v16 (apply_v16_walls.py): سور المدينة بطراز أسوار القدس ودمشق — حجر كلسي كريمي بكتل كبيرة ومداميك ظاهرة، قاعدة مائلة، شرفات مدرّجة (شُرّافات)، أبراج مربعة بسقّاطات (مشربيات دفاعية) وفتحات رمي، وبوابة بقوس مدبّب بصنج متناوبة (أبلق) وإطار ولوحة كتابة — نفس المواضع والمصادمات ═══ */
(() => { const TL = new THREE.TextureLoader(), V2 = THREE.Vector2, V3 = THREE.Vector3;
  const tx = (p, srgb) => { const t = TL.load(p); t.wrapS = t.wrapT = THREE.RepeatWrapping; t.anisotropy = 4; if (srgb) t.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace; return t; };
  const cr = tx('landmarks/cream.webp', 1), crn = tx('landmarks/cream_n.webp'), M0 = C15.mats;
  const cvs = (w, h, f) => { const c = document.createElement('canvas'); c.width = w; c.height = h; f(c.getContext('2d'), w, h); const t = new THREE.CanvasTexture(c); t.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace; t.anisotropy = 4; return t; };
  const NAVY = '#13285a', GOLD = '#d9b04a', GOLD2 = '#f2d27a';
  const star8 = (g, x, y, r, fill) => { g.beginPath(); for (let i = 0; i < 16; i++) { const a = i * Math.PI / 8 - Math.PI / 2, rr = i % 2 ? r * 0.62 : r; g.lineTo(x + Math.cos(a) * rr, y + Math.sin(a) * rr); } g.closePath(); g.fillStyle = fill; g.fill(); };
  const banTex = cvs(256, 512, (g, w, h) => { g.fillStyle = NAVY; g.fillRect(0, 0, w, h); g.strokeStyle = GOLD; g.lineWidth = 14; g.strokeRect(10, -10, w - 20, h); g.lineWidth = 4; g.strokeRect(30, 10, w - 60, h - 20);
    for (let y = 40; y < h; y += 46) { g.fillStyle = GOLD; g.save(); g.translate(w / 2, y + 380); g.restore(); }
    star8(g, w / 2, h * 0.42, 74, GOLD); star8(g, w / 2, h * 0.42, 50, NAVY); star8(g, w / 2, h * 0.42, 30, GOLD2);
    g.fillStyle = GOLD; g.beginPath(); g.arc(w / 2, h * 0.17, 34, 0, Math.PI * 2); g.fill(); g.fillStyle = NAVY; g.beginPath(); g.arc(w / 2 + 13, h * 0.17 - 6, 29, 0, Math.PI * 2); g.fill();
    for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) { g.save(); g.translate(w / 2, h * 0.62 + i * 26); g.rotate(Math.PI / 4); g.fillStyle = i % 2 ? GOLD : GOLD2; g.fillRect(-7, -7, 14, 14); g.restore(); } });
  const insTex = cvs(1024, 128, (g, w, h) => { g.fillStyle = NAVY; g.fillRect(0, 0, w, h); g.strokeStyle = GOLD; g.lineWidth = 6; g.strokeRect(6, 6, w - 12, h - 12); g.lineWidth = 2; g.strokeRect(16, 16, w - 32, h - 32);
    let s = 7; const r = () => (s = (s * 16807) % 2147483647) / 2147483647; g.strokeStyle = GOLD2; g.lineCap = 'round'; g.lineWidth = 5;
    for (let x = 70; x < w - 70; x += 13 + r() * 10) { const hh = 22 + r() * 58; g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(x, 98); g.lineTo(x, 98 - hh); g.stroke(); if (r() < 0.45) { g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(x, 98); g.quadraticCurveTo(x + 14, 108, x + 26, 92); g.stroke(); } if (r() < 0.25) { g.beginPath(); g.arc(x + 8, 74, 6, 0, Math.PI * 2); g.stroke(); } }
    g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(60, 100); g.lineTo(w - 60, 100); g.stroke(); star8(g, 38, h / 2, 20, GOLD); star8(g, w - 38, h / 2, 20, GOLD); });
  const rosTex = cvs(256, 256, (g, w, h) => { g.fillStyle = '#e9dfca'; g.beginPath(); g.arc(128, 128, 126, 0, Math.PI * 2); g.fill(); g.strokeStyle = '#8a7350'; g.lineWidth = 6; g.beginPath(); g.arc(128, 128, 118, 0, Math.PI * 2); g.stroke();
    for (let i = 0; i < 16; i++) { const a = i * Math.PI / 8; g.beginPath(); g.ellipse(128 + Math.cos(a) * 62, 128 + Math.sin(a) * 62, 38, 13, a, 0, Math.PI * 2); g.lineWidth = 4; g.stroke(); }
    star8(g, 128, 128, 52, '#b8913f'); star8(g, 128, 128, 30, '#e9dfca'); g.fillStyle = '#8a7350'; g.beginPath(); g.arc(128, 128, 11, 0, Math.PI * 2); g.fill(); });
  const M = [new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: cr, normalMap: crn, normalScale: new V2(0.42, 0.42), color: 0xf1eee8, roughness: 0.88 }),     // 0 حجر كبير
    new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: cr, normalMap: crn, normalScale: new V2(0.5, 0.5), color: 0xd9cfbd, roughness: 0.86 }),               // 1 أحزمة وأطر
    M0[2], M0[3], new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x17140f, roughness: 1 }), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: banTex, side: THREE.DoubleSide, roughness: 0.75 }), M0[6],                                             // 2 قرميد، 3 خشب، 4 فتحات، 5 رايات، 6 ذهب
    new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: cr, normalMap: crn, normalScale: new V2(0.35, 0.35), color: 0x4e4640, roughness: 0.8 }),               // 7 حجر أبلق داكن
    new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: insTex, roughness: 0.6 }), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: rosTex, roughness: 0.8 })];   // 8 لوح الكتابة، 9 وردة                                                                     // 8 لوح كحلي
  const TILE = 5.2, NM = M.length;
  const proj = (geo, tile) => { const g = geo.index ? geo.toNonIndexed() : geo, p = g.attributes.position, uv = new Float32Array(p.count * 2), a = new V3(), b = new V3(), c = new V3(), n = new V3();
    for (let i = 0; i < p.count; i += 3) { a.fromBufferAttribute(p, i); b.fromBufferAttribute(p, i + 1); c.fromBufferAttribute(p, i + 2); n.subVectors(c, b).cross(new V3().subVectors(a, b)).normalize();
      const ax = Math.abs(n.x), ay = Math.abs(n.y), az = Math.abs(n.z);
      for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) { const q = [a, b, c][k]; let u, v; if (ay >= ax && ay >= az) { u = q.x; v = q.z; } else if (ax >= az) { u = q.z; v = q.y; } else { u = q.x; v = q.y; } uv[(i + k) * 2] = u / tile; uv[(i + k) * 2 + 1] = v / tile; } }
    g.setAttribute('uv', new THREE.BufferAttribute(uv, 2)); g.deleteAttribute('normal'); g.computeVertexNormals(); return g; };
  const merge = parts => { const by = Array.from({ length: NM }, () => []); for (const [g, mi, keepUV] of parts) by[mi].push(keepUV ? (g.index ? g.toNonIndexed() : g) : proj(g, mi === 1 ? 2.2 : TILE));
    let n = 0; for (const L of by) for (const g of L) n += g.attributes.position.count;
    const P = new Float32Array(n * 3), N = new Float32Array(n * 3), U = new Float32Array(n * 2), out = new THREE.BufferGeometry(); let o = 0;
    by.forEach((L, mi) => { const s = o; for (const g of L) { P.set(g.attributes.position.array, o * 3); N.set(g.attributes.normal.array, o * 3); U.set(g.attributes.uv.array, o * 2); o += g.attributes.position.count; } if (o > s) out.addGroup(s, o - s, mi); });
    out.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(P, 3)); out.setAttribute('normal', new THREE.BufferAttribute(N, 3)); out.setAttribute('uv', new THREE.BufferAttribute(U, 2));
    out.computeBoundingBox(); out.computeBoundingSphere(); out.userData.mat = M; SHARED.add(out); return out; };
  const box = (w, h, d, x, y, z) => new THREE.BoxGeometry(w, h, d).translate(x || 0, (y || 0) + h / 2, z || 0);
  const talus = (w, h, d0, d1, y, w1) => { const g = new THREE.BoxGeometry(w, h, d0), p = g.attributes.position; for (let i = 0; i < p.count; i++) if (p.getY(i) > 0) { p.setZ(i, p.getZ(i) * d1 / d0); if (w1) p.setX(i, p.getX(i) * w1 / w); } g.computeVertexNormals(); return g.translate(0, (y || 0) + h / 2, 0); };
  // شُرّافة مدرّجة (ثلاث درجات) بعرض w
  const merlon = (p, x, y, z, w, d, ry) => { for (const [k, hh, yy] of [[1, 0.46, 0], [0.7, 0.3, 0.46]]) { const g = box(w * k, hh, d, 0, yy, 0); if (ry) g.rotateY(ry); p.push([g.translate(x, y, z), 0]); }
    const cap = new THREE.CylinderGeometry(w * 0.35, w * 0.35, d, 6, 1, false, 0, Math.PI).rotateZ(Math.PI / 2).rotateY(Math.PI / 2).translate(0, 0.76, 0); const tip = new THREE.ConeGeometry(0.07, 0.22, 4).translate(0, 0.76 + w * 0.35 + 0.08, 0); if (ry) { cap.rotateY(ry); tip.rotateY(ry); } p.push([cap.translate(x, y, z), 0], [tip.translate(x, y, z), 0]); };
  const merlRow = (p, x0, x1, y, z, step, w, d) => { const n = Math.max(1, Math.round((x1 - x0) / step)), s = (x1 - x0) / n; for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) merlon(p, x0 + s * (i + 0.5), y, z, w, d); };
  const banner = (p, x, y, z, ry, w, h) => { const s = new THREE.Shape(), t = h * 0.16; s.moveTo(-w / 2, 0); s.lineTo(w / 2, 0); s.lineTo(w / 2, -h); s.lineTo(0, -h + t); s.lineTo(-w / 2, -h); s.closePath();
    const g = new THREE.ShapeGeometry(s).toNonIndexed(), q = g.attributes.position, uv = new Float32Array(q.count * 2); for (let i = 0; i < q.count; i++) { uv[i * 2] = q.getX(i) / w + 0.5; uv[i * 2 + 1] = 1 + q.getY(i) / h; }
    g.setAttribute('uv', new THREE.BufferAttribute(uv, 2)); g.rotateY(ry).translate(x, y, z); g.computeVertexNormals(); p.push([g, 5, true]); p.push([new THREE.BoxGeometry(w + 0.3, 0.09, 0.09).rotateY(ry).translate(x, y + 0.04, z), 6]); };
  const slit = (p, x, y, z, ry, hh) => { const g = box(0.2, hh || 1.0, 0.1); if (ry) g.rotateY(ry); p.push([g.translate(x, y, z), 4]); const c = box(0.5, 0.12, 0.14); if (ry) c.rotateY(ry); p.push([c.translate(x, y + (hh || 1.0), z), 1]); };
  // صف سقّاطات: كوابيل مدرّجة تحت شرفة بارزة على وجه طوله len (محلي x، الوجه نحو +z عند zf)
  const machi = (p, len, y, zf, ry) => { const n = Math.max(3, Math.round(len / 1.1)), s = len / n, add = (g, mi) => { if (ry) g.rotateY(ry); p.push([g, mi]); };
    for (let i = 0; i <= n; i++) { const x = -len / 2 + s * i; add(box(0.34, 0.32, 0.62, x, y - 0.32, zf + 0.31), 1); add(box(0.34, 0.3, 0.36, x, y - 0.62, zf + 0.18), 1); }
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) add(box(s - 0.34, 0.04, 0.5, -len / 2 + s * (i + 0.5), y - 0.06, zf + 0.3), 4); };
  const A = 1.5, SP = 3.3, AH = 1.75;
  const arch = (a, s, h, n) => { const R = (a * a + h * h) / (2 * a), c = a - R, th = Math.atan2(h, -c), pts = []; for (let i = 0; i <= n; i++) { const t = th * i / n; pts.push([c + R * Math.cos(t), s + R * Math.sin(t)]); } return pts; };
  const ARC = arch(A, SP, AH, 10);
  // ── قطعة السور: نواة ٧م (سطح المشي كالسابق)، قاعدة مائلة، حزامان، ممشى بشرفات مدرّجة على الوجهين ──
  const wall = () => { const p = [];
    p.push([box(8.6, 7.0, 2.3, 0, 0, 0), 0], [talus(8.6, 1.5, 3.2, 2.3, 0), 0], [box(8.6, 0.2, 2.5, 0, 1.5, 0), 1], [box(8.6, 0.18, 2.44, 0, 5.3, 0), 1], [box(8.6, 0.16, 2.56, 0, 6.86, 0), 1]);
    for (const z of [1.0, -1.0]) { p.push([box(8.6, 0.55, 0.36, 0, 7.0, z), 0]); merlRow(p, -4.3, 4.3, 7.55, z, 1.43, 0.9, 0.36); }
    for (const x of [-2.15, 2.15]) slit(p, x, 2.9, 1.16, 0, 1.1);
    return merge(p); };
  // ── برج مربّع بسقّاطات وشرفات مدرّجة ورايات (أبراج البوابات والأبراج الوسطى) ──
  const towerQ = (banners) => { const p = [], hw = 3.3, H = 10.6;
    p.push([box(6.6, H, 6.6), 0], [talus(7.8, 1.8, 7.8, 6.6, 0, 6.6), 0], [box(6.9, 0.22, 6.9, 0, 1.8, 0), 1], [box(6.8, 0.18, 6.8, 0, 6.4, 0), 1]);
    for (let f = 0; f < 4; f++) machi(p, 6.6, H + 0.02, hw, f * Math.PI / 2);
    p.push([box(7.8, 1.15, 7.8, 0, H, 0), 0], [box(8.0, 0.16, 8.0, 0, H + 1.15, 0), 1]);
    for (let f = 0; f < 4; f++) { const sub = []; merlRow(sub, -3.9, 3.9, H + 1.31, 3.72, 1.3, 0.85, 0.34); for (const [g, mi] of sub) { g.rotateY(f * Math.PI / 2); p.push([g, mi]); } }
    for (const [x, z, ry] of [[0, 3.31, 0], [0, -3.31, 0], [3.31, 0, Math.PI / 2], [-3.31, 0, Math.PI / 2]]) { slit(p, x, 3.4, z, ry, 1.2); slit(p, x - (ry ? 0 : 1.6), 7.2, z - (ry ? 1.6 : 0), ry); slit(p, x + (ry ? 0 : 1.6), 7.2, z + (ry ? 1.6 : 0), ry); }
    if (banners) for (const [x, z, ry] of [[0, 3.95, 0], [0, -3.95, Math.PI], [3.95, 0, Math.PI / 2], [-3.95, 0, -Math.PI / 2]]) banner(p, x, H + 0.9, z, ry, 1.6, 4.6);
    return merge(p); };
  // ── بدل البرج الدائري: برج مربّع أعلى (حصن الزاوية) بلا رايات ──
  const towerR = () => { const p = [], hw = 2.75, H = 14.8;
    p.push([box(5.5, H, 5.5), 0], [talus(6.6, 2.0, 6.6, 5.5, 0, 5.5), 0], [box(5.8, 0.22, 5.8, 0, 2.0, 0), 1], [box(5.7, 0.18, 5.7, 0, 8.2, 0), 1]);
    for (let f = 0; f < 4; f++) machi(p, 5.5, H + 0.02, hw, f * Math.PI / 2);
    p.push([box(6.7, 1.15, 6.7, 0, H, 0), 0], [box(6.9, 0.16, 6.9, 0, H + 1.15, 0), 1]);
    for (let f = 0; f < 4; f++) { const sub = []; merlRow(sub, -3.35, 3.35, H + 1.31, 3.17, 1.3, 0.85, 0.34); for (const [g, mi] of sub) { g.rotateY(f * Math.PI / 2); p.push([g, mi]); } }
    for (const [x, z, ry] of [[0, 2.76, 0], [0, -2.76, 0], [2.76, 0, Math.PI / 2], [-2.76, 0, Math.PI / 2]]) { slit(p, x, 4.0, z, ry, 1.2); slit(p, x, 7.8, z, ry, 1.2); slit(p, x, 11.4, z, ry, 1.1); }
    return merge(p); };
  // ── البوابة: قوس مدبّب، صنج متناوبة (أبلق)، إطار مستطيل، لوح كتابة كحلي بحافة ذهبية، سقّاطة فوق القوس، شرفات مدرّجة ──
  const gateShape = () => { const s = new THREE.Shape(); s.moveTo(-4.3, 0); s.lineTo(-A, 0); for (let i = 0; i < ARC.length; i++) s.lineTo(-ARC[i][0], ARC[i][1]); for (let i = ARC.length - 2; i >= 0; i--) s.lineTo(ARC[i][0], ARC[i][1]);
    s.lineTo(A, 0); s.lineTo(4.3, 0); s.lineTo(4.3, 7.4); s.lineTo(-4.3, 7.4); s.closePath(); return s; };
  const vouss = (p, z, dep) => { const W = 0.55, IN = arch(A, SP, AH, 40), OUT = arch(A + W, SP, AH + W * 0.95, 40), pts = []; // نصف القوس من اليمين للقمة، ثم مرآة
    const full = [...IN.map((q, i) => [q, OUT[i]]), ...IN.slice(0, -1).reverse().map((q, i) => { const o = OUT[OUT.length - 2 - i]; return [[-q[0], q[1]], [-o[0], o[1]]]; })];
    const NV = 15, per = (full.length - 1) / NV; for (let v = 0; v < NV; v++) { const i0 = Math.round(v * per), i1 = Math.round((v + 1) * per), s = new THREE.Shape(); s.moveTo(full[i0][0][0], full[i0][0][1]);
      for (let i = i0 + 1; i <= i1; i++) s.lineTo(full[i][0][0], full[i][0][1]); for (let i = i1; i >= i0; i--) s.lineTo(full[i][1][0], full[i][1][1]); s.closePath();
      const g = new THREE.ExtrudeGeometry(s, { depth: dep, bevelEnabled: false }).translate(0, 0, z); p.push([g, v % 2 ? 7 : 0]); }
    for (const sx of [-1, 1]) { p.push([box(W + 0.1, 0.5, dep + 0.04, sx * (A + W / 2), SP - 0.5, z + dep / 2), 1]); p.push([box(W + 0.02, SP - 0.5, dep, sx * (A + W / 2), 0, z + dep / 2), 0]); } };
  const leaf = side => { const s = new THREE.Shape(); s.moveTo(0, 0); s.lineTo(side * A, 0); s.lineTo(side * A, SP); for (let i = 1; i < ARC.length; i++) s.lineTo(side * ARC[i][0], ARC[i][1]); s.lineTo(0, 0); return new THREE.ExtrudeGeometry(s, { depth: 0.16, bevelEnabled: false }).translate(0, 0, -0.08); };
  const doorUV = g => { g = g.index ? g.toNonIndexed() : g; const q = g.attributes.position, uv = new Float32Array(q.count * 2); for (let i = 0; i < q.count; i++) { uv[i * 2] = (q.getX(i) + q.getZ(i)) / 1.5; uv[i * 2 + 1] = q.getY(i) / 2.4; } g.setAttribute('uv', new THREE.BufferAttribute(uv, 2)); g.computeVertexNormals(); return g; };

  // ── v16b: البوابة الكبرى (طراز مملوكي/عثماني): بيشتاق بارز بقوس مدبّب كبير مجوّف، صنج أبلق واضحة، مقرنصات تحت القوس، إطار ألفيز، شريط كتابة، وردتان، مداميك أبلق ──
  const voussG = (p, z, dep, a, s, h, W, NV, mA, mB) => { const IN = arch(a, s, h, 48), OUT = arch(a + W, s, h + W * 0.95, 48);
    const full = [...IN.map((q, i) => [q, OUT[i]]), ...IN.slice(0, -1).reverse().map((q, i) => { const o = OUT[OUT.length - 2 - i]; return [[-q[0], q[1]], [-o[0], o[1]]]; })];
    const per = (full.length - 1) / NV; for (let v = 0; v < NV; v++) { const i0 = Math.round(v * per), i1 = Math.round((v + 1) * per), sh = new THREE.Shape(); sh.moveTo(full[i0][0][0], full[i0][0][1]);
      for (let i = i0 + 1; i <= i1; i++) sh.lineTo(full[i][0][0], full[i][0][1]); for (let i = i1; i >= i0; i--) sh.lineTo(full[i][1][0], full[i][1][1]); sh.closePath();
      p.push([new THREE.ExtrudeGeometry(sh, { depth: dep, bevelEnabled: false }).translate(0, 0, z), v % 2 ? mB : mA]); } };
  const A2 = 2.25, S2 = 4.4, H2 = 2.75, PW = 3.25, PH = 9.7, PD = 0.8, ARC2 = arch(A2, S2, H2, 24);
  const portal = (p, dir) => { const z0 = dir > 0 ? 1.45 : -1.45 - PD, zf = dir > 0 ? 1.45 + PD : -1.45 - PD, fo = dir > 0 ? zf + 0.005 : zf - 0.005, sgn = dir;
    const sh = new THREE.Shape(); sh.moveTo(-PW, 0); sh.lineTo(-A2, 0); for (let i = 0; i < ARC2.length; i++) sh.lineTo(-ARC2[i][0], ARC2[i][1]); for (let i = ARC2.length - 2; i >= 0; i--) sh.lineTo(ARC2[i][0], ARC2[i][1]); sh.lineTo(A2, 0); sh.lineTo(PW, 0); sh.lineTo(PW, PH); sh.lineTo(-PW, PH); sh.closePath();
    p.push([new THREE.ExtrudeGeometry(sh, { depth: PD, bevelEnabled: false }).translate(0, 0, z0), 0]);
    for (const sx of [-1, 1]) p.push([talus(1.0, 1.1, PD + 0.5, PD, 0).translate(sx * (PW - 0.5), 0, z0 + PD / 2 + (dir > 0 ? 0.25 : -0.25)), 0]);
    voussG(p, dir > 0 ? zf : zf - 0.12, 0.12, A2 - 0.02, S2, H2, 0.62, 19, 0, 7);                                                   // صنج أبلق
    for (const y of [1.15, 2.3, 3.45]) for (const sx of [-1, 1]) p.push([box(PW - A2 - 0.02, 0.42, 0.04, sx * (A2 + (PW - A2) / 2), y, fo), 7]);   // مداميك داكنة
    const ft = S2 + H2 + 0.95, fx = A2 + 0.82;                                                                                     // ألفيز
    for (const sx of [-1, 1]) p.push([box(0.18, ft, 0.1, sx * fx, 0, fo + sgn * 0.03), 1]); p.push([box(2 * fx + 0.18, 0.18, 0.1, 0, ft, fo + sgn * 0.03), 1]);
    for (const sx of [-1, 1]) { const r = new THREE.CircleGeometry(0.36, 24).translate(sx * (A2 + 0.42), S2 + H2 * 0.62, 0); if (dir < 0) r.rotateY(Math.PI); r.translate(0, 0, dir > 0 ? fo + 0.04 : -(-fo + 0.04) ); p.push([r, 9, true]);
      p.push([new THREE.TorusGeometry(0.37, 0.04, 6, 24).translate(sx * (A2 + 0.42), S2 + H2 * 0.62, fo + sgn * 0.04), 6]); }
    const ins = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(2 * PW - 0.7, 0.72).translate(0, ft + 0.62, 0); if (dir < 0) ins.rotateY(Math.PI); ins.translate(0, 0, fo + sgn * 0.02); p.push([ins, 8, true]);     // شريط الكتابة
    p.push([box(2 * PW - 0.5, 0.07, 0.07, 0, ft + 0.22, fo + sgn * 0.04), 6], [box(2 * PW - 0.5, 0.07, 0.07, 0, ft + 0.99, fo + sgn * 0.04), 6]);
    p.push([box(2 * PW + 0.3, 0.22, PD + 0.3, 0, PH, z0 + PD / 2), 1], [box(2 * PW + 0.1, 0.16, PD + 0.1, 0, PH - 0.3, z0 + PD / 2), 7]);
    merlRow(p, -PW, PW, PH + 0.22, z0 + PD / 2, 1.08, 0.78, 0.4);
    // مقرنصات: ثلاث طبقات من الخلايا المعلّقة بين قمة باب القوس وقمة التجويف، كل طبقة أبرز من التي تحتها
    const back = dir > 0 ? 1.45 : -1.45, cellW = 0.34;
    [[5.15, 1.95], [5.6, 1.6], [6.05, 1.2], [6.5, 0.75]].forEach(([y, hw], k) => { const n = Math.max(2, Math.round(2 * hw / cellW)), s = 2 * hw / n, dep = 0.16 + 0.15 * k;
      p.push([box(2 * hw, 0.08, dep + 0.04, 0, y + 0.36, back + sgn * (dep + 0.04) / 2), 1]);
      for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) { const x = -hw + s * (i + 0.5) + (k % 2 ? s * 0.25 : 0); if (Math.abs(x) > hw - s * 0.3) continue;
        p.push([box(s * 0.86, 0.34, dep, x, y, back + sgn * dep / 2), (i + k) % 3 === 0 ? 7 : 1]);
        p.push([new THREE.ConeGeometry(s * 0.5, 0.24, 4).rotateX(Math.PI).rotateY(Math.PI / 4).translate(x, y - 0.12, back + sgn * dep * 0.6), 1]); } });
  };
  const gate = closed => { const p = [];
    p.push([new THREE.ExtrudeGeometry(gateShape(), { depth: 2.9, bevelEnabled: false }).translate(0, 0, -1.45), 0]);
    for (const sx of [-1, 1]) p.push([talus(1.7, 1.2, 3.5, 2.9, 0).translate(sx * 3.45, 0, 0), 0]);
    for (const [z, dir] of [[1.45, 1], [-1.45, -1]]) { vouss(p, dir > 0 ? z : z - 0.16, 0.16);
      portal(p, dir); }
    p.push([box(8.6, 0.3, 3.1, 0, 7.4, 0), 1]); for (const z of [1.38, -1.38]) merlRow(p, -4.3, 4.3, 7.7, z, 1.43, 0.9, 0.34);
    for (const zz of [1.62, -1.77]) for (const xx of [-3.8, 3.8]) banner(p, xx, 6.9, zz, zz > 0 ? 0 : Math.PI, 0.9, 3.0);
    for (const s of [-1, 1]) { const g = doorUV(leaf(s)); if (closed) p.push([g, 3, true]); else { g.translate(-s * A, 0, 0); g.rotateY(s * 1.62); g.translate(s * A, 0, -0.9); p.push([g, 3, true]); } }
    return merge(p); };
  let G = null; C15.mats = M;
  Object.defineProperty(C15, 'geo', { configurable: true, get() { if (!G) { const t0 = performance.now(); G = { wlS: wall(), wlQ: towerQ(true), wlR: towerR(), wlG: gate(false), wlC: gate(true) }; console.info('[v16] السور الجديد (طراز القدس/دمشق): ' + (performance.now() - t0).toFixed(0) + ' م.ث', JSON.stringify(Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(G).map(([k, g]) => [k, (g.index ? g.index.count : g.attributes.position.count) / 3])))); } return G; } });
})();

/* ═══ v15: محلات السوق: كل الأكشاك الخشبية القديمة صارت مباني محلات/بيوت من الستايل الجديد (مصغّرة لتناسب صف السوق) ═══ */
const STALL15 = { tools: ['mixSmith', 0.74], weapon: ['mixSmith', 0.74], general: ['mixH4', 0.88], pharm: ['mixH5', 0.8], bakery: ['mixFood', 0.68], cloth: ['mixH4', 0.88], spice: ['mixFood', 0.68] };
function stallRep15(type) { const k0 = SHOP_REP[type]; if (k0 && MODELS[k0] && MODELS[k0].house) return MODELS[k0];
  const q = STALL15[type], M = q && MODELS[q[0]]; if (!M || !M.house) return null; return Object.assign({}, M, { w: M.w * q[1], d: M.d * q[1], h: M.h * q[1], k15: q[1] }); }

/* ═══ v16 (apply_v16_hill.py): تل القصر — ثلاث مصاطب بجدران حجر كلسي كريمي (كتل كبيرة) وأفاريز وأعمدة جدارية ودرابزين، ودرج كبير بثلاثة أشواط ومساطح، أعمدة بداية ذهبية وفوانيس وأحواض زهر ═══ */
const V16 = (() => { const TL = new THREE.TextureLoader(), V2 = THREE.Vector2;
  const tex = (p, srgb) => { const t = TL.load(p, undefined, undefined, () => console.warn('[v16] لم يُحمَّل ' + p)); t.wrapS = t.wrapT = THREE.RepeatWrapping; t.anisotropy = 4; if (srgb) t.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace; return t; };
  let MT = null;
  const mats = () => { if (MT) return MT; const cr = tex('landmarks/cream.webp', 1), crn = tex('landmarks/cream_n.webp'), fl = tex('city/flag.webp', 1), fln = tex('city/flag_n.webp');
    MT = [new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: cr, normalMap: crn, normalScale: new V2(0.8, 0.8), color: 0xf1eee8, roughness: 0.9 }),          // 0 جدار
      new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: cr, normalMap: crn, normalScale: new V2(0.5, 0.5), color: 0xddd3c2, roughness: 0.84 }),           // 1 أفاريز وقواعد
      new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: fl, normalMap: fln, normalScale: new V2(0.7, 0.7), color: 0xf3f0ea, roughness: 0.88 }),           // 2 بلاط المصاطب
      new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: cr, normalMap: crn, normalScale: new V2(0.35, 0.35), color: 0xfaf7f1, roughness: 0.62 }),         // 3 درجات (أفتح وأنعم)
      new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0xc9a043, roughness: 0.38, metalness: 0.7 }),                                                   // 4 ذهب
      new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ vertexColors: true, roughness: 0.85 })];                                                               // 5 نبات (ألوان رؤوس)
    return MT; };
  // دمج قطع بمواد متعددة في هندسة واحدة بمجموعات (رسمة لكل مادة) — UV مسقطة حسب الوجه بالإحداثيات المحلية (القطع مكتوبة بإحداثيات المدينة)
  const V3 = THREE.Vector3, _a = new V3(), _b = new V3(), _c = new V3(), _n = new V3();
  const proj = (g, tile) => { g = g.index ? g.toNonIndexed() : g; const p = g.attributes.position, uv = new Float32Array(p.count * 2);
    for (let i = 0; i < p.count; i += 3) { _a.fromBufferAttribute(p, i); _b.fromBufferAttribute(p, i + 1); _c.fromBufferAttribute(p, i + 2); _n.subVectors(_c, _b).cross(new V3().subVectors(_a, _b)).normalize();
      const ax = Math.abs(_n.x), ay = Math.abs(_n.y), az = Math.abs(_n.z);
      for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) { const q = [_a, _b, _c][k]; let u, v; if (ay >= ax && ay >= az) { u = q.x; v = q.z; } else if (ax >= az) { u = q.z; v = q.y; } else { u = q.x; v = q.y; } uv[(i + k) * 2] = u / tile; uv[(i + k) * 2 + 1] = v / tile; } }
    g.setAttribute('uv', new THREE.BufferAttribute(uv, 2)); if (!g.attributes.normal) g.computeVertexNormals(); return g; };
  const merge = (parts, nm) => { const by = Array.from({ length: nm }, () => []); for (const [g, mi, keep] of parts) { let q = g.index ? g.toNonIndexed() : g; if (!keep) q = proj(q, mi === 2 ? 3.2 : mi === 3 ? 2.2 : 3.4); if (!q.attributes.color) { const c = new Float32Array(q.attributes.position.count * 3).fill(1); q.setAttribute('color', new THREE.BufferAttribute(c, 3)); } by[mi].push(q); }
    let n = 0; for (const L of by) for (const g of L) n += g.attributes.position.count; const P = new Float32Array(n * 3), N = new Float32Array(n * 3), U = new Float32Array(n * 2), C = new Float32Array(n * 3), out = new THREE.BufferGeometry(); let o = 0;
    by.forEach((L, mi) => { const s = o; for (const g of L) { P.set(g.attributes.position.array, o * 3); N.set(g.attributes.normal.array, o * 3); U.set(g.attributes.uv.array, o * 2); C.set(g.attributes.color.array, o * 3); o += g.attributes.position.count; } if (o > s) out.addGroup(s, o - s, mi); });
    out.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(P, 3)); out.setAttribute('normal', new THREE.BufferAttribute(N, 3)); out.setAttribute('uv', new THREE.BufferAttribute(U, 2)); out.setAttribute('color', new THREE.BufferAttribute(C, 3));
    out.computeBoundingBox(); out.computeBoundingSphere(); return out; };
  // شريط أسطواني بارتفاعات لكل زاوية (وجه الجدار) — UV: طول القوس × الارتفاع
  const band = (ox, oz, R, a0, a1, yb, yt, tile, out) => { const n = Math.max(2, Math.ceil(Math.abs(a1 - a0) * R / 1.5)), P = [], U = [], I = [];
    for (let i = 0; i <= n; i++) { const a = a0 + (a1 - a0) * i / n, x = ox + R * Math.cos(a), z = oz + R * Math.sin(a), y0 = typeof yb === 'function' ? yb(x, z) : yb, y1 = typeof yt === 'function' ? yt(x, z) : yt, u = a * R / tile;
      P.push(x, y0, z, x, y1, z); U.push(u, y0 / tile, u, y1 / tile); if (i) { const k = i * 2; out ? I.push(k - 2, k - 1, k, k, k - 1, k + 1) : I.push(k - 2, k, k - 1, k, k + 1, k - 1); } }
    const g = new THREE.BufferGeometry(); g.setAttribute('position', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(P, 3)); g.setAttribute('uv', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(U, 2)); g.setIndex(I); g.computeVertexNormals(); return g; };
  const annulus = (ox, oz, r0, r1, a0, a1, y, tile, down) => { const n = Math.max(2, Math.ceil(Math.abs(a1 - a0) * r1 / 1.6)), P = [], U = [], I = [];
    for (let i = 0; i <= n; i++) { const a = a0 + (a1 - a0) * i / n, c = Math.cos(a), s = Math.sin(a); for (const r of [r0, r1]) { const x = ox + r * c, z = oz + r * s; P.push(x, typeof y === 'function' ? y(x, z) : y, z); U.push(x / tile, z / tile); }
      if (i) { const k = i * 2; I.push(k - 2, k - 1, k, k, k - 1, k + 1); } }
    const g = new THREE.BufferGeometry(); g.setAttribute('position', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(P, 3)); g.setAttribute('uv', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(U, 2)); g.setIndex(I); g.computeVertexNormals();
    const nn = g.attributes.normal; if ((nn.getY(0) < 0) !== !!down) { for (let i = 0; i < nn.count; i++) nn.setY(i, -nn.getY(i)); const ix = g.index.array; for (let i = 0; i < ix.length; i += 3) { const t = ix[i + 1]; ix[i + 1] = ix[i + 2]; ix[i + 2] = t; } } return g; };
  const box = (w, h, d, x, y, z, ry) => { const g = new THREE.BoxGeometry(w, h, d); if (ry) g.rotateY(ry); return g.translate(x, y + h / 2, z); };
  // درابزين: قاعدة الدرابزين (بالوستر) — مخروط/مخرطة بسيطة (٦ أوجه)
  let BAL = null; const baluster = () => BAL || (BAL = (() => { const pts = [[0.13, 0], [0.13, 0.08], [0.07, 0.12], [0.1, 0.32], [0.05, 0.52], [0.075, 0.62], [0.11, 0.66], [0.11, 0.72]].map(q => new THREE.Vector2(q[0], q[1]));
    const g = proj(new THREE.LatheGeometry(pts, 6), 2.2); const c = new Float32Array(g.attributes.position.count * 3).fill(1); g.setAttribute('color', new THREE.BufferAttribute(c, 3)); SHARED.add(g); return g; })());
  let BALL = null; const balLo = () => BALL || (BALL = (() => { const pts = [[0.12, 0], [0.07, 0.12], [0.085, 0.4], [0.06, 0.56], [0.11, 0.68], [0.11, 0.72]].map(q => new THREE.Vector2(q[0], q[1]));
    const g = proj(new THREE.LatheGeometry(pts, 4), 2.2); const c = new Float32Array(g.attributes.position.count * 3).fill(1); g.setAttribute('color', new THREE.BufferAttribute(c, 3)); SHARED.add(g); return g; })());
  let URN = null; const urn = () => URN || (URN = (() => { const pts = [[0.42, 0], [0.42, 0.12], [0.24, 0.2], [0.22, 0.32], [0.5, 0.55], [0.58, 0.86], [0.52, 0.98], [0.56, 1.04], [0.0, 1.04]].map(q => new THREE.Vector2(q[0], q[1]));
    const parts = [[proj(new THREE.LatheGeometry(pts, 10), 2.2), 1]];
    const bush = [], col = [[0.2, 0.42, 0.14], [0.16, 0.36, 0.12], [0.86, 0.36, 0.52], [0.95, 0.82, 0.92], [0.92, 0.3, 0.32]];
    for (let i = 0; i < 9; i++) { const a = i * 2.39, r = i ? 0.28 + 0.12 * (i % 2) : 0, s = i ? 0.3 : 0.42, g = new THREE.IcosahedronGeometry(s, 0).translate(Math.cos(a) * r, 1.15 + (i ? 0.1 + 0.08 * (i % 3) : 0.22), Math.sin(a) * r), c = new Float32Array(g.attributes.position.count * 3), k = col[i % 2];
      for (let j = 0; j < c.length; j += 3) { c[j] = k[0]; c[j + 1] = k[1]; c[j + 2] = k[2]; } g.setAttribute('color', new THREE.BufferAttribute(c, 3)); g.setAttribute('uv', new THREE.BufferAttribute(new Float32Array(g.attributes.position.count * 2), 2)); bush.push([g, 5, true]); }
    for (let i = 0; i < 14; i++) { const a = i * 1.71, r = 0.22 + 0.2 * ((i * 7) % 3) / 2, g = new THREE.IcosahedronGeometry(0.075, 0).translate(Math.cos(a) * r, 1.3 + 0.12 * (i % 3), Math.sin(a) * r), c = new Float32Array(g.attributes.position.count * 3), k = col[2 + i % 3];
      for (let j = 0; j < c.length; j += 3) { c[j] = k[0]; c[j + 1] = k[1]; c[j + 2] = k[2]; } g.setAttribute('color', new THREE.BufferAttribute(c, 3)); g.setAttribute('uv', new THREE.BufferAttribute(new Float32Array(g.attributes.position.count * 2), 2)); bush.push([g, 5, true]); }
    const geo = merge([...parts, ...bush], 6); SHARED.add(geo); return geo; })());
  let NEWEL = null; const newel = () => NEWEL || (NEWEL = (() => { const p = [[box(0.86, 0.22, 0.86, 0, 0, 0), 1], [box(0.7, 1.05, 0.7, 0, 0.22, 0), 0], [box(0.9, 0.16, 0.9, 0, 1.27, 0), 1],
      [new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.16, 0.24, 0.16, 10).translate(0, 1.51, 0), 4], [new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.22, 12, 8).translate(0, 1.78, 0), 4], [new THREE.ConeGeometry(0.06, 0.26, 8).translate(0, 2.1, 0), 4]];
    const geo = merge(p, 6); SHARED.add(geo); return geo; })());

  function build(L, ctx) { const { cx, cz, H, add, lamps, D2R } = ctx, q = WORLD.hill, T = q.t; if (!T) return; const t0 = performance.now();
    const ox = q.x - cx, oz = q.z - cz, Lv = q.level, H0 = (x, z) => LIB.height(x, z, true), M = mats(), parts = [], PI = Math.PI;
    const LV = [Lv, Lv - 4, Lv - 8], RR = [T.r0, T.r1, T.r2], GW = T.cw + 0.75;   // GW: نصف عرض فتحة الدرج في الدرابزين
    const base = (x, z) => H0(x + cx, z + cz) - 0.7;
    // الزوايا بنظام المدينة المحلي (x = R cos a، z = R sin a): الجنوب a = π/2
    const gapA = R => Math.asin(Math.min(1, GW / R));
    const segs = R => { const g = gapA(R); return [[PI / 2 + g, PI / 2 + 2 * PI - g]]; };            // كل الحلقة ما عدا فتحة الدرج
    const solid = (x, z, w, d, yaw, top) => { const b = topOf(solidBox(x + cx, z + cz, w, d, yaw), top); L.obst.push(b); L.solid.push(b); };
    const balPos = [], urnPos = [], newelPos = [];
    for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) { const R = RR[k], yt = LV[k], yb = k < 2 ? LV[k + 1] : base;
      // وجه الجدار (كامل الحلقة: الدرج يقف أمامه) + قاعدة بارزة + إفريز وغطاء
      parts.push([band(ox, oz, R, 0, 2 * PI, yb, yt - 0.42, 3.4, true), 0, true]);
      parts.push([band(ox, oz, R + 0.22, 0, 2 * PI, k < 2 ? yb : (x, z) => base(x, z), (x, z) => (k < 2 ? yb : base(x, z) + 0.7) + 0.75, 2.6, true), 1, true]);
      parts.push([annulus(ox, oz, R, R + 0.22, 0, 2 * PI, (x, z) => (k < 2 ? yb : base(x, z) + 0.7) + 0.75, 2.6), 1, true]);
      parts.push([band(ox, oz, R + 0.26, 0, 2 * PI, yt - 0.44, yt - 0.14, 2.6, true), 1, true], [annulus(ox, oz, R, R + 0.26, 0, 2 * PI, yt - 0.44, 2.6, true), 1, true]);
      parts.push([band(ox, oz, R + 0.42, 0, 2 * PI, yt - 0.14, yt + 0.06, 2.6, true), 1, true], [annulus(ox, oz, R + 0.26, R + 0.42, 0, 2 * PI, yt - 0.14, 2.6, true), 1, true]);
      // أعمدة جدارية بارزة كل ٩° (بعيداً عن الدرج)
      for (let a = 4.5; a < 360; a += 9) { const ar = a * D2R, x = ox + (R + 0.12) * Math.cos(ar), z = oz + (R + 0.12) * Math.sin(ar); if (Math.sin(ar) > 0 && Math.abs(x - ox) < GW + 1.2) continue;
        const y0 = k < 2 ? yb : base(x, z), g = box(1.05, yt - 0.42 - y0, 0.34, 0, y0, 0); g.rotateY(-ar + PI / 2).translate(x, 0, z); parts.push([g, 1]); }
      // بلاط المصطبة: من الحلقة الأعلى (أو سور القصر للهضبة) حتى حافة الجدار
      const rIn = k === 0 ? 29.4 : RR[k - 1] - 0.05; parts.push([annulus(ox, oz, rIn, R + 0.42, 0, 2 * PI, yt + 0.035, 3.2), 2, true]);
      // الدرابزين على حافة المصطبة (فتحة الدرج جنوباً)
      const Rb = R - 0.38;
      for (const [a0, a1] of segs(R)) { parts.push([band(ox, oz, Rb + 0.24, a0, a1, yt, yt + 0.2, 2.6, true), 1, true], [band(ox, oz, Rb - 0.24, a0, a1, yt, yt + 0.2, 2.6, false), 1, true], [annulus(ox, oz, Rb - 0.24, Rb + 0.24, a0, a1, yt + 0.2, 2.6), 1, true]);
        parts.push([band(ox, oz, Rb + 0.27, a0, a1, yt + 0.86, yt + 1.02, 2.6, true), 1, true], [band(ox, oz, Rb - 0.27, a0, a1, yt + 0.86, yt + 1.02, 2.6, false), 1, true], [annulus(ox, oz, Rb - 0.27, Rb + 0.27, a0, a1, yt + 1.02, 2.6), 1, true], [annulus(ox, oz, Rb - 0.27, Rb + 0.27, a1, a0, yt + 0.86, 2.6), 1, true]);
        const nP = Math.round((a1 - a0) * Rb / 4.2); for (let i = 0; i <= nP; i++) { const a = a0 + (a1 - a0) * i / nP, x = ox + Rb * Math.cos(a), z = oz + Rb * Math.sin(a); parts.push([box(0.62, 1.1, 0.62, x, yt, z, -a), 0], [box(0.74, 0.12, 0.74, x, yt + 1.06, z, -a), 1]);
          if (i < nP) { const nb = 9; for (let j = 1; j < nb; j++) { const aa = a + (a1 - a0) / nP * j / nb; balPos.push([ox + Rb * Math.cos(aa), yt + 0.2, oz + Rb * Math.sin(aa), -aa]); } } }
        // مصدّات: الجدار من الأسفل والدرابزين من الأعلى (قطع ~٣م بنفس صيغة سور القصر)
        const nC = Math.ceil((a1 - a0) * R / 3); for (let i = 0; i < nC; i++) { const a = a0 + (a1 - a0) * (i + 0.5) / nC, ch = (a1 - a0) * R / nC, rc = R - 0.5, x = ox + rc * Math.cos(a), z = oz + rc * Math.sin(a); solid(x, z, ch + 0.35, 1.7, -a + PI / 2, yt + 1.05); } }
      // فوانيس على المصطبة كل ١٥° وأحواض زهر بينها
      for (let a = 7.5; a < 360; a += 15) { const ar = a * D2R, rl = R - 1.35, x = ox + rl * Math.cos(ar), z = oz + rl * Math.sin(ar); if (Math.sin(ar) > 0 && Math.abs(x - ox) < GW + 2.2) continue;
        lamps.push([x + cx, z + cz, Math.atan2(ox - x, oz - z)]); add('pLamp', x + cx, z + cz, Math.atan2(ox - x, oz - z), 3.4); }
      if (k > 0) for (let a = 0; a < 360; a += 15) { const ar = a * D2R, ru = RR[k - 1] + 1.6, x = ox + ru * Math.cos(ar), z = oz + ru * Math.sin(ar); if (Math.sin(ar) > 0 && Math.abs(x - ox) < GW + 2.5) continue; urnPos.push([x, LV[k], z]); solid(x, z, 1.1, 1.1, 0, LV[k] + 1.1); }
    }
    // ── الدرج: ثلاثة أشواط (١٣ درجة) أمام كل جدار، بجدارين جانبيين ودرابزين مائل وأعمدة ذهبية ──
    for (const F of T.fl) { const z0 = oz + F.z0, z1 = oz + F.z1, y0 = F.y0, yb = F.yb, n = F.n, rise = (y0 - yb) / n, run = (z1 - z0) / n, w = 2 * T.cw, yBot = base(ox, z1) - 0.2;
      for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) { const top = yb + rise * (i + 1), zf = z1 - run * i, zb = z0 - 0.8, d = zf - zb;
        parts.push([box(w, top - yBot - 0.07, d, ox, yBot, zb + d / 2), 1], [box(w, 0.07, d + 0.06, ox, top - 0.07, zb + d / 2 + 0.03), 3]); }   // قائمة الدرجة بحجر أغمق وسطحها رخامي فاتح بأنف بارز
      for (const s of [-1, 1]) { const xc = ox + s * (T.cw + 0.36), sh = new THREE.Shape(); sh.moveTo(z0 - 0.8, yBot); sh.lineTo(z1 + 0.35, yBot); sh.lineTo(z1 + 0.35, yb + 0.45); sh.lineTo(z1, yb + 0.45); sh.lineTo(z0, y0 + 0.45); sh.lineTo(z0 - 0.8, y0 + 0.45); sh.closePath();
        const g = new THREE.ExtrudeGeometry(sh, { depth: 0.72, bevelEnabled: false }); g.rotateY(-PI / 2).translate(xc + 0.36, 0, 0); parts.push([g, 0]);
        // الدرابزين المائل: قاعدة وحاجز علوي + بالوسترات على الميل
        const len = Math.hypot(z1 - z0, y0 - yb), ang = Math.atan2(y0 - yb, z1 - z0);
        for (const [yy, hh, ww] of [[0.45, 0.16, 0.5], [1.28, 0.14, 0.56]]) { const g2 = new THREE.BoxGeometry(ww, hh, len + 0.1); g2.rotateX(ang).translate(xc, (y0 + yb) / 2 + yy + hh / 2, (z0 + z1) / 2); parts.push([g2, 1]); }
        const nb = Math.round(len / 0.42); for (let j = 1; j < nb; j++) { const t = j / nb; balPos.push([xc, yb + (y0 - yb) * t + 0.53, z1 - (z1 - z0) * t, 0]); }
        newelPos.push([xc, yb + 0.45, z1 + 0.05], [xc, y0 + 0.45, z0 - 0.2]);
        solid(xc, (z0 + z1) / 2 - 0.4, 0.95, z1 - z0 + 1.0, 0, y0 + 1.5);
  } }
    const geo = merge(parts, 6), mesh = new THREE.Mesh(geo, M); mesh.castShadow = window.__QUALITY === 'high'; mesh.receiveShadow = true; L.det.add(mesh); SHARED.add(geo);
    const inst = (g, mat, arr, sc) => { if (!arr.length) return; const im = new THREE.InstancedMesh(g, mat, arr.length), m4 = new THREE.Matrix4(), Q = new THREE.Quaternion(), S = new THREE.Vector3(sc, sc, sc), P = new THREE.Vector3(), UP = new V3(0, 1, 0);
      arr.forEach((p, i) => im.setMatrixAt(i, m4.compose(P.set(p[0], p[1], p[2]), Q.setFromAxisAngle(UP, p[3] || 0), S))); im.receiveShadow = true; im.castShadow = window.__QUALITY === 'high'; im.computeBoundingSphere(); L.det.add(im); return im; };
    // البالوسترات بمجموعات حسب الربع (لتعمل إزالة ما خارج الكاميرا)
    const BQ = [[], [], [], []]; balPos.forEach(p => BQ[Math.min(3, ((Math.atan2(p[2] - oz, p[0] - ox) + PI) / (PI / 2)) | 0)].push(p)); if (window.LOD16 && window.__LOD16_ON) { const bg = baluster(), FM = { lod: balLo(), lod2: balLo(), lodMat: M[3], lod2Mat: M[3], nsm: true, cull: [60, 140] }, m4 = new THREE.Matrix4(), Q = new THREE.Quaternion(), S1 = new V3(1, 1, 1), P = new V3(), UP = new V3(0, 1, 0);   // v16: الدرابزين عبر LOD16 (إزالة ما خارج الكاميرا + نسخة أخف بعيداً)
      for (const p of balPos) LOD16.addInst(L, bg, M[3], FM, 1, m4.compose(P.set(p[0], p[1], p[2]), Q.setFromAxisAngle(UP, p[3] || 0), S1).clone(), null); } else BQ.forEach(a => inst(baluster(), M[3], a, 1));
    inst(urn(), M, urnPos, 1); inst(newel(), M, newelPos, 1);
    L.hill16 = { tris: geo.index ? geo.index.count / 3 : geo.attributes.position.count / 3, bal: balPos.length, ms: Math.round(performance.now() - t0) }; console.info('[v16] تل القصر', JSON.stringify(L.hill16)); }
  return { build, mats }; })();
window.__V16 = V16;

/* ═══ v16 (apply_v16_dense.py): مدير مستويات التفاصيل لبيوت المدينة — نسخ مكررة بثلاثة مستويات (كامل / مبسّط ~٣٫٥ألف مثلث / بعيد ~٩٠٠) + إزالة ما خارج الكاميرا لكل بيت
   (الأقرب من ~٢٨م يبقى للظل). نفس الشكل من قريب؛ التبسيط فقط حيث لا يُرى الفرق ═══ */
const LOD16 = (() => { const E = new Map(), V3 = THREE.Vector3, fr = new THREE.Frustum(), pm = new THREE.Matrix4(), sp = new THREE.Sphere(), lastP = new V3(1e9, 0, 0), lastQ = new THREE.Quaternion(), _m = new THREE.Matrix4(), _q = new THREE.Quaternion(), _p = new V3(), _s = new V3(), UP = new V3(0, 1, 0), _c = new THREE.Color();
  let frame = 0, stat = { hi: 0, lo: 0, lo2: 0, n: 0 };
  const HQ = () => window.__QUALITY === 'high', modelOf = g => g && g.userData && g.userData.mk ? MODELS[g.userData.mk] : null;
  const lodGeo = (e, lv) => { const M = e.model; if (!M) return null; const src = lv === 1 ? M.lod : M.lod2; if (!src) return null; if (e.k === 1) return src; const key = 'lodk' + lv + ':' + e.k; if (!M[key]) { M[key] = src.clone().scale(e.k, e.k, e.k); M[key].computeBoundingSphere(); SHARED.add(M[key]); } return M[key]; };
  const mkIM = (e, g, mt) => { const im = new THREE.InstancedMesh(g, mt || e.mat, e.cap); im.count = 0; im.frustumCulled = false; im.castShadow = true; im.receiveShadow = true; im.userData.lod16 = 1;
    if (e.tint) { im.setColorAt(0, _c.set(1, 1, 1)); im.instanceColor.setUsage(THREE.DynamicDrawUsage); } im.instanceMatrix.setUsage(THREE.DynamicDrawUsage); e.det.add(im); return im; };
  // تسجيل نسخة: g = الهندسة الكاملة (قد تكون مكبّرة بـ k)، model = نموذج البيت الأصلي (لهندسة التبسيط)، mat4 = مصفوفة النسخة المحلية (داخل det)
  function addInst(L, g, mat, model, k, m4, tint) { let e = E.get(g); if (!e || e.L !== L) { if (e) e.im.forEach(im => im && im.dispose()); e = { L, det: L.det, gq: L.g, g, mat, model, k: k || 1, list: [], tint: !!tint, im: [null, null, null] }; E.set(g, e); }
    if (!g.boundingSphere) g.computeBoundingSphere(); const bs = g.boundingSphere; _p.copy(bs.center).applyMatrix4(m4); const sc = Math.max(m4.elements[0] ** 2 + m4.elements[1] ** 2 + m4.elements[2] ** 2, m4.elements[4] ** 2 + m4.elements[5] ** 2 + m4.elements[6] ** 2, m4.elements[8] ** 2 + m4.elements[9] ** 2 + m4.elements[10] ** 2) ** 0.5;
    e.list.push({ m: m4.toArray(), c: tint || null, x: _p.x, y: _p.y, z: _p.z, r: bs.radius * sc }); e.dirty = true; }
  function addMesh(L, m) { const g = m.geometry, M = modelOf(g); if (!M || !window.__LOD16_ON) return false; m.updateMatrix(); addInst(L, g, m.material, M, 1, m.matrix.clone(), null); return true; }
  function finish(L) { for (const e of E.values()) if (e.L === L && e.dirty) { e.dirty = false; e.cap = e.list.length; e.im.forEach(im => { if (im) { e.det.remove(im); im.dispose(); } }); e.im = [mkIM(e, e.g), null, null]; } lastP.set(1e9, 0, 0); }
  function drop(L) { for (const [g, e] of E) if (e.L === L) { e.im.forEach(im => im && im.dispose()); E.delete(g); } }
  function tick(force) { if (!E.size) return; frame++; const c = camera;
    if (!force && c.position.distanceToSquared(lastP) < 0.8 && 1 - Math.abs(c.quaternion.dot(lastQ)) < 2e-5 && frame % 45) return;
    lastP.copy(c.position); lastQ.copy(c.quaternion); c.updateMatrixWorld(); pm.multiplyMatrices(c.projectionMatrix, c.matrixWorldInverse); fr.setFromProjectionMatrix(pm);
    const hq = HQ(), D1 = hq ? 90 : 20, D2 = hq ? 200 : 52,   /* v17: الموبايل 22→20 / 55→52 م تعويضاً عن حواف الشوارع والزينة */ NS = hq ? 28 : 14, far = (scene.fog ? scene.fog.far : 900) + 40; stat = { hi: 0, lo: 0, lo2: 0, n: 0 };
    for (const e of E.values()) { if (!e.gq.parent || !e.det.visible) continue; const gp = e.gq.position, cx = c.position.x - gp.x, cy = c.position.y - gp.y, cz = c.position.z - gp.z;
      for (let lv = 1; lv < 3; lv++) if (!e.im[lv]) { const lg = lodGeo(e, lv); if (lg) e.im[lv] = mkIM(e, lg, e.model && e.model[lv === 1 ? 'lodMat' : 'lod2Mat']); }
      const cnt = [0, 0, 0], IM = e.im, CUL = e.model && e.model.cull ? e.model.cull[hq ? 1 : 0] : 0;   // تفاصيل صغيرة جداً (درابزين) تُترك بعد مسافة لا تُرى فيها
      for (let i = 0; i < e.list.length; i++) { const q = e.list[i], dx = q.x - cx, dy = q.y - cy, dz = q.z - cz, d = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz) - q.r; if (d > far || (CUL && d > CUL)) continue;
        sp.center.set(q.x + gp.x, q.y + gp.y, q.z + gp.z); sp.radius = q.r; if (!fr.intersectsSphere(sp) && d > NS) continue;
        let lv = window.__LOD16_LV !== undefined ? window.__LOD16_LV : d < D1 ? 0 : d < D2 ? 1 : 2; while (lv > 0 && !IM[lv]) lv--; const im = IM[lv], j = cnt[lv]++;
        im.instanceMatrix.array.set(q.m, j * 16); if (e.tint) { const t = q.c || [1, 1, 1]; im.instanceColor.array[j * 3] = t[0]; im.instanceColor.array[j * 3 + 1] = t[1]; im.instanceColor.array[j * 3 + 2] = t[2]; } }
      for (let lv = 0; lv < 3; lv++) { const im = IM[lv]; if (!im) continue; im.count = cnt[lv]; im.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate = true; if (im.instanceColor) im.instanceColor.needsUpdate = true; im.castShadow = (lv === 0 || hq) && !(e.model && e.model.nsm && !hq); }
      stat.hi += cnt[0]; stat.lo += cnt[1]; stat.lo2 += cnt[2]; stat.n += e.list.length; } }
  return { addInst, addMesh, finish, drop, tick, get stat() { return stat; }, E }; })();
window.LOD16 = LOD16; window.__LOD16_ON = !/nolod/.test(location.search);

/* ═══ v16: تكثيف المدينة — صفوف بيوت متلاصقة على حلقات بأزقة ضيقة وممرات عرضية وساحات صغيرة، تدوير/مرآة/تكبير ٠٫٩–١٫١٥ وارتفاعات مختلفة،
   خزانات ماء وقباب صغيرة ومظلات على بعض الأسطح — كلها نسخ مكررة عبر LOD16، ولكل بيت مصادم. الشوارع الرئيسية والساحة والسوق والجامع وطريق القصر والدرج تبقى فارغة ═══ */
function lod16Load(k, loader) { const M = MODELS[k]; if (!M || !M.house || !window.__LOD16_ON) return; const base = HOUSE_GLB[k] || SHOP_GLB[k]; if (!base) return;
  for (const lv of [1, 2]) loader.load(base.replace('.glb', lv === 1 ? '-lod.glb' : '-lod2.glb'), g => { try { g.scene.updateMatrixWorld(true); let mesh = null; g.scene.traverse(o => { if (o.isMesh && !mesh) mesh = o; }); if (!mesh) return;
      const src = mesh.geometry, geo = new THREE.BufferGeometry(), f32 = (a, n) => { const out = new Float32Array(a.count * n); for (let i = 0; i < a.count; i++) for (let j = 0; j < n; j++) out[i * n + j] = a[GXYZW[j]](i); return new THREE.BufferAttribute(out, n); };
      geo.setAttribute('position', f32(src.attributes.position, 3)); if (src.attributes.normal) geo.setAttribute('normal', f32(src.attributes.normal, 3)); if (src.attributes.uv) geo.setAttribute('uv', f32(src.attributes.uv, 2));
      if (src.index) geo.setIndex(src.index.clone()); geo.applyMatrix4(mesh.matrixWorld); if (!src.attributes.normal) geo.computeVertexNormals(); if (M.off) geo.translate(M.off[0], M.off[1], M.off[2]); geo.computeBoundingSphere(); SHARED.add(geo);
      const lm = mesh.material; if (lm && lm.map) { lm.map.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace; lm.map.anisotropy = 4; lm.map.needsUpdate = true; lm.envMap = CHARENV; lm.envMapIntensity = 0.45; lm.metalness = 0; lm.roughness = 0.85; lm.onBeforeCompile = M.mat.onBeforeCompile; lm.customProgramCacheKey = M.mat.customProgramCacheKey; GLB_MAT.add(lm); M[lv === 1 ? 'lodMat' : 'lod2Mat'] = lm; }
      M[lv === 1 ? 'lod' : 'lod2'] = geo; } catch (e) { console.warn('[v16] LOD ' + k + ': ' + e.message); } }, undefined, () => console.warn('[v16] LOD غير موجود: ' + k + ' ' + lv)); }

/* ═══ v17: المدينة المرتبة (حسب فيديو مصطفى) — شوارع حجر مرصوف بصفوف مستقيمة وحواف ورصيف، واجهات البيوت على خط واحد مع الشارع،
   أقواس حجرية فوق الشوارع، بسطات بمظلات وصناديق خضار، براميل وجرار بجانب الجدران، فوانيس جدارية تضيء عند المغرب، وضوء ذهبي مع ضباب خفيف داخل السور.
   كل الإضافات نسخ مكررة (InstancedMesh) تُعرض قرب اللاعب فقط، وحواف الشوارع والأقواس شبكة واحدة — لا زيادة على الموبايل ═══ */
const V17 = (() => { const D2R = Math.PI / 180, T = {}, ST = { cw: 0, t: 0, dyn: [], L: null };
  const rs = seed => { let s = seed; return () => (s = (s * 16807) % 2147483647) / 2147483647; };
  // ── قوام حجري مرسوم (لون + خريطة نتوء) قابل للتكرار ──
  function stoneTex(kind) { const S = 512, r = rs(kind === 'sett' ? 1717 : 2929), c = document.createElement('canvas'); c.width = c.height = S; const g = c.getContext('2d'), img = g.createImageData(S, S), d = img.data, hg = new Float32Array(S * S);
    const sett = kind === 'sett', rows = sett ? 12 : 6, cols = sett ? 8 : 4, rh = S / rows, cw = S / cols, J = sett ? 3.4 : 2.4, rad = sett ? 9 : 5, bev = sett ? 11 : 6;
    const pal = sett ? [[150, 138, 122], [166, 152, 132], [138, 126, 110], [178, 164, 142], [156, 140, 118], [146, 136, 124]] : [[222, 200, 162], [208, 184, 146], [230, 212, 176], [200, 176, 138], [216, 194, 152]];
    const mo = sett ? [84, 76, 66] : [168, 148, 114];
    for (let i = 0; i < S * S; i++) { d[i * 4] = mo[0]; d[i * 4 + 1] = mo[1]; d[i * 4 + 2] = mo[2]; d[i * 4 + 3] = 255; }
    for (let j = 0; j < rows; j++) { const off = (j % 2) * cw / 2;
      for (let i = 0; i < cols; i++) { const x0 = i * cw + off + J / 2, y0 = j * rh + J / 2, w = cw - J, h = rh - J, pc = pal[(r() * pal.length) | 0], tone = 0.92 + r() * 0.16, hx = w / 2, hy = h / 2, tilt = (r() - 0.5) * 0.25;
        for (let py = 0; py < h; py++) for (let px = 0; px < w; px++) { const qx = Math.abs(px - hx) - (hx - rad), qy = Math.abs(py - hy) - (hy - rad), sd = Math.hypot(Math.max(qx, 0), Math.max(qy, 0)) + Math.min(Math.max(qx, qy), 0) - rad; if (sd > 0) continue;
          const e = Math.min(1, -sd / bev), hh = e * e * (3 - 2 * e) * (0.85 + tilt * (px / w - 0.5)) , n = 0.94 + r() * 0.12, X = ((x0 + px) | 0) % S, Y = ((y0 + py) | 0) % S, k = Y * S + X;
          hg[k] = hh; const sh = tone * n * (0.86 + 0.14 * hh); d[k * 4] = Math.min(255, pc[0] * sh); d[k * 4 + 1] = Math.min(255, pc[1] * sh); d[k * 4 + 2] = Math.min(255, pc[2] * sh); } } }
    g.putImageData(img, 0, 0);
    const cn = document.createElement('canvas'); cn.width = cn.height = S; const gn = cn.getContext('2d'), im2 = gn.createImageData(S, S), dn = im2.data, K = sett ? 3.2 : 2.2;
    for (let y = 0; y < S; y++) for (let x = 0; x < S; x++) { const hL = hg[y * S + ((x + S - 1) % S)], hR = hg[y * S + ((x + 1) % S)], hU = hg[((y + S - 1) % S) * S + x], hD = hg[((y + 1) % S) * S + x];
      let nx = (hL - hR) * K, ny = (hD - hU) * K, nz = 1; const l = Math.hypot(nx, ny, nz); const k = (y * S + x) * 4; dn[k] = (nx / l * 0.5 + 0.5) * 255; dn[k + 1] = (ny / l * 0.5 + 0.5) * 255; dn[k + 2] = (nz / l * 0.5 + 0.5) * 255; dn[k + 3] = 255; }
    gn.putImageData(im2, 0, 0);
    const tc = new THREE.CanvasTexture(c), tn = new THREE.CanvasTexture(cn); for (const t of [tc, tn]) { t.wrapS = t.wrapT = THREE.RepeatWrapping; t.anisotropy = 4; } tc.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace; return [tc, tn]; }
  const tex = k => T[k] || (T[k] = stoneTex(k));
  // ── المواد ──
  let MS = null, MK = null, MP = null, MV = null;
  function settMat() { if (MS) return MS; const [t, n] = tex('sett'); MS = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: t, normalMap: n, normalScale: new THREE.Vector2(1.15, 1.15), roughness: 0.9, polygonOffset: true, polygonOffsetFactor: -2, polygonOffsetUnits: -2 });
    MS.onBeforeCompile = sh => { sh.vertexShader = sh.vertexShader.replace('#include <common>', '#include <common>\nattribute vec3 rq;\nvarying vec3 vRq7;').replace('#include <begin_vertex>', '#include <begin_vertex>\nvRq7 = rq;');
      sh.fragmentShader = sh.fragmentShader.replace('#include <common>', '#include <common>\nvarying vec3 vRq7;')
        .replace('#include <map_fragment>', `float aa7 = abs(vRq7.x), hw7 = max(vRq7.z, 0.6), e7 = hw7 - aa7, bd7 = 1.0 - step(0.56, e7);
          vec2 suv = mix(vec2(vRq7.x * 0.62, vRq7.y * 0.62), vec2(vRq7.y * 0.34, vRq7.x * 0.34 + 0.41), bd7);
          vec4 sc7 = texture2D(map, suv); sc7.rgb *= mix(1.0, 0.84, bd7) * mix(0.72, 1.0, smoothstep(0.0, 0.16, e7));
          diffuseColor *= sc7;
          { vec2 q24 = vW22.xz + uOrg22; float sd24 = n22(q24 * 1.6 + 4.1) * 0.6 + n22(q24 * 0.37 + 9.2) * 0.4;   // v24: حافة الشارع متكسّرة ومغطاة بالرمل بدل خط مستقيم
            diffuseColor.rgb *= 0.84 + 0.3 * h22(floor(suv * 3.0) + 1.3) * (0.6 + 0.4 * n22(q24 * 0.5));   // تفاوت لون الحجارة يكسر تكرار الرصف
            float ed24 = e7 - (sd24 - 0.3) * 0.75; if (ed24 < 0.0) discard;
            float dr24 = (1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 0.9, ed24)) * (0.55 + 0.45 * n22(q24 * 3.3 + 1.7));
            diffuseColor.rgb = mix(diffuseColor.rgb, vec3(0.56, 0.40, 0.22) * (0.85 + 0.3 * n22(q24 * 5.1)), clamp(dr24, 0.0, 0.9)); }`)
        .replace('#include <normal_fragment_maps>', `{ vec3 mapN = texture2D(normalMap, suv).xyz * 2.0 - 1.0; mapN.xy *= normalScale; mat3 tbn7 = getTangentFrame(-vViewPosition, normal, suv); normal = normalize(tbn7 * mapN); }`); };
    MS.customProgramCacheKey = () => 'sett17'; return gv22(MS, 'sett17', 0); }
  function stoneMat() { if (MK) return MK; const [t, n] = tex('flag'); MK = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: t, normalMap: n, vertexColors: true, roughness: 0.88 }); return gv22(MK, 'flag17', 2); }
  function gv22(M, key, bomb) {                          // v22: تنويع لوني عالمي للأرضيات (بقع، اهتراء، تدرّج) حتى لا يظهر البلاط مكرراً ولا يتسطّح فجأة بالبعيد
    const f0 = M.onBeforeCompile;
    M.onBeforeCompile = (sh, r) => { if (f0) f0.call(M, sh, r); sh.uniforms.uOrg22 = U.uOrg;
      sh.vertexShader = sh.vertexShader.replace('#include <common>', '#include <common>\nvarying vec3 vW22;').replace('#include <project_vertex>', '#include <project_vertex>\nvW22 = (modelMatrix * vec4(transformed, 1.0)).xyz;');
      sh.fragmentShader = sh.fragmentShader.replace('#include <common>', '#include <common>\nvarying vec3 vW22; uniform vec2 uOrg22;\nfloat h22(vec2 p){return fract(sin(dot(p,vec2(127.1,311.7)))*43758.5453);}\nfloat n22(vec2 p){vec2 i=floor(p),f=fract(p);f=f*f*(3.-2.*f);return mix(mix(h22(i),h22(i+vec2(1,0)),f.x),mix(h22(i+vec2(0,1)),h22(i+vec2(1,1)),f.x),f.y);}\nvec2 m22(vec2 uv){vec2 c=floor(uv),f=fract(uv);if(h22(c)>0.5)f.x=1.0-f.x;if(h22(c+17.31)>0.5)f.y=1.0-f.y;return c+f;}')
        .replace('#include <emissivemap_fragment>', `{ vec2 q22 = vW22.xz + uOrg22; float a22 = n22(q22 * 0.045), b22 = n22(q22 * 0.21 + 7.3), c22 = n22(q22 * 1.3 + 3.1);
          vec3 dc22 = diffuseColor.rgb * (0.71 + 0.40 * a22 + 0.18 * b22 + 0.12 * (c22 - 0.5)) * mix(vec3(1.05, 0.98, 0.90), vec3(0.95, 0.98, 1.04), n22(q22 * 0.018 + 5.0)); float lu22 = dot(dc22, vec3(0.3, 0.59, 0.11));
          float dt22 = smoothstep(0.55, 0.85, n22(q22 * 0.065 + 11.0)) * 0.6, wt22 = smoothstep(0.66, 0.9, n22(q22 * 0.11 + 23.0)) * 0.25;
          dc22 = mix(dc22, lu22 * vec3(0.98, 0.84, 0.62), dt22 * 0.8) * (1.0 - dt22 * 0.35); dc22 = mix(dc22, lu22 * vec3(1.05, 1.02, 0.96) * 1.12, wt22);
          diffuseColor.rgb = dc22; }
#include <emissivemap_fragment>`);
      if (bomb === 1) sh.fragmentShader = sh.fragmentShader.replace('texture2D(map, vMapUv)', 'textureGrad(map, m22(vMapUv), dFdx(vMapUv), dFdy(vMapUv))');   // قلب عشوائي لكل تكرار للقوام: بلا خطوط وصل لأن القوام قابل للتبليط
      if (bomb === 2) sh.fragmentShader = sh.fragmentShader.replace('#include <map_fragment>', 'diffuseColor *= textureGrad(map, m22(vMapUv), dFdx(vMapUv), dFdy(vMapUv));'); };
    M.customProgramCacheKey = () => key + '+g22'; return M; }

  function paveMat() { if (MV) return MV; const [t, n] = tex('flag'); MV = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: t, normalMap: n, normalScale: new THREE.Vector2(0.8, 0.8), color: 0xe8dccb, roughness: 0.92, polygonOffset: true, polygonOffsetFactor: -1, polygonOffsetUnits: -1 });
    MV.onBeforeCompile = sh => { sh.uniforms.pvN = { value: ROADTEX ? ROADTEX.nz : null };
      sh.vertexShader = sh.vertexShader.replace('#include <common>', '#include <common>\nattribute float pk;\nvarying float vPk;\nvarying vec2 vPw;').replace('#include <begin_vertex>', '#include <begin_vertex>\nvPk = pk; vPw = position.xz;');
      sh.fragmentShader = sh.fragmentShader.replace('#include <common>', '#include <common>\nvarying float vPk;\nvarying vec2 vPw;\nuniform sampler2D pvN;')
        .replace('#include <map_fragment>', `vec3 nz2 = texture2D(pvN, vPw * 0.047).rgb, nz = texture2D(pvN, vPw * 0.009).rgb; if (vPk + (nz2.r - 0.5) * 0.5 < 0.5) discard;
          vec4 col = texture2D(map, vMapUv); col.rgb *= 0.9 + 0.16 * nz.g; diffuseColor *= col;
          { vec2 q24 = vW22.xz + uOrg22; vec2 tc24 = floor(vMapUv * 2.0); diffuseColor.rgb *= 0.86 + 0.26 * h22(tc24 + 3.7);   // v24: لون مختلف لكل بلاطة + رمل منجرف بلطخات كبيرة يكسر التكرار
            float sd24 = smoothstep(0.42, 0.78, n22(q24 * 0.075 + 2.0)) * (0.55 + 0.45 * n22(q24 * 0.6 + 8.0)) + smoothstep(0.7, 0.95, n22(q24 * 0.33 + 5.5)) * 0.35;
            diffuseColor.rgb = mix(diffuseColor.rgb, vec3(0.56, 0.40, 0.22) * (0.8 + 0.4 * n22(q24 * 2.7)), clamp(sd24, 0.0, 0.85)); }`); };
    MV.customProgramCacheKey = () => 'pave17'; return gv22(MV, 'pave17', 1); }
  function propMat() { if (MP) return MP; MP = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ vertexColors: true, roughness: 0.8, emissive: 0xffa040, emissiveIntensity: 0.1 });
    MP.onBeforeCompile = sh => { sh.fragmentShader = sh.fragmentShader.replace('#include <emissivemap_fragment>', '#include <emissivemap_fragment>\ntotalEmissiveRadiance *= step(0.95, vColor.r) * step(0.8, vColor.g) * (1.0 - step(0.62, vColor.b));'); };
    MP.customProgramCacheKey = () => 'prop17'; return MP; }
  // ── دمج أجزاء بألوان رؤوس (مع uv اختيارياً) ──
  function merge(parts, keepUV) { const gs = parts.map(([g0, c]) => { let g = g0.index ? g0.toNonIndexed() : g0; const n = g.attributes.position.count, col = new Float32Array(n * 3); for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) { col[i * 3] = c[0]; col[i * 3 + 1] = c[1]; col[i * 3 + 2] = c[2]; } g.setAttribute('color', new THREE.BufferAttribute(col, 3)); if (!keepUV && g.attributes.uv) g.deleteAttribute('uv'); if (g.attributes.uv1) g.deleteAttribute('uv1'); return g; });
    let n = 0; gs.forEach(g => n += g.attributes.position.count); const P = new Float32Array(n * 3), N = new Float32Array(n * 3), C = new Float32Array(n * 3), U2 = keepUV ? new Float32Array(n * 2) : null; let o = 0;
    gs.forEach(g => { P.set(g.attributes.position.array, o * 3); N.set(g.attributes.normal.array, o * 3); C.set(g.attributes.color.array, o * 3); if (U2) U2.set(g.attributes.uv ? g.attributes.uv.array : new Float32Array(g.attributes.position.count * 2), o * 2); o += g.attributes.position.count; });
    const out = new THREE.BufferGeometry(); out.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(P, 3)); out.setAttribute('normal', new THREE.BufferAttribute(N, 3)); out.setAttribute('color', new THREE.BufferAttribute(C, 3)); if (U2) out.setAttribute('uv', new THREE.BufferAttribute(U2, 2)); out.computeBoundingSphere(); return out; }
  // ── قطع الزينة (النقطة: أسفل الجدار، +z نحو الشارع) ──
  const WOOD = [0.42, 0.28, 0.16], WOOD2 = [0.52, 0.37, 0.22], IRON = [0.13, 0.12, 0.11], CLAY = [0.66, 0.37, 0.22], CLAY2 = [0.58, 0.31, 0.18], GLASS = [1.0, 0.86, 0.5], CREAM = [0.93, 0.88, 0.76], RED = [0.66, 0.16, 0.12];
  const V2 = (x, y) => new THREE.Vector2(x, y);
  const barrel = (x, z, s) => { const pr = [V2(0.27, 0), V2(0.32, 0.2), V2(0.34, 0.45), V2(0.32, 0.7), V2(0.27, 0.9)].map(p => V2(p.x * s, p.y * s));
    return [[new THREE.LatheGeometry(pr, 10).translate(x, 0, z), WOOD], [new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.27 * s, 0.27 * s, 0.02, 10).translate(x, 0.9 * s, z), WOOD2], [new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.335 * s, 0.335 * s, 0.05, 10, 1, true).translate(x, 0.22 * s, z), IRON], [new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.335 * s, 0.335 * s, 0.05, 10, 1, true).translate(x, 0.68 * s, z), IRON]]; };
  const jar = (x, z, s, c) => { const pr = [V2(0.01, 0), V2(0.12, 0.02), V2(0.21, 0.16), V2(0.25, 0.36), V2(0.21, 0.56), V2(0.11, 0.69), V2(0.09, 0.76), V2(0.13, 0.8)].map(p => V2(p.x * s, p.y * s)); return [[new THREE.LatheGeometry(pr, 9).translate(x, 0, z), c || CLAY]]; };
  const fruitC = [[0.74, 0.12, 0.08], [0.95, 0.5, 0.1], [0.9, 0.79, 0.2], [0.32, 0.52, 0.16], [0.36, 0.14, 0.32], [0.62, 0.7, 0.2]];
  const crate = (x, y, z, yaw, ci, full) => { const out = [[new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.56, 0.26, 0.4).translate(0, 0.13, 0), WOOD2]]; if (full) { out.push([new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.5, 0.06, 0.34).translate(0, 0.25, 0), fruitC[ci].map(v => v * 0.8)]);
      for (const [fx, fz] of [[-0.14, -0.07], [0.12, -0.08], [-0.02, 0.08], [0.17, 0.09]]) out.push([new THREE.IcosahedronGeometry(0.075, 0).translate(fx, 0.3, fz), fruitC[ci]]); }
    return out.map(([g, c]) => [g.rotateY(yaw).translate(x, y, z), c]); };
  const lantern = (x, y, z) => [[new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.05, 0.05, z + 0.02).translate(x, y + 0.42, z / 2), IRON], [new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.24, 0.32, 0.24).translate(x, y, z + 0.04), GLASS], [new THREE.ConeGeometry(0.2, 0.18, 6).translate(x, y + 0.25, z + 0.04), IRON], [new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.26, 0.04, 0.26).translate(x, y - 0.17, z + 0.04), IRON], [new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.03, 0.1, 0.03).translate(x, y + 0.37, z + 0.04), IRON]];
  let G = null; function geos() { if (G) return G;
    const cA = merge([...barrel(-0.38, 0.4, 1), ...barrel(0.32, 0.42, 0.92), ...jar(0.95, 0.34, 1.15), ...lantern(0.0, 2.55, 0.32)]);
    const cB = merge([...jar(-0.55, 0.3, 1.0), ...jar(-0.05, 0.3, 0.8, CLAY2), ...jar(0.45, 0.32, 1.12), ...crate(1.15, 0, 0.32, 0.1, 1, true), ...crate(1.12, 0.26, 0.3, -0.05, 3, true), ...lantern(0.05, 2.55, 0.32)]);
    const st = []; const post = (x, z, h) => st.push([new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.08, h, 0.08).translate(x, h / 2, z), WOOD]);
    post(-1.15, 0.62, 2.25); post(1.15, 0.62, 2.25); post(-1.15, -0.62, 2.55); post(1.15, -0.62, 2.55);
    st.push([new THREE.BoxGeometry(2.36, 0.07, 1.1).translate(0, 0.82, 0.05), WOOD2], [new THREE.BoxGeometry(2.36, 0.62, 0.04).translate(0, 0.5, 0.6), WOOD], [new THREE.BoxGeometry(2.3, 0.05, 0.9).translate(0, 0.3, 0.05), WOOD]);
    for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) st.push(...crate(-0.86 + i * 0.575, 0.855, 0.12, 0, (i * 2 + 1) % 6, true));
    st.push(...crate(-0.55, 0, 0.2, 0.05, 4, false), ...crate(0.5, 0, 0.18, -0.04, 0, false));   // صناديق فارغة تحت الطاولة (لا شيء مبعثر على الأرض)
    { const NS = 7, wS = 2.6 / NS; for (let i = 0; i < NS; i++) { const g = new THREE.BoxGeometry(wS, 0.03, 1.95); g.rotateX(-Math.atan2(0.42, 1.95)); g.translate(-1.3 + wS * (i + 0.5), 2.43, 0.12); st.push([g, i % 2 ? RED : CREAM]);
        st.push([new THREE.BoxGeometry(wS, 0.24, 0.02).translate(-1.3 + wS * (i + 0.5), 2.1, 1.1), i % 2 ? CREAM : RED]); } }
    st.push(...lantern(0.0, 1.92, 0.95).slice(1));
    G = { cA, cB, stall: merge(st) }; return G; }
  // ── مدير النسخ القريبة: نعرض فقط ما هو ضمن نصف قطر من الكاميرا ──
  function dyn(L, geo, list) { if (!list.length) return null; const im = new THREE.InstancedMesh(geo, propMat(), list.length); im.count = 0; im.frustumCulled = false; im.visible = false; im.castShadow = false; im.receiveShadow = true;
    const M = new Float32Array(list.length * 16), m4 = new THREE.Matrix4(), q = new THREE.Quaternion(), UP = new THREE.Vector3(0, 1, 0), p = new THREE.Vector3(), s = new THREE.Vector3();
    list.forEach((e, i) => { m4.compose(p.set(e[0], e[1], e[2]), q.setFromAxisAngle(UP, e[3]), s.set(e[4], e[4], e[4])); m4.toArray(M, i * 16); });
    L.det.add(im); const D = { im, M, P: list.map(e => [e[0], e[2]]), L }; ST.dyn.push(D); return D; }
  function dynTick() { const hq = window.__QUALITY === 'high', R = hq ? 95 : 48, R2 = R * R; ST.dyn = ST.dyn.filter(D => D.L.g.parent);
    for (const D of ST.dyn) { const gp = D.L.g.position, cxp = camera.position.x - gp.x, czp = camera.position.z - gp.z, A = D.im.instanceMatrix.array; let n = 0;
      if (D.L.det.visible) for (let i = 0; i < D.P.length; i++) { const dx = D.P[i][0] - cxp, dz = D.P[i][1] - czp; if (dx * dx + dz * dz < R2) { for (let k = 0; k < 16; k++) A[n * 16 + k] = D.M[i * 16 + k]; n++; } }
      D.im.count = n; D.im.visible = n > 0; D.im.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate = true; D.im.castShadow = hq; } }
  // ── الشوارع المرسومة (نفس تخطيط buildCity) ──
  const streets = CR => ({ rings: [[72, 7], [118, 6], [143, 4]], rads: [[0, 62, CR, 6], [180, 62, CR, 6], [270, 62, CR, 10], [90, 143, CR, 6], [225, 72, 143, 6], [315, 72, 143, 6], [45, 118, 143, 6], [135, 118, 143, 6]] });
  function nearest(dx, dz, S) { let best = null; const r = Math.hypot(dx, dz) || 1e-6;
    for (const [R, w] of S.rings) { const d = Math.abs(r - R), sg = r > R ? 1 : -1, c = { dist: d, nx: dx / r * sg, nz: dz / r * sg, w }; if (!best || d - w / 2 < best.dist - best.w / 2) best = c; }
    for (const [a, r0, r1, w] of S.rads) { const ux = Math.cos(a * D2R), uz = -Math.sin(a * D2R), t = dx * ux + dz * uz; if (t < r0 - 1 || t > r1 + 1) continue; const px = dx - t * ux, pz = dz - t * uz, d = Math.hypot(px, pz) || 1e-6, c = { dist: d, nx: px / d, nz: pz / d, w };
      if (!best || d - w / 2 < best.dist - best.w / 2) best = c; }
    return best; }
  const wrap = a => { while (a > Math.PI) a -= 2 * Math.PI; while (a < -Math.PI) a += 2 * Math.PI; return a; };
  // ── (١) واجهات على خط واحد: تدوير كل بيت قريب من شارع ليوازيه، ودفعه حتى تقف واجهته خلف الرصيف مباشرة (بدون تداخل) ──
  function snap(out, props, ctx) { const t0 = performance.now(), { cx, cz, OC, cell } = ctx, S = streets(CITY_R), SB = 0.9, ID = new Int32Array(OC.length), st = { rot: 0, mv: 0, face: 0, side: 0, n: out.length };
    props.tank.length = 0; props.awn.length = 0; props.dome.length = 0;   // خزانات الماء والمظلات الزرقاء القديمة تُستبدل بزينة v17
    const fp = (q, x, z, yaw, pad) => { const M = MODELS[q.k], hw = M.w * q.s / 2 + pad, hd = M.d * q.s / 2 + pad, c = Math.cos(yaw), s = Math.sin(yaw), o = [];
      for (let u = -hw; u <= hw + 1e-3; u += 0.45) for (let v = -hd; v <= hd + 1e-3; v += 0.45) { const uu = Math.min(u, hw), vv = Math.min(v, hd), ce = cell(x + uu * c + vv * s, z - uu * s + vv * c); o.push(ce); } return o; };
    out.forEach((q, i) => { for (const ce of fp(q, q.x, q.z, q.yaw, 0.15)) if (ce >= 0) ID[ce] = i + 1; });
    const free = (i, q, x, z, yaw) => { for (const ce of fp(q, x, z, yaw, -0.2)) { if (ce < 0) return false; const o = ID[ce]; if (o && o !== i + 1) return false; if (!o && OC[ce] === 1) return false; } return true; };
    const order = out.map((q, i) => { const s2 = nearest(q.x - cx, q.z - cz, S); return [i, s2 ? s2.dist - s2.w / 2 : 1e9, s2]; }).sort((a, b) => a[1] - b[1]);
    const fronts = [];
    for (const [i, , s2] of order) { const q = out[i]; if (!s2) continue; const M = MODELS[q.k], w = M.w * q.s, d = M.d * q.s;
      const fyaw = Math.atan2(-s2.nx, -s2.nz), dl = wrap(fyaw - q.yaw), sq = Math.abs(w - d) < 0.9, dm = dl - Math.PI * Math.round(dl / Math.PI); let rot, side = false;
      if (sq || Math.abs(dm) < 0.45) rot = dl;                         // الواجهة نحو الشارع (قلب ١٨٠° يحفظ البصمة نفسها)
      else { rot = dl - Math.PI / 2 * Math.round(dl / (Math.PI / 2)); side = true; }   // الجانب على الشارع: موازاة فقط
      const hn = side ? w / 2 : d / 2, gap = s2.dist - hn - s2.w / 2; if (gap > 10) continue;
      const nyaw = q.yaw + rot, target = s2.w / 2 + 0.3 + SB + hn, mv0 = target - s2.dist; let done = false;
      for (const k of (Math.abs(mv0) < 7 ? [1, 0.7, 0.4, 0] : [0])) { const mv = mv0 * k, nx = q.x + s2.nx * mv, nz = q.z + s2.nz * mv;
        if (free(i, q, nx, nz, nyaw)) { for (const ce of fp(q, q.x, q.z, q.yaw, 0.15)) if (ce >= 0 && ID[ce] === i + 1) ID[ce] = 0; q.x = nx; q.z = nz; q.yaw = nyaw; for (const ce of fp(q, q.x, q.z, q.yaw, 0.15)) if (ce >= 0) ID[ce] = i + 1;
          st.rot++; if (k) st.mv++; done = true; if (!side && gap + mv0 * k < 4.5) { fronts.push([q.x + Math.sin(q.yaw) * d / 2, q.z + Math.cos(q.yaw) * d / 2, q.yaw, w]); st.face++; } else if (side) st.side++; break; } }
      if (!done) continue; }
    st.ms = Math.round(performance.now() - t0); ST.snap = st; return fronts; }
  // ── (٢) الشوارع: حجر مرصوف بصفوف + إطار حجري على الحواف + حافة رصيف مرفوعة + أقواس ──
  function build(L, rm, ctx) { const t0 = performance.now(), { cx, cz, H, onHill, gates } = ctx, CR = CITY_R, roads = L.roads || [], stat = {};
    rm.material = settMat(); if (L.pave15) L.pave15.material = paveMat();
    // حافة الرصيف: قطع ≤ ٣م على جانبي كل شارع، تُحذف داخل التقاطعات والساحة والسوق والبوابات
    const segD = (px, pz, ax, az, bx, bz) => { const vx = bx - ax, vz = bz - az, l2 = vx * vx + vz * vz || 1, t = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, ((px - ax) * vx + (pz - az) * vz) / l2)); return Math.hypot(px - ax - vx * t, pz - az - vz * t); };
    const inRoad = (x, z, self) => { for (const R of roads) { if (R === self) continue; const p = R.pts; for (let i = 0; i < p.length - 1; i++) if (Math.abs(p[i][0] - x) < 12 + R.w && Math.abs(p[i][1] - z) < 12 + R.w && segD(x, z, p[i][0], p[i][1], p[i + 1][0], p[i + 1][1]) < R.w / 2 + 0.45) return true; } return false; };
    const MKr = ctx.mk, bad = (x, z, self) => { const r = Math.hypot(x - cx, z - cz); return r < 65 || r > CR - 2.5 || onHill(x, z, 2) || (MKr && Math.abs(x - MKr.x) < MKr.hx + 1 && Math.abs(z - MKr.z) < MKr.hz + 1) || gates.some(g => Math.hypot(x - g[0], z - g[1]) < 11) || inRoad(x, z, self); };
    const PP = [], NN = [], UU = [], CC = [], KW = 0.3, KH = 0.15;
    const quad = (a, b, c, d, n, uv) => { for (const [p, t] of [[a, uv[0]], [b, uv[1]], [c, uv[2]], [a, uv[0]], [c, uv[2]], [d, uv[3]]]) { PP.push(p[0], p[1], p[2]); NN.push(n[0], n[1], n[2]); UU.push(t[0], t[1]); const v = 0.93 + 0.05 * Math.sin(p[0] * 1.7 + p[2] * 2.3); CC.push(0.98 * v, 0.93 * v, 0.84 * v); } };
    let kn = 0;
    for (const R of roads) { const p = R.pts; if (p.length < 2 || R.nk) continue; let al = 0;
      for (let i = 0; i < p.length - 1; i++) { const ax = p[i][0], az = p[i][1], bx = p[i + 1][0], bz = p[i + 1][1], L0 = Math.hypot(bx - ax, bz - az); if (L0 < 0.01) continue; const tx = (bx - ax) / L0, tz = (bz - az) / L0, ns = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(L0 / 3));
        for (let k = 0; k < ns; k++) { const f0 = k / ns, f1 = (k + 1) / ns, x0 = ax + (bx - ax) * f0, z0 = az + (bz - az) * f0, x1 = ax + (bx - ax) * f1, z1 = az + (bz - az) * f1, u0 = (al + L0 * f0) / 2.4, u1 = (al + L0 * f1) / 2.4;
          for (const s of [-1, 1]) { const lx = -tz * s, lz = tx * s, o0 = R.w / 2, o1 = R.w / 2 + KW, mx = (x0 + x1) / 2 + lx * (o0 + KW / 2), mz = (z0 + z1) / 2 + lz * (o0 + KW / 2); if (bad(mx, mz, R)) continue;
            const P = (x, z, o, dy) => { const X = x + lx * o, Z = z + lz * o; return [X - cx, H(X, Z) + dy, Z - cz]; };
            const a0 = P(x0, z0, o0, 0.12), a1 = P(x1, z1, o0, 0.12), t0_ = P(x0, z0, o0, 0.12 + KH), t1_ = P(x1, z1, o0, 0.12 + KH), b0 = P(x0, z0, o1, 0.12 + KH), b1 = P(x1, z1, o1, 0.12 + KH), g0 = P(x0, z0, o1, 0.05), g1 = P(x1, z1, o1, 0.05);
            quad(t0_, t1_, b1, b0, [0, 1, 0], [[u0, 0], [u1, 0], [u1, KW / 2.4], [u0, KW / 2.4]]);
            quad(a0, a1, t1_, t0_, [-lx, 0, -lz], [[u0, 0.3], [u1, 0.3], [u1, 0.3 + KH / 2.4], [u0, 0.3 + KH / 2.4]]);
            quad(b0, b1, g1, g0, [lx, 0, lz], [[u0, 0.6], [u1, 0.6], [u1, 0.6 + KH / 2.4], [u0, 0.6 + KH / 2.4]]); kn++; } }
        al += L0; } }
    // الأقواس: فوق الشوارع الشعاعية والحلقية في نقاط مختارة، الدعامتان خارج الحافة وتُحذف إن لامست بيتاً/مصادماً
    const arches = [], AC = [0.96, 0.86, 0.7], ART = [];
    const cand = []; const PR = (r, a) => [cx + r * Math.cos(a * D2R), cz - r * Math.sin(a * D2R)];
    for (const [a, rr, w] of [[270, 78, 10], [270, 104, 10], [270, 132, 10], [0, 82, 6], [0, 104, 6], [0, 131, 6], [180, 82, 6], [180, 104, 6], [180, 131, 6], [225, 96, 6], [315, 96, 6], [225, 131, 6], [315, 131, 6], [45, 131, 6], [135, 131, 6]]) { const [x, z] = PR(rr, a); cand.push([x, z, Math.cos(a * D2R), -Math.sin(a * D2R), w]); }
    for (let k = 0; k < 8; k++) { const a = 22.5 + k * 45; const [x, z] = PR(118, a); if (!onHill(x, z, 6)) cand.push([x, z, -Math.sin(a * D2R), -Math.cos(a * D2R), 6]); }
    for (const a of [200, 250, 290, 340]) { const [x, z] = PR(72, a); if (!onHill(x, z, 6)) cand.push([x, z, -Math.sin(a * D2R), -Math.cos(a * D2R), 7]); }
    const solidNear = (x, z, r) => L.solid.some(o => Math.abs(o[0] - x) < 30 && Math.abs(o[1] - z) < 30 && sdist(o, x, z) < r);
    for (const [x, z, tx, tz, w] of (window.V18 ? [] : cand)) { const sp = w + 2 * KW + 0.5, pw = 1.1, lx = -tz, lz = tx, pier = [[x + lx * (sp / 2 + pw / 2), z + lz * (sp / 2 + pw / 2)], [x - lx * (sp / 2 + pw / 2), z - lz * (sp / 2 + pw / 2)]];
      if (pier.some(([px, pz]) => solidNear(px, pz, 0.35) || gates.some(g => Math.hypot(px - g[0], pz - g[1]) < 14))) continue;
      if (arches.some(q => Math.hypot(q[0] - x, q[1] - z) < 14)) continue;
      const spH = w >= 10 ? 4.6 : 3.7, Ht = spH + sp / 2 + 0.9, W = sp + 2 * pw, sh = new THREE.Shape();
      sh.moveTo(-W / 2, -0.8); sh.lineTo(-sp / 2, -0.8); sh.lineTo(-sp / 2, spH); sh.absarc(0, spH, sp / 2, Math.PI, 0, true); sh.lineTo(sp / 2, -0.8); sh.lineTo(W / 2, -0.8); sh.lineTo(W / 2, Ht); sh.lineTo(-W / 2, Ht); sh.lineTo(-W / 2, -0.8);
      const g = new THREE.ExtrudeGeometry(sh, { depth: 1.0, bevelEnabled: false, curveSegments: 10 }); g.translate(0, 0, -0.5); { const uv = g.attributes.uv; for (let i = 0; i < uv.count; i++) uv.setXY(i, uv.getX(i) / 2.4, uv.getY(i) / 2.4); }
      const cor = new THREE.BoxGeometry(W + 0.3, 0.28, 1.3).translate(0, Ht + 0.14, 0), y0 = Math.min(H(pier[0][0], pier[0][1]), H(pier[1][0], pier[1][1]), H(x, z)) - 0.05, yaw = Math.atan2(tx, tz);
      for (const gg of [g, cor]) { gg.rotateY(yaw); gg.translate(x - cx, y0, z - cz); ART.push([gg, AC]); }
      for (const [px, pz] of pier) { const b = topOf(solidBox(px, pz, pw, 1.0, yaw), y0 + Ht + 0.28); L.obst.push(b); L.solid.push(b); }
      arches.push([x, z, yaw, sp, spH]); }
    let ag = null; if (ART.length) ag = merge(ART, true);
    const kg = new THREE.BufferGeometry(); kg.setAttribute('position', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(PP, 3)); kg.setAttribute('normal', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(NN, 3)); kg.setAttribute('uv', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(UU, 2)); kg.setAttribute('color', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(CC, 3));
    const all = ag ? (() => { const A = [kg, ag]; let n = 0; A.forEach(g => n += g.attributes.position.count); const o = new THREE.BufferGeometry(); for (const [k, c] of [['position', 3], ['normal', 3], ['uv', 2], ['color', 3]]) { const arr = new Float32Array(n * c); let off = 0; A.forEach(g => { arr.set(g.attributes[k].array, off); off += g.attributes[k].array.length; }); o.setAttribute(k, new THREE.BufferAttribute(arr, c)); } return o; })() : kg;
    all.computeBoundingSphere(); const km = new THREE.Mesh(all, stoneMat()); km.receiveShadow = true; km.castShadow = window.__QUALITY === 'high'; L.det.add(km); L.v17stone = km;
    stat.kerbs = kn; stat.arches = arches.length; stat.tris = all.attributes.position.count / 3; L.v17arches = arches; ST.build = stat; stat.ms = Math.round(performance.now() - t0); return stat; }
  // ── (٣) زينة الشارع: براميل وجرار وفوانيس عند الواجهات، وبسطات على جانبي الشارع الرئيسي ──
  function dress(L, ctx) { const { cx, cz, H, gates } = ctx, fr = (L.v17fronts || []).slice(), cl = [[], []], stalls = [], arches = L.v17arches || [];
    for (const h of L.houses || []) { if (!h.door || !h.T) continue; const dw = [h.door[0] - h.x, h.door[1] - h.z], dl = Math.hypot(dw[0], dw[1]) || 1, fd = Math.max(0.5, dl - 1.4); fr.push([h.x + dw[0] / dl * fd, h.z + dw[1] / dl * fd, Math.atan2(dw[0], dw[1]), h.T.W]); }
    const S = streets(CITY_R), used = [];
    fr.forEach((f, i) => { const [fx, fz, yaw, w] = f, s2 = nearest(fx - cx, fz - cz, S); if (!s2 || s2.dist - s2.w / 2 > 6.5) return; if (Math.hypot(fx - cx, fz - cz) < 66) return; if (gates.some(g => Math.hypot(fx - g[0], fz - g[1]) < 14)) return;
      const lx = Math.cos(yaw), lz = -Math.sin(yaw), off = Math.min(w * 0.3, 2.2), px = fx + lx * off, pz = fz + lz * off; if (used.some(u => Math.hypot(u[0] - px, u[1] - pz) < 5.5)) return;
      if (arches.some(a => Math.hypot(a[0] - px, a[1] - pz) < 5)) return; used.push([px, pz]); cl[used.length % 2].push([px - cx, H(px, pz) - 0.02, pz - cz, yaw, 1]); });
    // البسطات: صفّان مستقيمان على حافتي الشارع الرئيسي (جنوب)، بينهما ممر واسع
    const MKr = ctx.mk;
    if (false) /* v35: بسطات محذوفة */ for (let r = 70; r <= CITY_R - 14; r += 7.5) { if (Math.abs(r - 118) < 6 || Math.abs(r - 72) < 5.5 || Math.abs(r - 143) < 5) continue; for (const s of [-1, 1]) { const x = cx + s * 3.95, z = cz + r; if (MKr && Math.abs(x - MKr.x) < MKr.hx + 2 && Math.abs(z - MKr.z) < MKr.hz + 2) continue;
