        if (arches.some(a => Math.hypot(a[0] - x, a[1] - z) < 3.2)) continue; if (L.solid.some(o => Math.abs(o[0] - x) < 20 && Math.abs(o[1] - z) < 20 && sdist(o, x, z) < 1.4)) continue; const yaw = s > 0 ? -Math.PI / 2 : Math.PI / 2; stalls.push([x - cx, H(x, z) - 0.02, z - cz, yaw, 1]);
        const b = topOf(solidBox(x, z, 2.4, 1.3, yaw), H(x, z) + 0.9); L.obst.push(b); L.solid.push(b); } }
    const g = geos(); dyn(L, g.cA, cl[0]); dyn(L, g.cB, cl[1]); dyn(L, g.stall, stalls);
    ST.dress = { clA: cl[0].length, clB: cl[1].length, stalls: stalls.length }; return ST.dress; }
  // ── (٤) الإضاءة الذهبية والضباب الخفيف داخل المدينة + تحديث الفوانيس والنسخ القريبة ──
  const GOLD = new THREE.Color(1.0, 0.57, 0.27), WSKY = new THREE.Color(1.0, 0.78, 0.52), HAZE = new THREE.Color(0.98, 0.74, 0.47), HAZEZ = new THREE.Color(0.64, 0.69, 0.78), _d = new THREE.Vector3();
  function tick(dt, sd) { try { const C = typeof CITY !== 'undefined' ? CITY : null; if (!C) return; const dc = Math.hypot(PL.x - C.x, PL.z - C.z), tgt = THREE.MathUtils.smoothstep(CITY_R + 70 - dc, 0, 80);
      ST.cw += (tgt - ST.cw) * Math.min(1, Math.max(dt, 0.03) * 2.0); const day = 1 - U.uNight.value, k = ST.cw * day * (1 - ENV.cloud * 0.55) * (window.__V17_GRADE === 0 ? 0 : 1);
      if (MP) MP.emissiveIntensity = 0.08 + 4.0 * Math.pow(U.uNight.value, 0.6) + 1.2 * Math.max(0, 1 - Math.abs(sd.y) / 0.25);
      renderer.toneMappingExposure = 1.2 - 0.1 * k;
      if (k > 0.002) { sun.color.lerp(GOLD, 0.86 * k); sun.intensity *= 1 + 0.18 * k; hemi.color.lerp(WSKY, 0.6 * k); hemi.intensity *= 1 - 0.3 * k; U.uHor.value.lerp(HAZE, 0.8 * k); U.uZen.value.lerp(HAZEZ, 0.45 * k); scene.fog.color.lerp(HAZE, 0.8 * k);
        scene.fog.near += (10 - scene.fog.near) * 0.9 * k; scene.fog.far += (Math.max(300, scene.fog.far * 0.55) - scene.fog.far) * 0.85 * k;
        _d.copy(sd); if (_d.y > 0.3) { const y = _d.y + (0.3 - _d.y) * k; const h = Math.hypot(_d.x, _d.z) || 1e-3, hn = Math.sqrt(Math.max(0, 1 - y * y)); _d.set(_d.x / h * hn, y, _d.z / h * hn); sun.position.copy(_d).multiplyScalar(200).add(avatar.position); } }
      ST.t -= dt; if (ST.t <= 0) { ST.t = 0.25; dynTick(); } } catch (e) { if (!ST.err) { ST.err = 1; console.warn('[v17] tick', e); } } }
  return { snap, build, dress, tick, ST, tex, geos, dyn, lanternGeo: () => merge(lantern(0.0, 2.6, 0.32)) }; })();
window.V17 = V17;


/* ═══ v18: أزقّة ضيقة وبيوت حجرية متلاصقة (حسب فيديو مصطفى) — شوارع ٤٫٨–٦م (الرئيسي ٨م)، واجهات متصلة بطابقين وثلاثة، أبواب خشب بأقواس،
   شبابيك صغيرة ومشربيات خشبية (عيدان عمودية فقط)، أقواس فوق الأزقة، براميل وجرار وفوانيس أكبر وأكثر، وضوء أذهب.
   كل البيوت شبكات مدمجة بقطاعات (خامة أطلس واحدة مرسومة بالكود) بدل نماذج البيوت المنفصلة — أخف بكثير على الموبايل ═══ */
const V18 = (() => { const D2R = Math.PI / 180, PI = Math.PI, ST = { t0: 0 };
  const rs = seed => { let s = seed; return () => (s = (s * 16807) % 2147483647) / 2147483647; };
  const HQ = () => window.__QUALITY === 'high';
  // ───────── (١) أطلس الواجهات: ٨×٨ مربعات، كل مربع = فتحة ٣م × طابق ٣٫٣م، بحجر مقصوص متطابق الحواف ─────────
  const TU = 256, PADU = 2.5;
  let ATL = null;
  const T = { DOOR1: [0, 0], DOOR2: [1, 0], SHOP: [2, 0], SHOP2: [3, 0], GWIN: [4, 0], GPLAIN: [5, 0], BLIND: [6, 0], GATE: [7, 0],
    WIN2: [0, 1], WIN1: [1, 1], SMALL: [2, 1], UPLAIN: [3, 1], BALC: [4, 1], TRI: [5, 1], WINSH: [6, 1], UPLAIN2: [7, 1],
    PARA: [0, 2], ROOF: [1, 2], MASH: [2, 2], WOOD: [3, 2], SMOOTH: [4, 2], MASH2: [5, 2], DOOR3: [6, 2], SHOP3: [7, 2] };
  function atlas() { if (ATL) return ATL; const t0 = performance.now(), A = HQ() ? 2048 : 1024, k = A / 2048;
    const c = document.createElement('canvas'); c.width = c.height = A; const g = c.getContext('2d');
    const ch = document.createElement('canvas'); ch.width = ch.height = A; const h = ch.getContext('2d');
    g.fillStyle = 'rgb(200,172,128)'; g.fillRect(0, 0, A, A); h.fillStyle = 'rgb(128,128,128)'; h.fillRect(0, 0, A, A);
    const hash = (a, b, cc) => { const x = Math.sin(a * 127.1 + b * 311.7 + cc * 74.7) * 43758.5453; return x - Math.floor(x); };
    let R = rs(18018);
    const hv = v => { v = Math.max(0, Math.min(255, v | 0)); return 'rgb(' + v + ',' + v + ',' + v + ')'; };
    const rgb = (p, m) => 'rgb(' + Math.min(255, p[0] * (m || 1)) + ',' + Math.min(255, p[1] * (m || 1)) + ',' + Math.min(255, p[2] * (m || 1)) + ')';
    const at = (tx, ty) => { for (const cx of [g, h]) { cx.setTransform(k, 0, 0, k, tx * TU * k, ty * TU * k); } };
    const clip = on => { for (const cx of [g, h]) { if (on) { cx.save(); cx.beginPath(); cx.rect(0, 0, TU, TU); cx.clip(); } else cx.restore(); } };
    const rr = (x, y, w, hh, r) => { const p = new Path2D(); r = Math.min(r, w / 2, hh / 2); p.moveTo(x + r, y); p.lineTo(x + w - r, y); p.quadraticCurveTo(x + w, y, x + w, y + r); p.lineTo(x + w, y + hh - r); p.quadraticCurveTo(x + w, y + hh, x + w - r, y + hh); p.lineTo(x + r, y + hh); p.quadraticCurveTo(x, y + hh, x, y + hh - r); p.lineTo(x, y + r); p.quadraticCurveTo(x, y, x + r, y); p.closePath(); return p; };
    const arch = (cx, yb, w, ys) => { const p = new Path2D(); p.moveTo(cx - w / 2, yb); p.lineTo(cx - w / 2, ys); p.arc(cx, ys, w / 2, PI, 2 * PI); p.lineTo(cx + w / 2, yb); p.closePath(); return p; };
    const both = (p, col, ht) => { g.fillStyle = col; g.fill(p); h.fillStyle = hv(ht); h.fill(p); };
    const box = (x, y, w, hh, col, ht) => { g.fillStyle = col; g.fillRect(x, y, w, hh); h.fillStyle = hv(ht); h.fillRect(x, y, w, hh); };
    const line = (x0, y0, x1, y1, col, ht, lw) => { for (const [cx, st] of [[g, col], [h, hv(ht)]]) { cx.strokeStyle = st; cx.lineWidth = lw || 1.5; cx.beginPath(); cx.moveTo(x0, y0); cx.lineTo(x1, y1); cx.stroke(); } };
    const PAL = [[222, 200, 162], [210, 188, 150], [228, 208, 172], [200, 178, 142], [216, 194, 154], [194, 172, 138], [226, 204, 166], [206, 186, 152], [186, 166, 134]];
    const MORT = [198, 180, 148];
    function stone(x, y, w, hh, p, hb) { both(rr(x + 1.5, y + 1.5, w - 3, hh - 3, 2.5), rgb(p), hb); both(rr(x + 3, y + 3, w - 6, hh - 6, 3), rgb(p, 1.015), hb + 22); both(rr(x + 5, y + 4.5, w - 10, hh - 9, 3), rgb(p, 1.03), hb + 34);
      // تآكل خفيف على الحجر
      for (let i = 0; i < 9; i++) { const ex = x + 4 + R() * (w - 10), ey = y + 4 + R() * (hh - 8), er = 1 + R() * 4; g.fillStyle = (R() < 0.7 ? 'rgba(100,82,60,' : 'rgba(250,236,206,') + (0.08 + R() * 0.12) + ')'; g.fillRect(ex, ey, er * 2, er); h.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.12)'; h.fillRect(ex, ey, er * 2, er); } }
    function ashlar(tid, top, bot) { top = top || 0; bot = bot === undefined ? 256 : bot; box(0, top, 256, bot - top, rgb(MORT), 100);
      const CH = 32, BW = 64; for (let kq = 0; kq < 8; kq++) { const y0 = kq * CH; if (y0 + CH <= top || y0 >= bot) continue; const off = kq % 2 ? -BW / 2 : 0;
        for (let b = 0; b < 6; b++) { const x0 = off + b * BW; if (x0 >= 255.9) break; const sd = x0 < -0.1 || x0 + BW > 256.1, hs = sd ? hash(kq, 91, 7) : hash(tid, kq, b), p = PAL[(hs * PAL.length) | 0], m = 0.95 + (sd ? hash(kq, 3, 5) : hash(tid + 7, kq, b)) * 0.08, pc = [p[0] * m, p[1] * m, p[2] * m];
          if (sd) { stone(-BW / 2, y0, BW, CH, pc, 150); stone(256 - BW / 2, y0, BW, CH, pc, 150); } else stone(x0, y0, BW, CH, pc, 150); } }
      for (let i = 0; i < 260; i++) { const x = R() * 256, y = top + R() * (bot - top), w = 1 + R() * 5; g.fillStyle = R() < 0.6 ? 'rgba(110,84,56,0.07)' : 'rgba(255,240,210,0.08)'; g.fillRect(x, y, w, w * 0.6); } }
    function plinth(tid) { box(0, 226, 256, 30, rgb(MORT, 0.9), 70); for (let b = 0; b < 3; b++) { const p = PAL[(hash(tid, 9, b) * PAL.length) | 0]; stone(b * 96 - 16, 226, 96, 30, [p[0] * 0.86, p[1] * 0.84, p[2] * 0.8], 150); } }
    const SURR = [236, 218, 182], DARK = 'rgb(30,22,16)', WD = [116, 74, 42], WD2 = [96, 60, 34], TEAL = [64, 112, 106], BLUE = [58, 92, 118];
    function surround(cx, yb, wIn, ys, t) { both(arch(cx, yb, wIn + 2 * t, ys), rgb(SURR), 236);
      const r0 = wIn / 2, r1 = wIn / 2 + t; for (let a = 0; a <= 12; a++) { const an = PI + a / 12 * PI, cs = Math.cos(an), sn = Math.sin(an); line(cx + cs * r0, ys + sn * r0, cx + cs * r1, ys + sn * r1, 'rgba(120,96,66,0.8)', 150, 1.4); }
      for (const s of [-1, 1]) for (let y = ys + 16; y < yb - 4; y += 26) line(cx + s * r0, y, cx + s * r1, y, 'rgba(120,96,66,0.8)', 150, 1.4);
      const kp = new Path2D(); kp.moveTo(cx - 7, ys - r1 - 3); kp.lineTo(cx + 7, ys - r1 - 3); kp.lineTo(cx + 5, ys - r0 + 1); kp.lineTo(cx - 5, ys - r0 + 1); kp.closePath(); both(kp, rgb(SURR, 1.04), 245); }
    function planks(x0, x1, y0, y1, col, step) { for (let x = x0 + step; x < x1 - 1; x += step) line(x, y0, x, y1, rgb(col, 0.62), 92, 1.3); }
    function studs(x0, x1, y, n) { for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) { const x = x0 + (x1 - x0) * (i + 0.5) / n, p = new Path2D(); p.arc(x, y, 1.8, 0, 2 * PI); both(p, 'rgb(40,34,28)', 170); } }
    function door(cx, yb, w, ys, col, opt) { surround(cx, yb, w, ys, 12); both(arch(cx, yb, w, ys), DARK, 30); const wi = w - 6; both(arch(cx, yb, wi, ys + 2), rgb(col), 118);
      planks(cx - wi / 2, cx + wi / 2, ys - wi / 2 + 4, yb, col, 10); studs(cx - wi / 2 + 4, cx + wi / 2 - 4, ys + 18, 6); studs(cx - wi / 2 + 4, cx + wi / 2 - 4, yb - 34, 6);
      line(cx, ys - wi / 2 + 2, cx, yb, rgb(col, 0.5), 80, 2); const kn = new Path2D(); kn.arc(cx + 9, (ys + yb) / 2 + 8, 4, 0, 2 * PI); for (const [cx2, st] of [[g, 'rgb(46,40,32)'], [h, hv(180)]]) { cx2.strokeStyle = st; cx2.lineWidth = 1.6; cx2.stroke(kn); }
      box(cx - w / 2 - 14, yb - 2, w + 28, 10, rgb(SURR, 0.95), 205); }
    function window_(cx, yb, w, ys, shut, scol) { surround(cx, yb, w, ys, 7); both(arch(cx, yb, w, ys), 'rgb(26,28,32)', 26);
      const gp = new Path2D(); gp.rect(cx - w / 2 + 3, ys, w - 6, yb - ys - 2); g.fillStyle = 'rgba(70,82,96,0.55)'; g.fill(gp);
      line(cx, ys - w / 2 + 3, cx, yb - 2, 'rgb(70,50,32)', 60, 2.2);
      if (shut) for (const s of [-1, 1]) { const x = s < 0 ? cx - w / 2 - 9 - w * 0.48 : cx + w / 2 + 9, ww = w * 0.48; box(x, ys - 4, ww, yb - ys + 4, rgb(scol || WD), 130); planks(x, x + ww, ys - 4, yb, scol || WD, 5); }
      box(cx - w / 2 - 10, yb, w + 20, 8, rgb(SURR, 0.97), 220); }
    function rectWin(cx, y0, w, hh, shutCol) { box(cx - w / 2 - 7, y0 - 9, w + 14, 9, rgb(SURR), 232); box(cx - w / 2 - 5, y0 - 1, w + 10, hh + 2, rgb(SURR, 0.98), 225); box(cx - w / 2 - 8, y0 + hh, w + 16, 7, rgb(SURR), 228);
      if (shutCol) { for (const s of [0, 1]) { const x = cx - w / 2 + s * w / 2; box(x + 1, y0 + 1, w / 2 - 2, hh - 2, rgb(shutCol), 140); planks(x + 1, x + w / 2 - 1, y0 + 1, y0 + hh - 1, shutCol, 5); } }
      else { box(cx - w / 2, y0, w, hh, 'rgb(28,30,34)', 26); g.fillStyle = 'rgba(80,92,104,0.5)'; g.fillRect(cx - w / 2 + 2, y0 + 2, w - 4, hh * 0.45); line(cx, y0, cx, y0 + hh, 'rgb(70,50,32)', 60, 2); } }
    const tile = (nm, fn) => { const [tx, ty] = T[nm]; at(tx, ty); clip(true); R = rs(9000 + tx * 37 + ty * 101); fn(tx * 8 + ty * 64 + 1); clip(false); };
    // الطابق الأرضي
    tile('DOOR1', id => { ashlar(id); plinth(id); door(128, 236, 92, 128, WD); });
    tile('DOOR2', id => { ashlar(id); plinth(id); door(100, 236, 84, 134, WD2); window_(200, 140, 26, 108, false); });
    tile('DOOR3', id => { ashlar(id); plinth(id); door(128, 236, 86, 132, TEAL); rectWin(128, 22, 30, 26, null); });
    tile('SHOP', id => { ashlar(id); plinth(id); surround(128, 236, 190, 128, 14); both(arch(128, 236, 190, 128), 'rgb(26,20,15)', 22);
      for (const y of [150, 178, 206]) box(54, y, 148, 3, 'rgb(70,50,34)', 40);
      const JC = [[150, 84, 46], [176, 120, 58], [110, 70, 40], [186, 150, 70], [120, 130, 60], [160, 60, 40]];
      for (let i = 0; i < 14; i++) { const x = 60 + (i % 7) * 20, y = i < 7 ? 146 : 174, p = new Path2D(); p.ellipse(x + 6, y - 7, 6, 8, 0, 0, 2 * PI); both(p, rgb(JC[i % JC.length], 0.8), 60); }
      for (const s of [-1, 1]) { const x = s < 0 ? 33 : 193, ww = 30; box(x, 120, ww, 116, rgb(WD), 128); planks(x, x + ww, 120, 236, WD, 6); }
      box(40, 210, 176, 26, rgb(WD2), 150); planks(40, 216, 210, 236, WD2, 12); box(36, 206, 184, 5, rgb(WD, 1.1), 165); });
    tile('SHOP2', id => { ashlar(id); plinth(id); box(48, 82, 160, 18, rgb(SURR), 240); both(arch(128, 82, 96, 82), rgb(SURR, 0.96), 210); both(arch(128, 82, 76, 82), 'rgb(60,46,32)', 120);
      for (let a = 1; a < 8; a++) { const an = PI + a / 8 * PI; line(128, 82, 128 + Math.cos(an) * 38, 82 + Math.sin(an) * 38, 'rgb(90,70,48)', 100, 1.5); }
      box(56, 100, 144, 136, rgb(TEAL), 125); planks(56, 200, 100, 236, TEAL, 9); line(128, 100, 128, 236, rgb(TEAL, 0.5), 80, 2.4); studs(62, 194, 116, 9); studs(62, 194, 220, 9); box(46, 234, 164, 6, rgb(SURR), 205); });
    tile('SHOP3', id => { ashlar(id); plinth(id); surround(128, 236, 150, 120, 12); both(arch(128, 236, 150, 120), 'rgb(26,20,15)', 22);
      box(56, 120, 144, 116, rgb(BLUE), 120); planks(56, 200, 120, 236, BLUE, 8); line(128, 46, 128, 236, rgb(BLUE, 0.5), 80, 2.4);
      const fan = new Path2D(); fan.arc(128, 120, 72, PI, 2 * PI); fan.closePath(); both(fan, 'rgb(40,32,26)', 40); for (let a = 1; a < 6; a++) { const an = PI + a / 6 * PI; line(128, 120, 128 + Math.cos(an) * 72, 120 + Math.sin(an) * 72, rgb(WD), 110, 2.4); } });
    tile('GWIN', id => { ashlar(id); plinth(id); rectWin(128, 104, 40, 46, WD); const vp = new Path2D(); vp.rect(40, 190, 22, 12); both(vp, 'rgb(40,34,28)', 30); });
    tile('GPLAIN', id => { ashlar(id); plinth(id); g.fillStyle = 'rgba(90,72,50,0.12)'; g.fillRect(150, 0, 14, 226); });
    tile('BLIND', id => { ashlar(id); plinth(id); both(arch(128, 226, 150, 120), rgb([214, 190, 150]), 175); surround(128, 226, 150, 120, 9); both(arch(128, 226, 132, 124), rgb([206, 180, 140]), 170); rectWin(128, 150, 26, 30, null); });
    tile('GATE', id => { ashlar(id); plinth(id); door(128, 236, 150, 116, WD); const wk = new Path2D(); wk.rect(108, 170, 40, 64); for (const [cx2, st] of [[g, 'rgb(60,38,22)'], [h, hv(90)]]) { cx2.strokeStyle = st; cx2.lineWidth = 2; cx2.stroke(wk); } });
    // الطوابق العليا
    tile('WIN2', id => { ashlar(id); window_(94, 172, 40, 100, false); window_(162, 172, 40, 100, false); box(124, 96, 8, 76, rgb(SURR), 236); });
    tile('WIN1', id => { ashlar(id); window_(128, 184, 54, 98, true, WD); });
    tile('SMALL', id => { ashlar(id); rectWin(86, 104, 36, 44, null); rectWin(170, 104, 36, 44, null); });
    tile('UPLAIN', id => { ashlar(id); });
    tile('UPLAIN2', id => { ashlar(id); const oc = new Path2D(); oc.arc(128, 70, 16, 0, 2 * PI); both(oc, rgb(SURR), 232); const o2 = new Path2D(); o2.arc(128, 70, 10, 0, 2 * PI); both(o2, 'rgb(30,28,30)', 26);
      box(60, 250, 26, 6, rgb(SURR, 0.9), 220); g.fillStyle = 'rgba(80,64,44,0.18)'; g.fillRect(66, 0, 12, 250); });
    tile('BALC', id => { ashlar(id); surround(128, 256, 74, 98, 10); both(arch(128, 256, 74, 98), 'rgb(26,28,32)', 24); g.fillStyle = 'rgba(80,92,104,0.5)'; g.fillRect(95, 98, 66, 150); line(128, 62, 128, 256, rgb(WD2), 60, 3); });
    tile('TRI', id => { ashlar(id); surround(128, 164, 150, 112, 8); both(arch(128, 164, 150, 112), rgb([210, 184, 144]), 150);
      for (const x of [80, 128, 176]) window_(x, 164, 30, 112, false); for (const x of [104, 152]) box(x - 3, 108, 6, 56, rgb(SURR), 236); box(46, 164, 164, 9, rgb(SURR), 226); });
    tile('WINSH', id => { ashlar(id); rectWin(128, 92, 64, 78, TEAL); });
    // شرائح وخامات
    tile('PARA', id => { ashlar(id, 18); box(0, 0, 256, 15, rgb(SURR, 1.02), 245); box(0, 15, 256, 4, 'rgb(120,98,70)', 110); });
    tile('ROOF', id => { box(0, 0, 256, 256, 'rgb(206,190,162)', 128); for (let i = 0; i < 380; i++) { const x = R() * 256, y = R() * 256, w = 3 + R() * 16; g.fillStyle = R() < 0.5 ? 'rgba(150,128,96,0.12)' : 'rgba(240,228,206,0.12)'; g.fillRect(x, y, w, w * (0.4 + R())); h.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.04)'; h.fillRect(x, y, w, w * 0.5); } });
    tile('MASH', id => { box(0, 0, 256, 256, rgb(WD2), 120); box(0, 0, 256, 38, rgb(WD), 160); for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) both(arch(16 + i * 32, 32, 18, 18), 'rgb(28,18,12)', 40);
      box(0, 196, 256, 60, rgb(WD), 150); for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) box(10 + i * 62, 206, 50, 40, rgb(WD, 1.12), 180);
      for (let x = 0; x < 256; x += 8) { box(x, 38, 4, 158, 'rgb(28,18,12)', 30); box(x + 4, 38, 4, 158, rgb(WD, 1.05), 175); }
      box(0, 36, 256, 4, rgb(WD, 0.8), 140); box(0, 194, 256, 4, rgb(WD, 0.8), 140); });
    tile('MASH2', id => { box(0, 0, 256, 256, rgb(WD), 165); box(0, 0, 256, 30, rgb(WD2), 150); box(0, 206, 256, 50, rgb(WD2), 150);
      for (let j = 0; j < 11; j++) for (let i = 0; i < 17; i++) { const x = 8 + i * 15 + (j % 2) * 7.5, y = 42 + j * 15, p = new Path2D(); p.arc(x, y, 5, 0, 2 * PI); both(p, 'rgb(24,16,10)', 30); } });
    tile('WOOD', id => { box(0, 0, 256, 256, rgb(WD), 140); for (let y = 0; y < 256; y += 3) { g.fillStyle = 'rgba(60,36,20,' + (0.05 + R() * 0.12) + ')'; g.fillRect(0, y, 256, 1); } });
    tile('SMOOTH', id => { box(0, 0, 256, 256, rgb(SURR, 0.97), 200); for (let i = 0; i < 300; i++) { const x = R() * 256, y = R() * 256, w = 2 + R() * 8; g.fillStyle = R() < 0.5 ? 'rgba(150,124,90,0.1)' : 'rgba(255,246,226,0.1)'; g.fillRect(x, y, w, w * 0.7); } });
    for (const cx of [g, h]) cx.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0);
    // خريطة النتوء من الارتفاعات
    const hd = h.getImageData(0, 0, A, A).data, cn = document.createElement('canvas'); cn.width = cn.height = A; const gn = cn.getContext('2d'), im = gn.createImageData(A, A), dn = im.data, K = 2.0 * (A / 2048) * 2;
    for (let y = 0; y < A; y++) { const yu = y > 0 ? y - 1 : y, yd = y < A - 1 ? y + 1 : y; for (let x = 0; x < A; x++) { const xl = x > 0 ? x - 1 : x, xr = x < A - 1 ? x + 1 : x;
      let nx = (hd[(y * A + xl) * 4] - hd[(y * A + xr) * 4]) / 255 * K, ny = (hd[(yd * A + x) * 4] - hd[(yu * A + x) * 4]) / 255 * K; const l = Math.hypot(nx, ny, 1), o = (y * A + x) * 4; dn[o] = (nx / l * 0.5 + 0.5) * 255; dn[o + 1] = (ny / l * 0.5 + 0.5) * 255; dn[o + 2] = (1 / l * 0.5 + 0.5) * 255; dn[o + 3] = 255; } }
    gn.putImageData(im, 0, 0);
    const tc = new THREE.CanvasTexture(c), tn = new THREE.CanvasTexture(cn); tc.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace; for (const t of [tc, tn]) { t.anisotropy = HQ() ? 8 : 4; t.generateMipmaps = true; t.minFilter = THREE.LinearMipmapLinearFilter; }
    const mat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: tc, normalMap: tn, normalScale: new THREE.Vector2(1.1, 1.1), vertexColors: true, roughness: 0.9, metalness: 0 });
    ATL = { A, tc, tn, mat, canvas: c, ms: Math.round(performance.now() - t0) }; ST.atlas = ATL.ms; return ATL; }
  // إحداثيات UV لمربع (مقطع عمودي f0..f1 من الأعلى، أفقي uL..uR)
  const tuv = (nm, f0, f1, uL, uR) => { const [tx, ty] = T[nm], S = 2048, ux = q => (tx * TU + Math.max(PADU, Math.min(TU - PADU, q))) / S, vy = q => 1 - (ty * TU + Math.max(PADU, Math.min(TU - PADU, q))) / S;
    f0 = f0 === undefined ? 0 : f0; f1 = f1 === undefined ? TU : f1; uL = uL === undefined ? 0 : uL; uR = uR === undefined ? TU : uR;
    return [[ux(uL), vy(f1)], [ux(uR), vy(f1)], [ux(uR), vy(f0)], [ux(uL), vy(f0)]]; };

  // ───────── (٢) أدوات الهندسة: مثلثات بلون رؤوس وUV ─────────
  const GB = () => ({ P: [], N: [], U: [], C: [] });
  function quad(B, p, uv, col, n) { const [a, b, c, d] = p, ex = b[0] - a[0], ey = b[1] - a[1], ez = b[2] - a[2], fx = c[0] - a[0], fy = c[1] - a[1], fz = c[2] - a[2], cx = ey * fz - ez * fy, cy = ez * fx - ex * fz, cz = ex * fy - ey * fx;
    const flip = cx * n[0] + cy * n[1] + cz * n[2] < 0, ord = flip ? [0, 2, 1, 0, 3, 2] : [0, 1, 2, 0, 2, 3];
    for (const i of ord) { B.P.push(p[i][0], p[i][1], p[i][2]); B.N.push(n[0], n[1], n[2]); B.U.push(uv[i][0], uv[i][1]); const q = col[i]; B.C.push(q[0], q[1], q[2]); } }
  const AO = (y, yg) => { const t = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (y - yg) / 4.2)); return 0.66 + 0.34 * t * t * (3 - 2 * t); };
  const mulc = (t, a) => [t[0] * a, t[1] * a, t[2] * a];
  // جدار: A,B نقطتان أرضيتان، n العمودي الخارجي، y0..y1، مربع الأطلس
  function wall(B, A, Bp, n, y0, y1, uv, tint, yg) { let a = A, b = Bp; if ((b[0] - a[0]) * n[2] - (b[1] - a[1]) * n[0] < 0) { a = Bp; b = A; }
    const nn = [n[0], 0, n[1]], c0 = mulc(tint, AO(y0, yg)), c1 = mulc(tint, AO(y1, yg));
    quad(B, [[a[0], y0, a[1]], [b[0], y0, b[1]], [b[0], y1, b[1]], [a[0], y1, a[1]]], uv, [c0, c0, c1, c1], nn); }
  // صندوق موجّه: c مركز القاعدة، u محور العرض، n محور العمق (للخارج)
  function obox(B, c, y0, u, n, w, hh, d, nm, tint, yg, faces) { const hw = w / 2, P = (su, sn, y) => [c[0] + u[0] * su * hw + n[0] * sn * d, y, c[1] + u[1] * su * hw + n[1] * sn * d];
    const t = tuv(nm), y1 = y0 + hh, cc = mulc(tint, AO(y0 + hh * 0.5, yg));
    const F = { f: [[P(-1, 1, y0), P(1, 1, y0), P(1, 1, y1), P(-1, 1, y1)], [n[0], 0, n[1]]], t: [[P(-1, 0, y1), P(1, 0, y1), P(1, 1, y1), P(-1, 1, y1)], [0, 1, 0]], b: [[P(-1, 0, y0), P(1, 0, y0), P(1, 1, y0), P(-1, 1, y0)], [0, -1, 0]],
      l: [[P(-1, 0, y0), P(-1, 1, y0), P(-1, 1, y1), P(-1, 0, y1)], [-u[0], 0, -u[1]]], r: [[P(1, 0, y0), P(1, 1, y0), P(1, 1, y1), P(1, 0, y1)], [u[0], 0, u[1]]] };
    for (const k of faces || 'ftblr') { const [p, nn] = F[k]; quad(B, p, t, [cc, cc, cc, cc], nn); } }
  // مشربية خشبية بارزة (صندوق بعيدان عمودية) أو شرفة حجرية
  function mashrabiya(B, c, y0, u, n, w, tint, yg, kind) { const hh = 2.35, d = 0.78, hw = w / 2, P = (su, sn, y) => [c[0] + u[0] * su * hw + n[0] * sn * d, y, c[1] + u[1] * su * hw + n[1] * sn * d], y1 = y0 + hh, nm = kind ? 'MASH2' : 'MASH', cc = mulc([1, 1, 1], 0.92), cs = mulc([1, 1, 1], 0.8);
    quad(B, [P(-1, 1, y0), P(1, 1, y0), P(1, 1, y1), P(-1, 1, y1)], tuv(nm), [cc, cc, cc, cc], [n[0], 0, n[1]]);
    for (const s of [-1, 1]) quad(B, [P(s, 0, y0), P(s, 1, y0), P(s, 1, y1), P(s, 0, y1)], tuv(nm, 0, 256, 0, 80), [cs, cs, cs, cs], [u[0] * s, 0, u[1] * s]);
    const tw = tuv('WOOD'), cw = [0.7, 0.7, 0.7]; quad(B, [P(-1, 0, y0), P(1, 0, y0), P(1, 1, y0), P(-1, 1, y0)], tw, [cw, cw, cw, cw], [0, -1, 0]);
    // سقيفة صغيرة فوقها
    const r0 = (su, sn, y) => [c[0] + u[0] * su * (hw + 0.08) + n[0] * sn * (d + 0.14), y, c[1] + u[1] * su * (hw + 0.08) + n[1] * sn * (d + 0.14)];
    const ct = [0.82, 0.8, 0.78]; quad(B, [r0(-1, 0, y1 + 0.32), r0(1, 0, y1 + 0.32), r0(1, 1, y1 + 0.02), r0(-1, 1, y1 + 0.02)], tw, [ct, ct, ct, ct], [n[0] * 0.5, 0.86, n[1] * 0.5]);
    for (const s of [-1, 1]) quad(B, [r0(s, 0, y1), r0(s, 1, y1), r0(s, 1, y1 + 0.02), r0(s, 0, y1 + 0.32)], tw, [cw, cw, cw, cw], [u[0] * s, 0, u[1] * s]);
    for (const s of [-0.7, 0.7]) obox(B, [c[0] + u[0] * s * hw, c[1] + u[1] * s * hw], y0 - 0.42, u, n, 0.16, 0.42, d * 0.9, 'SMOOTH', tint, yg, 'ftlr'); }
  function balcony(B, c, y0, u, n, w, tint, yg) { const d = 0.85; obox(B, c, y0 - 0.16, u, n, w + 0.1, 0.16, d, 'SMOOTH', tint, yg, 'ftblr');
    const hw = w / 2; obox(B, [c[0] + n[0] * (d - 0.12), c[1] + n[1] * (d - 0.12)], y0, u, n, w + 0.1, 0.9, 0.12, 'SMOOTH', mulc(tint, 0.96), yg, 'ftlr');
    for (const s of [-1, 1]) obox(B, [c[0] + u[0] * s * (hw - 0.03) + n[0] * (d - 0.12) / 2, c[1] + u[1] * s * (hw - 0.03) + n[1] * (d - 0.12) / 2], y0, [n[0], n[1]], [u[0] * s, u[1] * s], d - 0.12, 0.9, 0.06, 'SMOOTH', mulc(tint, 0.94), yg, 'ft');
    for (const s of [-0.75, 0, 0.75]) obox(B, [c[0] + u[0] * s * hw, c[1] + u[1] * s * hw], y0 - 0.5, u, n, 0.14, 0.34, d * 0.85, 'SMOOTH', tint, yg, 'ftlr'); }

  // ───────── (٣) شبكة الأزقة والكتل ─────────
  const RINGS = [[72, 7, 1.9, 1], [96, 4.4, 0.6, 0], [118, 4.4, 0.6, 0], [143, 4.4, 0.6, 1]];   // نصف القطر، عرض الحجر، الرصيف حتى الجدار، بلا قطع
  const THRU = [[0, 62, 160, 5.0], [180, 62, 160, 5.0], [270, 62, 160, 7.0], [90, 143, 160, 5.0], [225, 72, 143, 4.4], [315, 72, 143, 4.4], [45, 118, 143, 4.4], [135, 118, 143, 4.4]];
  const ALLEYW = 3.6;
  const TINTS = [[1, 1, 1], [1.03, 0.98, 0.9], [0.97, 0.94, 0.9], [1.05, 0.97, 0.86], [0.96, 0.9, 0.82], [1.0, 0.95, 0.88], [1.02, 0.96, 0.93], [0.93, 0.9, 0.86], [1.04, 0.95, 0.84]];
  function alleyAngles(b) { const TT = b === 2 ? [0, 45, 90, 135, 180, 225, 270, 315] : [0, 90, 180, 225, 270, 315], out = [];
    const cand = b === 0 ? [...Array(16)].map((_, i) => i * 22.5) : b === 1 ? [...Array(16)].map((_, i) => 11.25 + i * 22.5) : [...Array(24)].map((_, i) => 7.5 + i * 15);
    for (const a of cand) if (TT.every(t => Math.abs(((a - t + 540) % 360) - 180) > (b === 2 ? 5 : 8))) out.push(a); return out; }
  function build(L, ctx) { const t0 = performance.now(), { cx, cz, H, pcx, pcz, HR, HL, RES } = ctx, gates = (L.gates || []).map(q => [q.x, q.z]), P = (r, a) => [cx + r * Math.cos(a * D2R), cz - r * Math.sin(a * D2R)];
    const rnd = rs(181818), RESb = (RES || []).filter(q => !q.r), foot = HL && HL.t ? HL.t.foot : 67, solids0 = L.solid.slice();
    const solidHit = (x, z, m) => { for (const o of solids0) { if (Math.abs(x - o[0]) > o[2] + m || Math.abs(z - o[1]) > o[2] + m) continue; if (sdist(o, x, z) < m) return true; } return false; };
    const hillHit = (x, z, m) => Math.hypot(x - pcx, z - pcz) < HR + m || (Math.abs(x - pcx) < 9 && z > pcz && z < pcz + foot + 14);
    const resHit = (x, z, m) => RESb.some(q => Math.abs(x - q.x) < q.hx + m && Math.abs(z - q.z) < q.hz + m);
    const gateHit = (x, z, m) => gates.some(g => Math.hypot(x - g[0], z - g[1]) < m);
    // الشوارع: الحلقات (تُقطع عند التل والمعالم) + الشعاعية + أزقة متداخلة بين الحلقات
    const strips = [], rads = [];
    for (const [rad, w, gu, keep] of RINGS) { const n = Math.max(48, Math.round(2 * PI * rad / 5)); let run = []; const flush = () => { if (run.length > 1) strips.push({ pts: run, w, ring: rad }); run = []; };
      for (let i = 0; i <= n; i++) { const q = P(rad, i / n * 360), bad = hillHit(q[0], q[1], 1) || (!keep && (solidHit(q[0], q[1], w / 2 + 0.3) || resHit(q[0], q[1], 0))); if (bad) flush(); else run.push(q); } flush(); }
    const line = (deg, r0, r1) => { const pts = [], n = Math.max(2, Math.ceil((r1 - r0) / 6)); for (let i = 0; i <= n; i++) pts.push(P(r0 + (r1 - r0) * i / n, deg)); return pts; };
    for (const [a, r0, r1, w] of THRU) { strips.push({ pts: line(a, r0, r1), w, rad: a }); rads.push([a, r0, r1, w]); }
    let nAl = 0;
    for (let b = 0; b < 3; b++) { const ri = RINGS[b][0], ro = RINGS[b + 1][0];
      for (const a of alleyAngles(b)) { let ok = true; const lx = Math.sin(a * D2R), lz = Math.cos(a * D2R);
        for (let rr = ri; rr <= ro && ok; rr += 1.5) { const q = P(rr, a); for (const s of [-1, 0, 1]) { const x = q[0] + lx * s * (ALLEYW / 2 + 0.6), z = q[1] + lz * s * (ALLEYW / 2 + 0.6); if (hillHit(x, z, 4) || solidHit(x, z, 0.5) || resHit(x, z, 0.5) || gateHit(x, z, 18)) { ok = false; break; } } }
        if (ok) { rads.push([a, ri, ro, ALLEYW]); strips.push({ pts: line(a, ri, ro), w: ALLEYW, rad: a, alley: 1 }); nAl++; } } }
    // ── البيوت المتلاصقة: صفّان ظهراً لظهر في كل كتلة، كل بيت ٤٫٤–٧٫٢م عرضاً ──
    const units = [], DR = {};
    const inset = (FL, FR, BR, BL, u, v) => { const fx = FL[0] + (FR[0] - FL[0]) * u, fz = FL[1] + (FR[1] - FL[1]) * u, bx = BL[0] + (BR[0] - BL[0]) * u, bz = BL[1] + (BR[1] - BL[1]) * u; return [fx + (bx - fx) * v, fz + (bz - fz) * v]; };
    function mkUnit(b, FL, FR, BR, BL, sgn, laneW) { const mx = (FL[0] + FR[0]) / 2, mz = (FL[1] + FR[1]) / 2, rx = mx - cx, rz = mz - cz, rl = Math.hypot(rx, rz), nF = [rx / rl * sgn, rz / rl * sgn];
      let bad = false; for (let i = 0; i <= 4 && !bad; i++) for (let j = 0; j <= 4 && !bad; j++) { const [x, z] = inset(FL, FR, BR, BL, 0.04 + 0.92 * i / 4, 0.04 + 0.92 * j / 4); bad = hillHit(x, z, 6) ? 'hill' : resHit(x, z, 1) ? 'res' : gateHit(x, z, 20) ? 'gate' : solidHit(x, z, 0.25) ? 'solid' : false; } if (bad) DR[bad] = (DR[bad] || 0) + 1;
      const r1 = rnd(), plazaF = b === 0 && sgn < 0, ns = plazaF ? (r1 < 0.7 ? 3 : 2) : r1 < 0.5 ? 2 : r1 < 0.93 ? 3 : 4;
      const bm = [(BL[0] + BR[0]) / 2, (BL[1] + BR[1]) / 2], dep = Math.abs((mx - bm[0]) * nF[0] + (mz - bm[1]) * nF[1]);
      return { b, FL, FR, BR, BL, nF, sgn, mid: [mx, mz], bm, dep, W: Math.hypot(FR[0] - FL[0], FR[1] - FL[1]), ns, bad, tint: TINTS[(rnd() * TINTS.length) | 0], r: rnd(), laneW, plazaF }; }
    function row(b, a1, h1, a2, h2, rf, rk, sgn, laneW) { const da = (h, r) => Math.asin(Math.min(0.95, h / r)) / D2R, ang = (r, t) => (a1 + da(h1, r)) + ((a2 - da(h2, r)) - (a1 + da(h1, r))) * t;
      const arc = ((a2 - da(h2, rf)) - (a1 + da(h1, rf))) * D2R * rf; if (arc < 3.4) return;
      const ws = []; let tot = 0; while (tot < arc - 0.01) { const w = 4.4 + rnd() * 2.8; ws.push(w); tot += w; } if (ws.length > 1 && tot - arc > ws[ws.length - 1] * 0.5) tot -= ws.pop();
      const sc = arc / tot; let t = 0; const rowU = [];
      for (const w of ws) { const t1 = Math.min(1, t + w * sc / arc); rowU.push(mkUnit(b, P(rf, ang(rf, t)), P(rf, ang(rf, t1)), P(rk, ang(rk, t1)), P(rk, ang(rk, t)), sgn, laneW)); t = t1; }
      for (let i = 0; i < rowU.length; i++) { rowU[i].prev = rowU[i - 1] || null; rowU[i].next = rowU[i + 1] || null; rowU[i].a1 = a1; rowU[i].a2 = a2; rowU[i].w1 = h1; rowU[i].w2 = h2; } units.push(...rowU); }
    const radW = a => { const q = rads.find(r => Math.abs(((r[0] - a + 540) % 360) - 180) < 0.01); return q ? q[3] : ALLEYW; };
    for (let b = 0; b < 3; b++) { const [Ri, wi, gi] = RINGS[b], [Ro, wo, go] = RINGS[b + 1], fi = Ri + wi / 2 + gi, fo = Ro - wo / 2 - go;
      const rl = rads.filter(q => q[1] <= Ri + 1 && q[2] >= Ro - 1).map(q => [q[0], q[3] / 2 + 0.6]).sort((p, q) => p[0] - q[0]);
      for (let k = 0; k < rl.length; k++) { const [a1, h1] = rl[k], [a2r, h2] = rl[(k + 1) % rl.length], a2 = k + 1 < rl.length ? a2r : a2r + 360, rb = fi + (fo - fi) * (0.44 + rnd() * 0.12);
        row(b, a1, h1, a2, h2, fi, rb, -1, b === 0 ? wi + 2 * gi : wi + 2 * gi); row(b, a1, h1, a2, h2, fo, rb, 1, wo + 2 * go); } }
    const live = units.filter(u => !u.bad);
    for (const u of live) { u.yg = H(u.mid[0], u.mid[1]); u.y0 = u.yg - 0.12; u.yT = u.y0 + 3.3 * u.ns; u.g0 = Math.min(H(u.FL[0], u.FL[1]), H(u.FR[0], u.FR[1]), H(u.BR[0], u.BR[1]), H(u.BL[0], u.BL[1])) - 0.4; }
    // ── اختيار الواجهات: باب بقوس، دكاكين، شبابيك صغيرة، مشربيات ──
    const UPP = ['WIN2', 'WIN1', 'SMALL', 'WINSH', 'TRI', 'UPLAIN2', 'WIN2', 'SMALL', 'UPLAIN'], TOPP = ['SMALL', 'UPLAIN2', 'WINSH', 'UPLAIN', 'WIN1', 'SMALL'];
    const pick = a => a[(rnd() * a.length) | 0];
    const sideLane = (u, left) => { const a = left ? u.a1 : u.a2; return radW(((a % 360) + 360) % 360); };
    const CH = {}, chunkOf = (x, z, b) => { const a = ((Math.atan2(-(z - cz), x - cx) / D2R) + 360) % 360, k = Math.floor(a / 45); return CH[k] || (CH[k] = GB()); };   // ٨ قطاعات فقط: ٣٣ ألف مثلث كلها أرخص من نداءات رسم كثيرة
    const bays = [], homesC = [];

    // ═══ v20: فتحات حقيقية محفورة بالجدار (أبواب وشبابيك بعمق ١٥–٤٥سم، أقواس حجرية بارزة، عتبات، أفاريز، درف خشب) — للمستوى القريب فقط؛ البعيد يبقى مرسوماً ═══
    const V20O = (() => { const AR = (cx, yb, w, ys, seg) => { const p = [[cx - w / 2, yb], [cx + w / 2, yb]]; if (ys < yb - 0.5) p.push([cx + w / 2, ys]); for (let i = 1; i < seg; i++) { const an = i / seg * PI; p.push([cx + Math.cos(an) * w / 2, ys - Math.sin(an) * w / 2]); } if (ys < yb - 0.5) p.push([cx - w / 2, ys]); return p; };
      const RC = (x0, y0, x1, y1) => [[x0, y1], [x1, y1], [x1, y0], [x0, y0]], CI = (cx, cy, r, n) => [...Array(n)].map((_, i) => [cx + Math.cos(i / n * 2 * PI) * r, cy - Math.sin(i / n * 2 * PI) * r]);
      const A = (cx, yb, w, ys, seg, o) => Object.assign({ p: AR(cx, yb, w, ys, seg), a: [cx, yb, w, ys, seg] }, o), R = (x0, y0, x1, y1, o) => Object.assign({ p: RC(x0, y0, x1, y1), r: [x0, y0, x1, y1] }, o);
      const S = { DOOR1: [A(128, 236, 92, 128, 10, { d: 0.3, k: 'door', ring: 12 })],
        DOOR2: [A(100, 236, 84, 134, 10, { d: 0.3, k: 'door', ring: 12 }), A(200, 140, 26, 108, 6, { d: 0.2, k: 'win', ring: 7, sill: 1 })],
        DOOR3: [A(128, 236, 86, 132, 10, { d: 0.3, k: 'door', ring: 12 }), R(113, 22, 143, 48, { d: 0.18, k: 'win', lint: 1, sill: 1 })],
        GATE: [A(128, 236, 150, 116, 12, { d: 0.4, k: 'door', ring: 12 })],
        SHOP: [A(128, 236, 190, 128, 12, { d: 0.5, k: 'shop', ring: 14 })],
        SHOP2: [R(56, 100, 200, 236, { d: 0.3, k: 'door', lint: 2 }), A(128, 82, 76, 82, 8, { d: 0.14, k: 'win' })],
        SHOP3: [A(128, 236, 150, 120, 12, { d: 0.42, k: 'shop', ring: 12 })],
        GWIN: [R(108, 104, 148, 150, { d: 0.24, k: 'win', lint: 1, sill: 1 })],
        BLIND: [A(128, 226, 150, 120, 12, { d: 0.13, k: 'niche', ring: 9 })],
        WIN2: [A(94, 172, 40, 100, 8, { d: 0.26, k: 'win', ring: 7, sill: 1 }), A(162, 172, 40, 100, 8, { d: 0.26, k: 'win', ring: 7, sill: 1 })],
        WIN1: [A(128, 184, 54, 98, 8, { d: 0.28, k: 'win', ring: 7, sill: 1, shut: 1 })],
        SMALL: [R(68, 104, 104, 148, { d: 0.24, k: 'win', lint: 1, sill: 1 }), R(152, 104, 188, 148, { d: 0.24, k: 'win', lint: 1, sill: 1 })],
        WINSH: [R(96, 92, 160, 170, { d: 0.24, k: 'win', lint: 1, sill: 1 })],
        TRI: [A(80, 164, 30, 112, 6, { d: 0.22, k: 'win' }), A(128, 164, 30, 112, 6, { d: 0.22, k: 'win' }), A(176, 164, 30, 112, 6, { d: 0.22, k: 'win' })],
        BALC: [A(128, 250, 74, 98, 8, { d: 0.28, k: 'door', ring: 10 })],
        UPLAIN2: [{ p: CI(128, 70, 10, 10), d: 0.16, k: 'win', ring: 6, c: [128, 70, 10] }] };
      const V2 = q => new THREE.Vector2(q[0], q[1]), ST2 = { n: 0, tris: 0 };
      const hsh = (x, z) => { const s = Math.sin(x * 12.9898 + z * 78.233) * 43758.5453; return s - Math.floor(s); };
      function tri(X, p, uv, col, n) { const ex = p[1][0] - p[0][0], ey = p[1][1] - p[0][1], ez = p[1][2] - p[0][2], fx = p[2][0] - p[0][0], fy = p[2][1] - p[0][1], fz = p[2][2] - p[0][2], cx = ey * fz - ez * fy, cy = ez * fx - ex * fz, cz = ex * fy - ey * fx;
        const ord = cx * n[0] + cy * n[1] + cz * n[2] < 0 ? [0, 2, 1] : [0, 1, 2]; for (const i of ord) { X.P.push(p[i][0], p[i][1], p[i][2]); X.N.push(n[0], n[1], n[2]); X.U.push(uv[i][0], uv[i][1]); const q = col[i]; X.C.push(q[0], q[1], q[2]); } }
      function carve(X, p0, p1, n, ys, nm, tint, yg) { const sp = S[nm]; if (!sp) return false; const [tx, ty] = T[nm], U0 = (tx * TU + PADU) / 2048, U1 = (tx * TU + TU - PADU) / 2048, V0 = 1 - (ty * TU + PADU) / 2048, V1 = 1 - (ty * TU + TU - PADU) / 2048;
        const ex = p1[0] - p0[0], ez = p1[1] - p0[1], W = Math.hypot(ex, ez), ux = ex / W, uz = ez / W, nx = n[0], nz = n[1], N3 = [nx, 0, nz], kx = W / 256, ky = 3.3 / 256;
        const P = (s, t, d) => [p0[0] + ex * s / 256 - nx * d, ys + 3.3 * (1 - t / 256), p0[1] + ez * s / 256 - nz * d], UV = (s, t) => [U0 + (U1 - U0) * s / 256, V0 + (V1 - V0) * t / 256];
        const col = (y, k) => mulc(tint, AO(y, yg) * k), [su0, sv0] = tuv('SMOOTH')[3], [su1, sv1] = tuv('SMOOTH')[1];
        const flat = (poly, holes, d, k) => { const c = poly.map(V2), hs = holes.map(h => h.map(V2)), all = c.concat(...hs), tr = THREE.ShapeUtils.triangulateShape(c, hs);
          for (const f of tr) { const q = f.map(i => all[i]), pp = q.map(v => P(v.x, v.y, d)); tri(X, pp, q.map(v => UV(v.x, v.y)), pp.map(v => col(v[1], k)), N3); } };
        const band = (poly, d0, d1, k, out, skipFlat) => { let cxp = 0, cyp = 0; for (const q of poly) { cxp += q[0]; cyp += q[1]; } cxp /= poly.length; cyp /= poly.length; const c3 = P(cxp, cyp, 0);
          for (let i = 0; i < poly.length; i++) { const a = poly[i], b = poly[(i + 1) % poly.length]; if (skipFlat && Math.abs(a[1] - b[1]) < 0.01 && a[1] > cyp) continue;
            const A0 = P(a[0], a[1], d0), B0 = P(b[0], b[1], d0), B1 = P(b[0], b[1], d1), A1 = P(a[0], a[1], d1), e = [B0[0] - A0[0], B0[1] - A0[1], B0[2] - A0[2]];
            let m = [e[1] * nz, e[2] * nx - e[0] * nz, -e[1] * nx]; const l = Math.hypot(m[0], m[1], m[2]) || 1; m = [m[0] / l, m[1] / l, m[2] / l];
            const mid = [(A0[0] + B0[0]) / 2, (A0[1] + B0[1]) / 2, (A0[2] + B0[2]) / 2], toC = (c3[0] - mid[0]) * m[0] + (c3[1] - mid[1]) * m[1] + (c3[2] - mid[2]) * m[2]; if ((toC < 0) !== !!out) m = [-m[0], -m[1], -m[2]];
            const el = Math.hypot(e[0], e[1], e[2]), du = Math.min(0.9, el * 0.35 + 0.05) * (su1 - su0), dv = Math.min(0.9, Math.abs(d1 - d0) * 0.8 + 0.05) * (sv1 - sv0), uv = [[su0, sv0], [su0 + du, sv0], [su0 + du, sv0 + dv], [su0, sv0 + dv]];
            const kk = k * (m[1] > 0.5 ? 1.05 : m[1] < -0.5 ? 0.62 : 0.8); quad(X, [A0, B0, B1, A1], uv, [col(A0[1], kk), col(B0[1], kk), col(B1[1], kk), col(A1[1], kk)], m); } };
        // ١) الجدار نفسه بثقوب الفتحات (نفس إحداثيات الأطلس فالإطار المرسوم يبقى حول الفتحة تماماً)
        flat([[0, 0], [256, 0], [256, 256], [0, 256]], sp.map(o => o.p), 0, 1);
        const bx = (s, t, w, hh, d, out, faces) => { const c = [p0[0] + ex * s / 256 + nx * out, p0[1] + ez * s / 256 + nz * out]; obox(X, c, ys + 3.3 * (1 - t / 256), [ux, uz], [nx, nz], w, hh, d, 'SMOOTH', tint, yg, faces || 'ftblr'); };
        for (const o of sp) { const f0 = o.ring ? -0.05 : 0;
          // ٢) الإطار الحجري البارز (قوس/دائرة) — وجهه بنفس الرسم
          if (o.ring && o.a) { const [cx, yb, w, ys2, seg] = o.a, outer = AR(cx, yb, w + 2 * o.ring, ys2, seg); flat(outer, [o.p], f0, 1.04); band(outer, 0, f0, 1, true, true); }
          else if (o.ring && o.c) { const [cx, cy, r] = o.c, outer = CI(cx, cy, r + o.ring, o.p.length); flat(outer, [o.p], f0, 1.04); band(outer, 0, f0, 1, true, false); }
          // ٣) جوانب العمق (الكتف والقوس من الداخل) ثم ٤) الباب/الشباك نفسه راجع لورا
          band(o.p, f0, o.d, 0.86, false, false); flat(o.p, [], o.d, o.k === 'niche' ? 0.9 : 0.8);
          let s0 = 1e9, s1 = -1e9, t0 = 1e9, t1 = -1e9; for (const q of o.p) { s0 = Math.min(s0, q[0]); s1 = Math.max(s1, q[0]); t0 = Math.min(t0, q[1]); t1 = Math.max(t1, q[1]); } const sc = (s0 + s1) / 2, wpx = s1 - s0;
          // ٥) عتبة/حافة شباك بارزة، ساكف فوق الشبابيك المربعة، درجة حجرية تحت الأبواب
          if (o.sill) bx(sc, t1 + 0.085 / ky, (wpx + 18) * kx, 0.085, 0.14, 0);
          if (o.lint && o.r) bx(sc, t0 - (o.lint === 2 ? 0 : 1), (wpx + (o.lint === 2 ? 30 : 16)) * kx, o.lint === 2 ? 0.2 : 0.11, o.lint === 2 ? 0.1 : 0.07, 0);
          if (o.k === 'door' && t1 > 200 && nm !== 'BALC') bx(sc, 256, (wpx + 16) * kx, Math.max(0.05, (256 - t1) * ky), 0.2, 0, 'ftlr');
          // ٦) درفتا خشب مفتوحتان (بنفس رسمهما على الحائط تحتهما)
          if (o.shut && o.a) { const [cx, yb, w, ys2] = o.a, ww = w * 0.48, th = 0.17 + hsh(p0[0], p0[1]) * 0.2;
            for (const sg of [-1, 1]) { const sh = cx + sg * (w / 2 + 9), so = sh + sg * ww, ta = ys2 - 4, tb = yb, H0 = P(sh, tb, -0.02), H1 = P(sh, ta, -0.02), L = ww * kx, dx = ux * sg * L * Math.cos(th) + nx * L * Math.sin(th), dz = uz * sg * L * Math.cos(th) + nz * L * Math.sin(th);
              const O0 = [H0[0] + dx, H0[1], H0[2] + dz], O1 = [H1[0] + dx, H1[1], H1[2] + dz], uvF = [UV(sh, tb), UV(so, tb), UV(so, ta), UV(sh, ta)], cc = col(H0[1] + 1, 0.95);
              let m = [ (dz) , 0, -(dx)]; const l = Math.hypot(m[0], m[2]) || 1; m = [m[0] / l, 0, m[2] / l]; if (m[0] * nx + m[2] * nz < 0) m = [-m[0], 0, -m[2]];
              quad(X, [H0, O0, O1, H1], uvF, [cc, cc, cc, cc], m); const cb = mulc(cc, 0.7), tw = tuv('WOOD'); quad(X, [H0, O0, O1, H1], tw, [cb, cb, cb, cb], [-m[0], 0, -m[2]]); } }
          ST2.n++; }
        return true; }
      return { S, carve, ST: ST2 }; })();
    const V20B = (ST.v20 = { bays: [], carve: V20O.carve, S: V20O.S, cx: ctx.cx, cz: ctx.cz, L, ST: V20O.ST }).bays;
    function V20W(B, p0, p1, n, ys, nm, tint, yg) { const s0 = B.P.length; wall(B, p0, p1, n, ys, ys + 3.3, tuv(nm), tint, yg); if (!V20O.S[nm]) return;
      const x = (p0[0] + p1[0]) / 2, z = (p0[1] + p1[1]) / 2; (B.R || (B.R = [])).push(s0, B.P.length, x, z); V20B.push({ x, z, a: [p0, p1, n, ys, nm, tint, yg], g: null }); }

    function V20F(Bq, m, mat) { const np = Bq.P.length / 3, A = new Float32Array(np * 3), R = Bq.R; for (let i = 0; i < R.length; i += 4) for (let v = R[i] / 3; v < R[i + 1] / 3; v++) { A[v * 3] = R[i + 2]; A[v * 3 + 1] = R[i + 3]; A[v * 3 + 2] = 1; }
      m.geometry.setAttribute('aBay', new THREE.BufferAttribute(A, 3)); const U = ST.v20U || (ST.v20U = { uP: { value: new THREE.Vector2(1e9, 1e9) }, uR: { value: 0 } });
      const hook = sh => { sh.uniforms.uP = U.uP; sh.uniforms.uR = U.uR; sh.vertexShader = sh.vertexShader.replace('#include <common>', '#include <common>\nattribute vec3 aBay; uniform vec2 uP; uniform float uR;').replace('#include <begin_vertex>', '#include <begin_vertex>\nif (aBay.z > 0.5 && distance(aBay.xy, uP) < uR) transformed = vec3(0.0);'); };
      if (!ST.v20M) { const fm = mat.clone(); fm.onBeforeCompile = hook; fm.customProgramCacheKey = () => 'v20far'; const dm = new THREE.MeshDepthMaterial({ depthPacking: THREE.RGBADepthPacking }); dm.onBeforeCompile = hook; dm.customProgramCacheKey = () => 'v20fard'; ST.v20M = { fm, dm }; }
      m.material = ST.v20M.fm; m.customDepthMaterial = ST.v20M.dm; }
    function facade(B, u, A, Bp, n, kind, laneW, isMain) { let a = A, b = Bp; if ((b[0] - a[0]) * n[1] - (b[1] - a[1]) * n[0] < 0) { a = Bp; b = A; }
      const len = Math.hypot(b[0] - a[0], b[1] - a[1]), nb = Math.max(1, Math.round(len / 3.05)), ux = (b[0] - a[0]) / len, uz = (b[1] - a[1]) / len, tint = u.tint, yg = u.yg, y0 = u.y0;
      const gt = []; let door = -1;
      if (kind === 'front' || rnd() < 0.45 || isMain) { if (rnd() < (kind === 'front' ? 0.9 : 0.7)) door = (rnd() * nb) | 0; }
      for (let j = 0; j < nb; j++) { if (j === door) gt.push(nb === 1 && len > 3.6 && rnd() < 0.3 ? 'GATE' : pick(['DOOR1', 'DOOR1', 'DOOR2', 'DOOR2', 'DOOR1', 'DOOR3']));
        else gt.push(isMain || (u.plazaF && rnd() < 0.6) ? pick(['SHOP', 'SHOP2', 'SHOP3', 'SHOP', 'SHOP']) : pick(['GWIN', 'GPLAIN', 'BLIND', 'GWIN', 'SHOP', 'GPLAIN', 'BLIND'])); }
      const ut = [null]; for (let s = 1; s < u.ns; s++) ut.push(s === u.ns - 1 && u.ns > 2 ? pick(TOPP) : pick(UPP));
      const feat = kind === 'front' && u.ns >= 2 && rnd() < 0.34 ? (rnd() < 0.62 ? 'mash' : 'balc') : null, fb = feat ? (nb > 1 ? (door + 1 + ((rnd() * (nb - 1)) | 0) + nb) % nb : 0) : -1;
      for (let j = 0; j < nb; j++) { const p0 = [a[0] + ux * len * j / nb, a[1] + uz * len * j / nb], p1 = [a[0] + ux * len * (j + 1) / nb, a[1] + uz * len * (j + 1) / nb];
        wall(B, p0, p1, n, u.g0, y0, tuv('GPLAIN', 226, 256), tint, yg);
        V20W(B, p0, p1, n, y0, ({ SHOP: 'BLIND', SHOP2: 'GWIN', SHOP3: 'BLIND' })[gt[j]] || gt[j], tint, yg);   /* v35: لا دكاكين بالواجهات — قوس مسدود/شباك */
        for (let s = 1; s < u.ns; s++) V20W(B, p0, p1, n, y0 + 3.3 * s, s === 1 && j === fb ? 'BALC' : ut[s], tint, yg);
        wall(B, p0, p1, n, u.yT, u.yT + 0.7, tuv('PARA', 0, 54), tint, yg);
        bays.push({ c: [(p0[0] + p1[0]) / 2, (p0[1] + p1[1]) / 2], n, door: j === door, shop: /SHOP/.test(gt[j]), u, kind, laneW, isMain, end: j === 0 || j === nb - 1, len: len / nb });
        if (/SHOP/.test(gt[j]) && rnd() < (isMain ? 0.85 : 0.55) && false) {   /* v35: بلا مظلات الدكاكين */ const CL = [[0.78, 0.3, 0.2], [0.24, 0.46, 0.46], [0.82, 0.62, 0.28], [0.55, 0.22, 0.2]], c1 = CL[(rnd() * CL.length) | 0], c2 = [0.95, 0.9, 0.78], ex = Math.min(1.5, laneW * 0.24), ya = y0 + 2.8, yb = y0 + 2.3, ns = 4, t = tuv('SMOOTH');
          for (let q = 0; q < ns; q++) { const f0 = 0.06 + 0.88 * q / ns, f1 = 0.06 + 0.88 * (q + 1) / ns, A = [p0[0] + (p1[0] - p0[0]) * f0, p0[1] + (p1[1] - p0[1]) * f0], Bq = [p0[0] + (p1[0] - p0[0]) * f1, p0[1] + (p1[1] - p0[1]) * f1], col = q % 2 ? c2 : c1, cc = [col, col, col, col], cd = cc.map(v => mulc(v, 0.62));
            const P4 = [[A[0] + n[0] * 0.05, ya, A[1] + n[1] * 0.05], [Bq[0] + n[0] * 0.05, ya, Bq[1] + n[1] * 0.05], [Bq[0] + n[0] * ex, yb, Bq[1] + n[1] * ex], [A[0] + n[0] * ex, yb, A[1] + n[1] * ex]], up = [n[0] * 0.7, 0.71, n[1] * 0.7], dn = [-n[0] * 0.7, -0.71, -n[1] * 0.7];
            quad(B, P4, t, cc, up); quad(B, P4, t, cd, dn); }
          ST.awn = (ST.awn || 0) + 1; } }
      const mc = [(a[0] + b[0]) / 2, (a[1] + b[1]) / 2], U = [ux, uz];
      obox(B, mc, u.yT + 0.7, U, n, len + 0.06, 0.2, 0.2, 'SMOOTH', mulc(tint, 1.04), yg, 'ftblr');
      obox(B, mc, y0 + 3.3 - 0.08, U, n, len, 0.16, 0.11, 'SMOOTH', tint, yg, 'ftb');
      if (feat) { const c = [a[0] + ux * len * (fb + 0.5) / nb, a[1] + uz * len * (fb + 0.5) / nb], w = Math.min(2.1, len / nb - 0.5); if (feat === 'mash') mashrabiya(B, c, y0 + 3.3 + 0.32, U, n, w, tint, yg, rnd() < 0.4); else balcony(B, c, y0 + 3.3 + 0.05, U, n, w, tint, yg); }
      if (door >= 0) return { j: door, c: [a[0] + ux * len * (door + 0.5) / nb, a[1] + uz * len * (door + 0.5) / nb], n }; return null; }
    function plainWall(B, u, A, Bp, n, yb, yt) { const len = Math.hypot(Bp[0] - A[0], Bp[1] - A[1]), nb = Math.max(1, Math.round(len / 3.05)), ux = (Bp[0] - A[0]) / len, uz = (Bp[1] - A[1]) / len;
      for (let j = 0; j < nb; j++) { const p0 = [A[0] + ux * len * j / nb, A[1] + uz * len * j / nb], p1 = [A[0] + ux * len * (j + 1) / nb, A[1] + uz * len * (j + 1) / nb];
        for (let y = yb; y < yt - 0.05; y += 3.3) { const y1 = Math.min(yt, y + 3.3); wall(B, p0, p1, n, y, y1, tuv('UPLAIN', 256 - (y1 - y) / 3.3 * 256, 256), u.tint, u.yg); } } }
    const outN = (S1, S2, u) => { const dx = S2[0] - S1[0], dz = S2[1] - S1[1], l = Math.hypot(dx, dz) || 1; let nx = -dz / l, nz = dx / l; const c = [(u.mid[0] + u.bm[0]) / 2, (u.mid[1] + u.bm[1]) / 2]; if ((S1[0] - c[0]) * nx + (S1[1] - c[1]) * nz < 0) { nx = -nx; nz = -nz; } return [nx, nz]; };
    let nSide = 0;
    for (const u of live) { const B = chunkOf(u.mid[0], u.mid[1], u.b);
      const dF = facade(B, u, u.FL, u.FR, u.nF, 'front', u.laneW, false); if (dF) homesC.push({ u, d: dF });
      u.ex = [false, false];
      for (const [si, S1, S2, nb] of [[0, u.FL, u.BL, u.prev], [1, u.FR, u.BR, u.next]]) { const ns = outN(S1, S2, u);
        if (!nb || nb.bad) { u.ex[si] = true; nSide++; const lw = (si === 0 ? !u.prev : !u.next) ? sideLane(u, si === 0) + 1.2 : 0, main = lw > 7.5; facade(B, u, S1, S2, ns, 'side', lw, main); }
        else if (nb.yT < u.yT - 0.05) plainWall(B, u, S1, S2, ns, nb.yT, u.yT + 0.7); }
      plainWall(B, u, u.BL, u.BR, [-u.nF[0], -u.nF[1]], u.g0, u.yT);
      const ct = mulc(u.tint, 0.97); quad(B, [[u.FL[0], u.yT + 0.02, u.FL[1]], [u.FR[0], u.yT + 0.02, u.FR[1]], [u.BR[0], u.yT + 0.02, u.BR[1]], [u.BL[0], u.yT + 0.02, u.BL[1]]], tuv('ROOF'), [ct, ct, ct, ct], [0, 1, 0]);
      // التصادم: صندوق الواجهة (+ صندوق الجانب المكشوف) — الجدار المرئي نفسه، بلا جدران خفية
      const tx = (u.FR[0] - u.FL[0]) / u.W, tz = (u.FR[1] - u.FL[1]) / u.W, sL = u.ex[0] ? 0.35 : 0, sR = u.ex[1] ? 0.35 : 0, wB = u.W - sL - sR, cxm = u.mid[0] + tx * (sL - sR) / 2 - u.nF[0] * u.dep / 2, czm = u.mid[1] + tz * (sL - sR) / 2 - u.nF[1] * u.dep / 2;
      const top = u.yT + 0.7, fb = topOf(solidBox(cxm, czm, wB, u.dep, Math.atan2(-tz, tx)), top); L.obst.push(fb); L.solid.push(fb);
      for (const [si, S1, S2] of [[0, u.FL, u.BL], [1, u.FR, u.BR]]) { if (!u.ex[si]) continue; const ns = outN(S1, S2, u), l = Math.hypot(S2[0] - S1[0], S2[1] - S1[1]), sx = (S2[0] - S1[0]) / l, sz = (S2[1] - S1[1]) / l, th = Math.min(1.8, u.W / 2);
        const sb = topOf(solidBox((S1[0] + S2[0]) / 2 - ns[0] * th / 2, (S1[1] + S2[1]) / 2 - ns[1] * th / 2, l, th, Math.atan2(-sz, sx)), top); L.obst.push(sb); L.solid.push(sb); } }
    // ── أقواس فوق الأزقة (قنطرة من جدار لجدار) ──
    const grid = {}, gk = (x, z) => Math.floor(x / 12) + ',' + Math.floor(z / 12); for (const u of live) { const k = gk(u.mid[0], u.mid[1]); (grid[k] = grid[k] || []).push(u); }
    const inPoly = (x, z, Q) => { let ins = false; for (let i = 0, j = Q.length - 1; i < Q.length; j = i++) { const xi = Q[i][0], zi = Q[i][1], xj = Q[j][0], zj = Q[j][1]; if ((zi > z) !== (zj > z) && x < (xj - xi) * (z - zi) / (zj - zi) + xi) ins = !ins; } return ins; };
    const unitAt = (x, z) => { const i0 = Math.floor(x / 12), j0 = Math.floor(z / 12); for (let i = i0 - 1; i <= i0 + 1; i++) for (let j = j0 - 1; j <= j0 + 1; j++) for (const u of grid[i + ',' + j] || []) if (inPoly(x, z, [u.FL, u.FR, u.BR, u.BL])) return u; return null; };
    const arches = [], ACAND = [];
    for (const s of strips) { if (s.ring === 72 || s.ring === 143) continue; const p = s.pts; let acc = 0; for (let i = 1; i < p.length; i++) { const l = Math.hypot(p[i][0] - p[i - 1][0], p[i][1] - p[i - 1][1]); for (let f = 0.25; f < 1; f += 0.5) ACAND.push([p[i - 1][0] + (p[i][0] - p[i - 1][0]) * f, p[i - 1][1] + (p[i][1] - p[i - 1][1]) * f, (p[i][0] - p[i - 1][0]) / l, (p[i][1] - p[i - 1][1]) / l, s.w, s.alley]); } }
    for (let i = ACAND.length - 1; i > 0; i--) { const j = (rnd() * (i + 1)) | 0; [ACAND[i], ACAND[j]] = [ACAND[j], ACAND[i]]; }
    const ARG = [];
    for (const [x, z, tx, tz, w, al] of ACAND) { if (arches.length >= (HQ() ? 22 : 18)) break; if (arches.some(q => Math.hypot(q[0] - x, q[1] - z) < 26)) continue; if (Math.hypot(x - cx, z - cz) < 78) continue;
      const S = w + 1.2, lx = -tz, lz = tx; let ok = true, ya = 1e9, ua = null;
      for (const f of [-1.2, 0, 1.2]) for (const s of [-1, 1]) { const u = unitAt(x + tx * f + lx * s * (S / 2 + 0.6), z + tz * f + lz * s * (S / 2 + 0.6)); if (!u) { ok = false; break; } ya = Math.min(ya, u.yT); ua = u; }
      if (!ok) continue; const yg = H(x, z), top = ya - yg + 0.12, spH = 3.3; if (top < spH + S / 2 + 0.9) continue;
      const sh = new THREE.Shape(), E = 0.35; sh.moveTo(-S / 2 - E, 0); sh.lineTo(-S / 2, 0); sh.lineTo(-S / 2, spH); sh.absarc(0, spH, S / 2, Math.PI, 0, true); sh.lineTo(S / 2, 0); sh.lineTo(S / 2 + E, 0); sh.lineTo(S / 2 + E, top); sh.lineTo(-S / 2 - E, top); sh.lineTo(-S / 2 - E, 0);
      let g = new THREE.ExtrudeGeometry(sh, { depth: 1.5, bevelEnabled: false, curveSegments: 9 }); if (g.index) g = g.toNonIndexed(); g.translate(0, 0, -0.75);
      const pa = g.attributes.position, na = g.attributes.normal, [tu0, tv0] = tuv('SMOOTH')[0], [tu1, tv1] = tuv('SMOOTH')[2], yaw = Math.atan2(tx, tz), cy = Math.cos(yaw), sy = Math.sin(yaw), B = chunkOf(x, z, 1);
      for (let i = 0; i < pa.count; i++) { const X = pa.getX(i), Y = pa.getY(i), Z = pa.getZ(i), NX = na.getX(i), NY = na.getY(i), NZ = na.getZ(i);
        B.P.push(x + X * cy + Z * sy, yg - 0.12 + Y, z - X * sy + Z * cy); B.N.push(NX * cy + NZ * sy, NY, -NX * sy + NZ * cy); B.U.push(tu0 + (X / (S + 2 * E) + 0.5) * (tu1 - tu0), tv0 + (Y / top) * (tv1 - tv0));
        const c = mulc(ua.tint, NY < -0.3 ? 0.62 : 0.6 + 0.4 * Math.min(1, Y / 5)); B.C.push(c[0], c[1], c[2]); }
      arches.push([x, z, yaw, S, spH]); }
    // ── الشبكات النهائية: قطاع ٣٠° لكل حلقة = نداء رسم واحد، مادة الأطلس الواحدة ──
    const mat = atlas().mat; let tris = 0, nm = 0;
    for (const k in CH) { const Bq = CH[k]; if (!Bq.P.length) continue; const g = new THREE.BufferGeometry(); g.setAttribute('position', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(Bq.P, 3)); g.setAttribute('normal', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(Bq.N, 3)); g.setAttribute('uv', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(Bq.U, 2)); g.setAttribute('color', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(Bq.C, 3));
      g.translate(-cx, 0, -cz); g.computeBoundingSphere(); const m = new THREE.Mesh(g, mat); m.castShadow = true; m.receiveShadow = true; m.userData.v18 = 1; L.det.add(m); tris += Bq.P.length / 9; nm++; if (Bq.R) try { V20F(Bq, m, mat); } catch (e) { console.warn('[v20] far', e); } }
    // ── بيوت السكان (أبواب حقيقية على الأزقة): ~٤٠ بيتاً كما كان عدد السكان سابقاً ──
    homesC.sort((p, q) => Math.atan2(p.u.mid[1] - cz, p.u.mid[0] - cx) - Math.atan2(q.u.mid[1] - cz, q.u.mid[0] - cx)); const NH = 40, step = Math.max(1, homesC.length / NH), CAPS = [2, 3, 3, 2, 4, 3, 2, 3];
    for (let i = 0; i < NH && Math.floor(i * step) < homesC.length; i++) { const { u, d } = homesC[Math.floor(i * step)], door = [d.c[0] + d.n[0] * 1.4, d.c[1] + d.n[1] * 1.4], cap = CAPS[i % CAPS.length];
      L.houses.push({ x: (u.mid[0] + u.bm[0]) / 2, z: (u.mid[1] + u.bm[1]) / 2, door, def: { role: cap >= 4 ? 'بيت كبير' : cap === 3 ? 'بيت متوسط' : 'بيت صغير', w: u.W, d: u.dep, h: u.yT - u.yg, doorSide: '+z', doorDist: u.dep / 2, cap }, key: 'v18', cap, yaw: Math.atan2(d.n[0], d.n[1]), ring: 'ABC'[u.b], T: { W: u.W, D: u.dep } }); }
    L.v18 = { cx, cz, strips, rads, units: live, bays, arches, unitAt };
    ST.build = { units: live.length, dropped: units.length - live.length, alleys: nAl, strips: strips.length, sides: nSide, arches: arches.length, meshes: nm, tris: Math.round(tris), homes: L.houses.filter(h => h.key === 'v18').length, drop: DR, ms: Math.round(performance.now() - t0) };
    console.info('[v18] أزقة وبيوت متلاصقة', JSON.stringify(ST.build)); return ST.build; }

  // ───────── (٤) زينة الأزقة: براميل وجرار وصناديق وفوانيس بحجم حقيقي (×١٫٢٥) على طول الجدران، وبسطات على جانبي الشارع الرئيسي ─────────
  function dress(L, ctx) { const t0 = performance.now(), V = L.v18; if (!V || !window.V17 || !V17.geos) return null; const { cx, cz, H } = ctx, rnd = rs(5151), G = V17.geos(), SC = 1.25;
    const cl = [[], []], stalls = [], lan = [], used = [], arches = V.arches, near = (x, z, r) => used.some(q => Math.hypot(q[0] - x, q[1] - z) < r);
    if (!G.lan) G.lan = V17.lanternGeo();
    const solid = (x, z, w, d, yaw, top) => { const b = topOf(solidBox(x, z, w, d, yaw), top); L.obst.push(b); L.solid.push(b); };
    for (const by of V.bays) { if (by.door) continue; const [bx, bz] = by.c, n = by.n, yaw = Math.atan2(n[0], n[1]), y = H(bx, bz) - 0.02, lw = by.laneW || (by.kind === 'front' ? by.u.laneW : 0);
      if (!lw || lw < 4.6) continue; if (arches.some(a => Math.hypot(a[0] - bx, a[1] - bz) < 4.5)) continue; if (Math.hypot(bx - cx, bz - cz) < 70) continue;
      if (by.isMain) { if (by.len > 2.4 && !near(bx, bz, 4.8) && rnd() < 0.8) { used.push([bx, bz]); /* v35: بسطات الشارع الرئيسي محذوفة */ } continue; }
      const narrow = lw < 5.2, pr = narrow ? 0.36 : by.u.plazaF ? 0.45 : 0.82; if (by.end && rnd() < 0.5) continue;
      if (rnd() < pr && !near(bx, bz, narrow ? 6 : 3.7)) { const k = rnd() < 0.5 ? 0 : 1, lx = -n[1], lz = n[0], ox = lx * 0.3 * SC, oz = lz * 0.3 * SC; cl[k].push([bx - cx, y, bz - cz, yaw, SC]); used.push([bx, bz]);
        solid(bx + ox + n[0] * 0.45 * SC, bz + oz + n[1] * 0.45 * SC, 2.3 * SC, 0.9 * SC, yaw, y + 1.05 * SC); }
      else if (rnd() < 0.3 && !lan.some(q => Math.hypot(q[0] + cx - bx, q[2] + cz - bz) < 6)) lan.push([bx - cx, y, bz - cz, yaw, 1.3]); }
    V17.dyn(L, G.cA, cl[0]); V17.dyn(L, G.cB, cl[1]); V17.dyn(L, G.stall, stalls); V17.dyn(L, G.lan, lan); L.lan35 = lan.map(q => [q[0] + cx, q[2] + cz, q[1], q[3]]); { const mm = []; for (const by of V.bays) { if (!by.isMain || Math.hypot(by.c[0] - cx, by.c[1] - cz) < 60) continue; if (mm.some(q => Math.hypot(q[0] - by.c[0], q[1] - by.c[1]) < 13)) continue; mm.push([by.c[0], by.c[1], H(by.c[0], by.c[1]), Math.atan2(by.n[0], by.n[1])]); } L.main35 = mm; }   /* v35: ضوء دافئ من بيوت الشارع الرئيسي ليلاً */
    ST.dress = { clA: cl[0].length, clB: cl[1].length, stalls: stalls.length, lanterns: lan.length, ms: Math.round(performance.now() - t0) }; return ST.dress; }
  // ───────── (٥) ملاحة الناس: شبكة إشغال ١م للمدينة كلها + A* بخلايا ١٫٥م (تمرّ في أزقة ٤٫٨م) ─────────
  function nav(L) { const t0 = performance.now(), V = L.v18; if (!V) return; const n = Math.ceil(2 * CITY_R + 24), x0 = V.cx - n / 2, z0 = V.cz - n / 2, a = new Uint8Array(n * n), clr = 0.46;
    for (const o of L.obst) { const e = o[2] + clr, i0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor(o[0] - e - x0)), i1 = Math.min(n - 1, Math.ceil(o[0] + e - x0)), j0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor(o[1] - e - z0)), j1 = Math.min(n - 1, Math.ceil(o[1] + e - z0));
      for (let j = j0; j <= j1; j++) for (let i = i0; i <= i1; i++) { const k = j * n + i; if (a[k]) continue; if (sdist(o, x0 + i + 0.5, z0 + j + 0.5) < clr) a[k] = 1; } }
    L.nav18 = { n, x0, z0, a }; ST.nav = Math.round(performance.now() - t0); }
  function astar(x0, z0, x1, z1, ob, ctx) { const L = ctx.L, G = L.nav18, R = 30, N = 61, C = 1.5, NN = N * N, ng = ctx.ng, road = ctx.road;
    const wx = i => x0 + (i - R) * C, wz = j => z0 + (j - R) * C, st = new Uint8Array(NN), HH = new Float32Array(NN), CO = new Float32Array(NN);
    const blocked = k => { if (st[k]) return st[k] === 2; const x = wx(k % N), z = wz((k / N) | 0), h = LIB.height(x, z); HH[k] = h; CO[k] = road ? road(x, z) : 1;
      let b = h < 0.3 || (ng && Math.hypot(x - ng[0], z - ng[1]) < ng[2]);
      if (!b) { const gi = Math.floor(x - G.x0), gj = Math.floor(z - G.z0); if (gi >= 0 && gj >= 0 && gi < G.n && gj < G.n) b = G.a[gj * G.n + gi] === 1;
        else for (const o of ob) { if (Math.abs(x - o[0]) < o[2] + 1 && Math.abs(z - o[1]) < o[2] + 1 && sdist(o, x, z) < 0.9) { b = true; break; } } }
      st[k] = b ? 2 : 1; return b; };
    const cl = q => Math.max(0, Math.min(N - 1, q)), gi = cl(Math.round((x1 - x0) / C) + R), gj = cl(Math.round((z1 - z0) / C) + R), gk = gj * N + gi, inside = Math.abs(x1 - x0) < R * C - 2 && Math.abs(z1 - z0) < R * C - 2;
    const sk = R * N + R, g = new Float32Array(NN).fill(1e9), par = new Int32Array(NN).fill(-1), closed = new Uint8Array(NN), hk = [], hf = [];
    const push = (k, f) => { hk.push(k); hf.push(f); let i = hk.length - 1; while (i > 0) { const p = (i - 1) >> 1; if (hf[p] <= hf[i]) break; let t = hk[p]; hk[p] = hk[i]; hk[i] = t; t = hf[p]; hf[p] = hf[i]; hf[i] = t; i = p; } };
    const pop = () => { const k = hk[0], lk = hk.pop(), lf = hf.pop(); if (hk.length) { hk[0] = lk; hf[0] = lf; let i = 0; for (;;) { const l = 2 * i + 1, r = l + 1; let m = i; if (l < hk.length && hf[l] < hf[m]) m = l; if (r < hk.length && hf[r] < hf[m]) m = r; if (m === i) break; let t = hk[m]; hk[m] = hk[i]; hk[i] = t; t = hf[m]; hf[m] = hf[i]; hf[i] = t; i = m; } } return k; };
    const hz = k => { const dx = Math.abs(k % N - gi), dz = Math.abs(((k / N) | 0) - gj); return Math.max(dx, dz) + 0.414 * Math.min(dx, dz); };
    const mk = k => { const p = []; for (let c = k; c !== -1 && c !== sk; c = par[c]) p.push([wx(c % N), wz((c / N) | 0)]); p.reverse(); return p; };
    blocked(sk); g[sk] = 0; push(sk, hz(sk)); let best = sk, bd = 1e9, it = 0;
    while (hk.length && it++ < 6000) { const k = pop(); if (closed[k]) continue; closed[k] = 1;
      if (k === gk && inside) { const p = mk(k); if (!p.length) p.push([x1, z1]); else p[p.length - 1] = [x1, z1]; return p; }
      const td = Math.hypot(wx(k % N) - x1, wz((k / N) | 0) - z1); if (td < bd) { bd = td; best = k; }
      const i = k % N, j = (k / N) | 0;
      for (let di = -1; di <= 1; di++) for (let dj = -1; dj <= 1; dj++) { if (!di && !dj) continue; const ni = i + di, nj = j + dj; if (ni < 0 || nj < 0 || ni >= N || nj >= N) continue; const nk = nj * N + ni;
        if (closed[nk]) continue; if (blocked(nk) && nk !== gk) continue; if (di && dj && (blocked(j * N + ni) || blocked(nj * N + i))) continue; if (Math.abs(HH[nk] - HH[k]) > 1.4) continue;
        const ngv = g[k] + (di && dj ? 1.414 : 1) * (CO[nk] || 1) + Math.abs(HH[nk] - HH[k]) * 0.5; if (ngv < g[nk]) { g[nk] = ngv; par[nk] = k; push(nk, ngv + hz(nk)); } } }
    if (inside) return null; if (best === sk) return null; const p = mk(best); return p.length ? p : null; }

  // ── للبعيدين (غير مرئيين): خطوة جشعة على شبكة الملاحة بدل الخط المستقيم عبر البيوت، وفكّ أي عالق داخل جدار ──
  function blk(L, x, z) { const G = L.nav18; if (!G) return false; const i = Math.floor(x - G.x0), j = Math.floor(z - G.z0); if (i < 0 || j < 0 || i >= G.n || j >= G.n) return false; return G.a[j * G.n + i] === 1; }
  function inside(L, x, z, r) { for (const o of L.solid) { if (Math.abs(x - o[0]) > o[2] + r || Math.abs(z - o[1]) > o[2] + r) continue; if (sdist(o, x, z) < r) return true; } return false; }
  function unstick(n, L) { if (!blk(L, n.x, n.z) || !inside(L, n.x, n.z, 0.05)) return false; const G = L.nav18, i0 = Math.floor(n.x - G.x0), j0 = Math.floor(n.z - G.z0);
    for (let r = 1; r <= 10; r++) { let bd = 1e9, bi = -1, bj = -1; for (let dj = -r; dj <= r; dj++) for (let di = -r; di <= r; di++) { if (Math.max(Math.abs(di), Math.abs(dj)) !== r) continue; const i = i0 + di, j = j0 + dj; if (i < 0 || j < 0 || i >= G.n || j >= G.n || G.a[j * G.n + i]) continue; const d = di * di + dj * dj; if (d < bd) { bd = d; bi = i; bj = j; } }
      if (bi >= 0) { n.x = G.x0 + bi + 0.5; n.z = G.z0 + bj + 0.5; n.path = null; ST.unstick = (ST.unstick || 0) + 1; return true; } } return false; }
  function farMove(n, L, gx, gz, s) { let left = s; n.fs = n.fs || (Math.random() < 0.5 ? 1 : -1);
    while (left > 1e-3) { const st = Math.min(1, left), dx = gx - n.x, dz = gz - n.z, dl = Math.hypot(dx, dz); if (dl < 0.5) break; const a0 = Math.atan2(dx, dz); let moved = false;
      for (const da of [0, 0.6, -0.6, 1.2, -1.2, 1.9, -1.9, 2.6, -2.6]) { const a = a0 + da * n.fs, nx = n.x + Math.sin(a) * st, nz = n.z + Math.cos(a) * st; if (blk(L, nx, nz)) continue; n.x = nx; n.z = nz; moved = true; break; }
      if (!moved) { n.fs = -n.fs; break; } left -= st; } }
  return { build, dress, nav, astar, atlas, ST, T, tuv, farMove, unstick, blk }; })();
window.V18 = V18;

const DENSE16 = (() => { let ROOF = null;
  const roofGeo = () => ROOF || (ROOF = (() => { const mk = (parts) => { const gs = parts.map(([g, c]) => { g = g.index ? g.toNonIndexed() : g; const col = new Float32Array(g.attributes.position.count * 3); for (let i = 0; i < col.length; i += 3) { col[i] = c[0]; col[i + 1] = c[1]; col[i + 2] = c[2]; } g.setAttribute('color', new THREE.BufferAttribute(col, 3)); g.deleteAttribute('uv'); return g; });
      let n = 0; gs.forEach(g => n += g.attributes.position.count); const P = new Float32Array(n * 3), N = new Float32Array(n * 3), C = new Float32Array(n * 3); let o = 0; gs.forEach(g => { P.set(g.attributes.position.array, o * 3); N.set(g.attributes.normal.array, o * 3); C.set(g.attributes.color.array, o * 3); o += g.attributes.position.count; });
      const out = new THREE.BufferGeometry(); out.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(P, 3)); out.setAttribute('normal', new THREE.BufferAttribute(N, 3)); out.setAttribute('color', new THREE.BufferAttribute(C, 3)); out.computeBoundingSphere(); SHARED.add(out); return out; };
    const tank = mk([[new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.55, 0.55, 1.0, 10).translate(0, 1.0, 0), [0.22, 0.22, 0.24]], [new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.58, 0.58, 0.08, 10).translate(0, 1.54, 0), [0.3, 0.3, 0.32]], ...[[-0.35, -0.35], [0.35, -0.35], [-0.35, 0.35], [0.35, 0.35]].map(([x, z]) => [new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.07, 0.5, 0.07).translate(x, 0.25, z), [0.25, 0.22, 0.2]])]);
    const dome = mk([[new THREE.CylinderGeometry(1.15, 1.2, 0.5, 14).translate(0, 0.25, 0), [0.93, 0.91, 0.87]], [new THREE.SphereGeometry(1.15, 14, 7, 0, Math.PI * 2, 0, Math.PI / 2).translate(0, 0.5, 0), [0.95, 0.94, 0.91]], [new THREE.ConeGeometry(0.06, 0.4, 6).translate(0, 1.85, 0), [0.75, 0.6, 0.25]]]);
    const awn = mk([[new THREE.BoxGeometry(2.4, 0.06, 1.1).rotateX(0.32).translate(0, 0, 0.5), [0.13, 0.22, 0.45]], [new THREE.BoxGeometry(2.4, 0.22, 0.04).translate(0, -0.27, 1.03), [0.85, 0.7, 0.3]]]);
    const line = mk([[new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.02, 0.02, 1).translate(0, 0, 0), [0.3, 0.28, 0.25]], ...[-0.35, -0.12, 0.12, 0.35].map((z, i) => [new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.02, 0.45, 0.22).translate(0, -0.24, z), [[0.92, 0.9, 0.85], [0.75, 0.3, 0.28], [0.2, 0.35, 0.6], [0.85, 0.75, 0.45]][i]])]);
    return { tank, dome, awn, line }; })());
  function build(L, ctx) { const t0 = performance.now(), { cx, cz, H, blockedAt, lamps, D2R, pcx, pcz } = ctx, PI = Math.PI;
    const MK = ['mixH1', 'mixH2', 'mixH4', 'mixH5', 'mixFood', 'mixSmith'].filter(k => MODELS[k] && MODELS[k].house), WT = { mixH1: 0.25, mixH2: 0.22, mixH4: 0.2, mixH5: 0.23, mixFood: 0.06, mixSmith: 0.04 };
    if (!MK.length || !window.__LOD16_ON) return; let seed = 16016; const rnd = () => (seed = (seed * 16807) % 2147483647) / 2147483647;
    const pick = () => { let t = rnd() * MK.reduce((a, k) => a + WT[k], 0); for (const k of MK) { t -= WT[k]; if (t <= 0) return k; } return MK[0]; };
    const RAD = [0, 45, 90, 135, 180, 225, 270, 315], WIDE = { 270: 5.8 }, CR = CITY_R, MQ = [cx + 92.5 * Math.cos(165 * D2R), cz - 92.5 * Math.sin(165 * D2R)];
    const gates = (L.gates || []).map(g => [g.x, g.z]);
    // شبكة إشغال ١م: 1 = ممنوع/مبنى، 2 = فناء أمام باب (زقاق مشترك)
    const N = 2 * Math.ceil(CR) + 2, G0 = Math.ceil(CR) + 1, OC = new Uint8Array(N * N), cell = (x, z) => { const i = Math.floor(x - cx) + G0, j = Math.floor(z - cz) + G0; return i < 0 || j < 0 || i >= N || j >= N ? -1 : j * N + i; };
    const RESx = (ctx.RES || []).filter(q => !q.r);   // مناطق محجوزة (السوق، الإسطبلات، ساحات التدريب) — التل يُفحص بنصف قطره الجديد
    const resHit = (x, z, m) => RESx.some(q => Math.abs(x - q.x) < q.hx + m && Math.abs(z - q.z) < q.hz + m);
    const blockedBase = (x, z) => { const dx = x - cx, dz = z - cz, R = Math.hypot(dx, dz); if (R > CR - 10.5 || R < 40) return 1;
      if (resHit(x, z, 1.5) || Math.hypot(x - MQ[0], z - MQ[1]) < 25 || Math.hypot(x - pcx, z - pcz) < WORLD.hill.t.r2 + 5 || (Math.abs(x - pcx) < 9 && z > pcz && z < pcz + WORLD.hill.t.foot + 14)) return 1;
      for (const g of gates) if (Math.hypot(x - g[0], z - g[1]) < 24) return 1;
      if (Math.abs(R - 72) < 4.2 || Math.abs(R - 118) < 3.7) return 2;                                   // الطرق: يُسمح عليها بفناء الباب فقط
      const ang = (Math.atan2(-dz, dx) / D2R + 360) % 360;
      for (const a of RAD) { const da = Math.abs(((ang - a + 540) % 360) - 180) * D2R; if (da < PI / 2 && Math.abs(Math.sin(da)) * R < (WIDE[a] || 3.8)) return 2; }
      return 0; };
    for (let j = 0; j < N; j++) for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) OC[j * N + i] = blockedBase(cx + i - G0 + 0.5, cz + j - G0 + 0.5);
    const disc = (x, z, r, v) => { for (let a = -r; a <= r; a++) for (let b = -r; b <= r; b++) if (a * a + b * b <= r * r + 0.5) { const c = cell(x + a, z + b); if (c >= 0 && OC[c] < v) OC[c] = v; } };
    for (const q of lamps) disc(q[0], q[1], 2, 1); for (const q of Object.values(L.poi || {}).flat()) disc(q.x, q.z, 2, 1); for (const q of L.houses) if (q.door) disc(q.door[0], q.door[1], 3, 1);
    // كل المصادمات الموجودة (بيوت، آبار، دكك، أسوار، التل...) بهامش
    for (const s of L.solid) { const e = Math.ceil(s[2] + 1); for (let a = -e; a <= e; a++) for (let b = -e; b <= e; b++) { const x = s[0] + a, z = s[1] + b; if (sdist(s, x, z) < 0.35) { const c = cell(x, z); if (c >= 0) OC[c] = 1; } } }
    // أزقة عرضية متعرجة: لكل حلقة سماكتها ~١٤م إزاحة زاوية مختلفة، وساحات صغيرة
    for (let rb = 40; rb < CR; rb += 14) { const off = rnd() * 30, stp = 24 + rnd() * 10; for (let a = off; a < 360; a += stp) { const ar = a * D2R; for (let rr = rb; rr < rb + 14; rr += 0.7) { const t = (rr - rb) / 14, aw = ar + (t - 0.5) * 0.06; disc(cx + rr * Math.cos(aw), cz - rr * Math.sin(aw), 1, 1); } } }
    for (let k = 0; k < 26; k++) { const a = rnd() * 2 * PI, rr = 50 + rnd() * 80; disc(cx + rr * Math.cos(a), cz - rr * Math.sin(a), 3, 1); }
    const placed = [], out = [], props = { tank: [], dome: [], awn: [], line: [] };
    const foot = (x, z, w, d, yaw, yd, test) => { // test: 0 = فحص، 1 = تعليم. البصمة بهامش ٠٫١ والفناء أمام الباب بعمق yd
      const c = Math.cos(yaw), s = Math.sin(yaw), hw = w / 2, hd = d / 2;
      for (let u = -hw; u <= hw + 0.01; u += 0.7) for (let v = -hd; v <= hd + yd + 0.01; v += 0.7) { const uu = Math.min(u, hw), vv = Math.min(v, hd + yd), x1 = x + uu * c + vv * s, z1 = z - uu * s + vv * c, ce = cell(x1, z1), yard = vv > hd;
        if (ce < 0) return false; if (!test) { if (yard ? OC[ce] === 1 : OC[ce] !== 0) return false; } else if (yard) { if (!OC[ce]) OC[ce] = 2; } else OC[ce] = 1; } return true; };
    let tries = 0;
    for (const [SMIN, SRNG, YD, STEP] of [[0.9, 0.25, 2.2, 1.0], [0.74, 0.14, 1.6, 0.6]]) {   // جولة ثانية أصغر تملأ الفجوات
    for (let rr = 44; rr < CR - 11; rr += STEP) { const n = Math.floor(2 * PI * rr / STEP), a0 = rnd() * 2 * PI;
      for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) { const ar = a0 + i / n * 2 * PI, x = cx + rr * Math.cos(ar), z = cz - rr * Math.sin(ar), c0 = cell(x, z); if (c0 < 0 || OC[c0]) continue;

        for (const fw of inward ? [0, PI, PI / 2, -PI / 2] : [PI, 0, -PI / 2, PI / 2]) { const yaw = Math.atan2(cx - x, cz - z) + fw + (rnd() - 0.5) * 0.05;
          if (foot(x, z, w, d, yaw, YD, 0)) { foot(x, z, w, d, yaw, YD, 1); const sy = s * (0.95 + rnd() * 0.35); out.push({ k, x, z, yaw, s, sy });
